[REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2 §E: the owner's doctrines of 2026-10-18 (verbatim on the kit's BOARD) read against this volume. THE WORLD HOSTS THE CAMPAIGN, NEVER COMPETES: renown is the world's memory of what was done; it gives the party no importance it did not earn by a recorded deed, generates no quest, and a voice naming the party is a hearing whose default decides nothing for the party (§5.5). MACRO OVER MICRO: a person's renown is a band and one reason per holder, never a trial; the landed W-H2 verdicts are the only justice RN reads (§3.1). NO ENGINE MORTALITY CLOCK: RN reads death only as the DM's KILL (the holder's entries skipped, the deed standing, §14); nothing in RN ends a life. MEDIEVAL FOR MILLENNIA: fame fades on the one half-life and the deed ledger keeps its facts for any span (the `reach` compaction, §14), so a long skip leaves old deeds as faded tales, never a new era. MENACES NEVER EMERGENT and PURPOSE ONLY BY THE DM: a menace holds no regard and is never a key (§2.2); RN gives no person a goal — a voice is a holder's regard crossing a band, and what any actor does with regard is its owning program's decision under the DM's direction. No other passage needed changing for them.]

**The owner's words (2026-10-17), verbatim.**
- "In terms of the renown system that we’ve thought of just though I would also like it to be appropriately applied to all of the other NPC‘s what do you think?"
- "But also for the court NPC as well"
- "Regarding reputation and sanctions, some often events by the party are simply just inherently good without any bias. Such as lifting a plague. Or getting rid of bandits. In such cases to whoever they have helped, that actors rivals may be upset that the act was helped, but it’s difficult difficulty for them to probably condemn the act at the party has made without reason. I think that this should be complicated and foiled by both alignment and personality of NPC’s and courts. Take the orphanage example again, if arrival settlement or court arrival is unambiguously of the evil alignment, it makes us that they still hold a grudge against the party. But if they were good or neutral and they were still rivals yeah they said that their enemy was helped, but the act of itself is fine. And so there should be some sort of inherent value for each action that the party may participate or champions may participate in." (the chair reads "arrival settlement or court arrival" as "a rival settlement or a rival court", and "it makes us that" as "it makes sense that")
- From the party message: "Every settlement NPC diety religion courts at large as their own individual reputation awareness with the party and those all contribute and conflict with each each other for the net effect." · "The DM can manually change all of these reputations for each actor at will or even direct their actions to the party at whim."
- From the champions messages: "They also have degrees of failure, configuraable in custom content, that can end them up failing, losing repuation (which would also affect their abilities to a degree)" · "I want them to also have relations with kingdoms, settlements, and other NPCs, champions, dieties that impact their behavior when nearby anything related to them foiled by their personalities, and such relationships are both emergent and dynamic over a set amount of time."
- The doctrines of the day: "Think of this tool as the DMs tool not the party … We only supply setting and consequence"; "The DM wants emergent experience that only when scrutinized upon shows coherence … we’re not trying to show the full machinery"; "Hearings only happen on the week."

**The answer in one paragraph.** Everyone the world remembers — an ordinary townsman, a high priest, a ruler, a champion, a villain, the party — is known and judged by ONE model. Four facts about a person are kept apart and never merged: how widely they are KNOWN (fame), how each other actor FEELS about them (regard), whether their WORD is trusted (credibility), and, for a ruler only, their RIGHT TO RULE (legitimacy, which renown never writes). Fame comes from office ("the High Priest of Kell") and from deeds; every deed is written down once, as lived history, with how far its tale could travel, and fame fades over years as the tale is forgotten — a town knows a person only as far as word of them has reached it. Regard is sparse: an actor feels something about someone only when the DM says so (the allies-and-enemies list, a champion's authored ties) or when it has heard of something they did; it reads every deed through two lenses — what the act is, judged by its own alignment, and whom it helped or hurt, judged by its friends and rivals — with its personality setting the mix, so a good rival grumbles that its enemy was helped and an evil one keeps the grudge. A court feels what its people of power feel, weighted by their power, plus what was done to the court itself; each of its acts comes from the officer whose office does that work, so on scrutiny the why is that officer's grudge. On the surface the DM reads words ("known across the river towns as a fair judge"; "Varn's lords curse the party for saving their rivals; their people do not"); bands, weights and reasons wait for an intentional look; and the DM can set any actor's regard at will. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-14: a person or a court enters this one model through a registered deed door (the party's, a paragon's, a verdict's); the engine's own doors for courts and ordinary persons are recorded SLOTS (§3.1), so until each lands such a person is known by office alone.]

**The binding prior words.**
- THE PROMISE (constitutional): a seed is a starting world forever; lived history is immutable; tuning is owner-signed. ⇒ deeds are append-only; a strike is a recorded mark, never a deletion.
- Law One (requirement 1): the engine models believers, never truth. ⇒ fame is belief; regard reads the version heard; a deed's REASON is a claim.
- Product scope: never a named character's fate; death is the DM's.
- The deity doctrine (ADDENDUM 15): "THE FOLLOWERS ACT, THE GOD DOES NOT." ⇒ a creed's faithful hold regard; a god holds none.
- FINITE SEMANTICS: closed vocabularies; the AI is a clerk, never a writer.
- The coup budget is full: `rulingPowerCoup.js :: resolveCoupVerdict` "FIVE signed terms … sum to exactly 0.815 against a DECLARED_BUDGET of 0.82 … taking it is an owner tuning act" (R3 §1d). ⇒ renown never enters a coup.
- The economy's statecraft rule, law E14 (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_EC.md`): personal enmity colours court-to-court deciders only through their existing inputs, never past the hard war gates.
- The champions brief §2 "Failure": "RENOWN (not 'reputation', a landed token) rises and falls with deeds, scales the gift and the price, and is known to courts by report."
- RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH (chair, 2026-10-17, by delegation): the person substrate is the world-NPC ledger (§1); deeds ledger, projected scope, persisted regard links, the party's credibility holder, `ACT_OFFICE`, the tokens (§5); the TIER lines (§7). Where this volume departs from them it says so with a STOP or a REFINED bracket.

**The population facts that bound the design** (CONFIRMED unless marked). A world holds tens of settlements and, per settlement, a roster of NPCs with POSITIONAL ids (`npc_${idx + 1}`, R1 S2) that REBIND on reroll; only graduated persons hold a durable `wnpc_` id, and graduation is refused in every shipped preset today (`npcConsequencesEnabled` lit nowhere). The rumor ledger keeps at most 24 tellings per settlement for 26 or 13 weeks; the feed 240 entries plus the newest year; `pulseHistory` 80. Eleven of the twelve party-impact kinds never reach the rumor road through the feed (R2 Q1, EXECUTED). Four of seven presets have no rumor road at all (omniscient), two delay without weathering, one weathers (R2 Q2, EXECUTED). ⇒ Nothing may be stored per NPC beyond what an office already gives; deeds are the store; every per-pair opinion is either sparse and event-born or derived.

---
## §1 THE LAWS (RN1–RN16)

**RN1 — FOUR FACTS, NEVER MERGED.** FAME (how widely a person is known), REGARD (how each actor feels about them), CREDIBILITY (whether their word is trusted) and LEGITIMACY (a ruler's right to rule) are four quantities with four homes, and no read, band or sentence composes two of them into one. Fame is RN's derived read (§4). Regard is RN's sparse ledger plus its derivation (§5). Credibility is the landed `npcCredibility` law for persons, a holder on the party's sub-ledger for the party (§7), and the landed court stock for courts (never touched by RN). Legitimacy is `governanceLedger.js :: governanceLedger`, which RN never writes. "Infamy" is not a fifth fact: it is fame read beside a negative regard, on read, for one surface (RN4). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6 · SEAMS-r2-13: FAME is held by an actor the world speaks of by name — a person (`p:`), a free company carrying the paragon mark (THE CORPS, `u:`, §2.2; CH §16b) or the party. A SETTLEMENT doer's deed (a court's act: a passage, a site taken, a patrol's clash) adds no fame to anyone: it travels as a TALE (its telling), the persons and paragons who hear it regard the doing court (§2.1 `doer`), and a court is known through its ruler's office (§4.2).] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (`cure/L-BD.md` §4 RN; L-BD 32b/33b, 38, 42 (e)): a NAMED BODY of THE BODIES — a gang or a conclave past BD's naming threshold — is an actor the world speaks of by name ("the Hollow Road gang"), so it holds fame by its deeds alone (no office, §4.1) and its notoriety reads through §4.4's one table; a nameless body and every beast hold no fame and are no key (§2.2).]

**RN2 — DEEDS ARE THE STORE; LIVED HISTORY IS IMMUTABLE.** Every renown fact that can change traces to a DEED: one append-only record in `spatialLedgers.deeds` (the `concludedWars` idiom: "APPEND-ONLY … never rewritten, folded or pruned — that is lived history"), minted with a zero-rng id that is never borrowed from `outcome.id` (R2 hazard 6: two removals in one settlement share an outcome id and would merge as one rumor). A deed's projected scope is computed ONCE at mint and frozen (§3.2). The DM's STRIKE writes a `struck` mark and the deed stops counting everywhere; the record stays (THE PROMISE; the landed party door has `undoState:'none'`, so the mark is its undo). A CORRECTION is its own deed carrying `correctsRef`, and it REPLACES the version it corrects wherever it is heard. Nothing is stored per NPC beyond what an office already gives.

**RN3 — FAME IS BELIEF, DERIVED ON READ, AND FADES OVER YEARS.** A person's fame derives from OFFICE (a ruler, a high priest, a guildmaster is known by position) and from the DEEDS they did, each deed contributing its frozen projected scope decayed on the spine's banded-stock half-life (`bandedStock.js :: decayTowardNeutral`, band `a_decade`, DRAFT; `a_year` for an `npc_verdict` deed, §3.1 [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7's pace]) — no second decay law. A town knows a person only as far as word of them has reached it: the observer read asks whether the town lies inside a deed's projected reach and whether the tale has ARRIVED there — in the road presets by the rumor road's own relay, while the omniscient arm applies no delay (`hopDelayTicks` reads 0 there, the landed identity) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the carried minor on §4.3]. Fame is never stored per town and never stored per NPC. The OBSERVER read decays with the same ordinal: a deed is known at a town only inside the ring its DECAYED scope still covers — its whole reach while undecayed, within the rumor road's notable hop budget (2) of its witnesses once decayed to `regional`, at its witnesses only once `settlement`, nowhere once `unknown` (§4.3). In the road presets a town knows a deed only if it HEARD it — a live telling in its rumor ledger, or a heard term the person stage wrote there; the projected scope stays the deed's fame BAND and the omniscient arm's knowing set. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-4 · RN-r1-11: the observer read decays and shrinks; the road arms read what was heard, not the projection.] FAME AND REGARD ARE TWO FACTS AND MAY PART ONCE A DEED HAS DECAYED: whether a town HEARD a deed (the one heard test, `heardAt`, §4.3 — the deed's WHOLE projected reach in the omniscient arm, the road's arrival in the belief presets) never shrinks, and a regard term born of the hearing fades only on its own half-life; whether the town still SPEAKS of the deed (fame) shrinks with the decayed ring. A town at the edge of a realm deed's reach may therefore no longer count the doer as known by it while its people still bear the grudge — the heard term is the town's memory, fame the world's — and the dossier says both in words ("little spoken of in Varn now; Varn has not forgotten the siege"). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-11 (RULINGS-ROUND2 B: restated honestly; the round-1 "so fame and regard agree at every town" was false — by the volume's own pace a realm deed's ring shrinks at week 137 while a heard term still stands at 0.544 of its size, EXECUTED `cure/D-RN-r2-probe/r2Probe.out.json` f) · RN-V1: the heard test for regard is the UNDECAYED reach, so fame's shrink never erases a term.]

**RN4 — ONE FAME, ONE FADE.** The landed roamer facet `notorietyBand` ("How widely and how badly a person is spoken of") stays STAMPED exactly as today — the W-H2 verdict's truth, written by `npcVerdictApply.js :: applyNpcVerdict`, never dropped — and under `renownEnabled` (ONE flag) every OBSERVER read of it goes through RN's PROJECTION: its "how widely" from RN's fame at that observer, its "how badly" from the observer's believed moral read (RN9), through one table (§4.4). The verdict ALSO has an additive `npc_verdict` deed, minted by THE ONE PERSON STAGE from the verdict's own ledger record (a PUBLIC verdict only — a `dm_only` turncoat is never told), so the person's fame has a deed behind it [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-6 (RULINGS-ROUND2 B): the round-1 host had no snapshot to project the deed's scope without a kernel line; §3.1] — a deed CAPPED at the stamp's breadth, never read louder than the stamp, and fading on the ladder's `a_year` (§3.1, §4.4) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7]. ONE FADE per person: a person with an RN deed is faded by RN's decay (§4.3) and the landed `beliefFadeSteps` is never stacked on top; a person stamped with no deed (sentenced while RN was dark) keeps the landed fade, byte-identical. `reputationRaceConsumer.js` reads the stamp directly and stays unchanged (truth, by its own design). ES's `operativeNotoriety01` IS fame by office (`embassyEnvoyWeight01` of importance and faction power) and is re-pointed to RN's fame at the target court through a signature car (§4.5): the office floor is identical, deeds can only raise it. There is no third fame. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-10/12: the notoriety facet folds by projection and its verdict writer becomes a deed source; the operative read re-points; both hosts are dark in every preset, so no shipped shift.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-6 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-6, executed): the stamp is kept and read THROUGH the projection, the deed is minted where the verdict is APPLIED, one flag; RN-r1-5: one fade per person — RN's for a person with a deed, the landed fade for a stamp-only person.]

**RN5 — REGARD IS SPARSE, EVENT-BORN, TWO-SIGNED, BANDED, WITH ONE PLAIN REASON.** An actor feels something about a subject only when an AUTHORED seed (the DM's allies-and-enemies list; a champion's authored relations) or a DEED it has heard of creates it; nothing is pre-populated. A regard holds two signs apart (warmth and grudge, the `factionPairStates` idiom, so "loved and hated" is expressible on scrutiny) and reads as one band on the seven-word ladder (§2.4); every change keeps ONE dominant reason in the world's words (the typed incident, concern 9b). Each holder tracks at most a capped number of EMERGENT subjects (DRAFT 12, UNMEASURED); the DM's seeds and sets (`declared`, `dmSet`) are exempt from the cap and from eviction; the weakest emergent subject — the one whose strongest decayed term is smallest — drops first. A town's or a court's feeling about ANOTHER town or court is never an RN entry: it is the landed pair state (`relationshipState`; law E14), and RN's writer refuses such a pair; where RN or its caller consults that pair state for an actor's CHOICE, it reads it THROUGH BELIEF (`beliefMap.js :: readBeliefRelationship`, A.8's form — the truth type only that read's fallback input), and RN's own regard ledger is belief by construction (it is what each holder has heard) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND4 §E OD-RN-48 (the verifier's FAIL A.8): "where they consult a landed pair state … the caller reads it THROUGH BELIEF (`readBeliefRelationship`, A.8's form); RN's refused-pair rule stands"]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-16: weakness defined and the DM's hand never evicted; RN-r1-1: no second pair-regard beside `relationshipState`.]

**RN6 — ONE DEED MOVES ONE HOLDER ONCE PER VERSION HEARD; NO RATCHETS.** A holder's regard of a subject carries at most one term per deed; a later, better or corrected version REPLACES that term, never adds (concern 9c). Every term decays toward its baseline on the half-life — authored links toward the authored band, emergent ones toward indifference (`decayTowardNeutral` is anti-ratchet by property: "never further from neutral … never on the far side"); repeated contact reinforces only by new deeds. Grudges fade unless renewed (requirement 2).

**RN7 — A BODY FEELS WHAT ITS PEOPLE OF POWER FEEL.** A court's (or a faction's, or a creed's faithful's) regard of anyone is DERIVED: its members' regards weighted by each member's power in it (the governing faction's share, ladder standing, the seat), plus its INSTITUTIONAL RECORD (the deeds whose target was the body itself), plus the DM's authored baseline for that body. There is never a second stored court opinion. A new ruler brings new attitudes and the court's stance moves with the people. In the belief presets a body knows only the VERSIONS ITS TOWN HEARD (the heard terms the person stage writes at arrival): it re-reads each through its ruler's character and never reads the deed ledger directly, so a court never knows a part its commons did not hear (§5.3). A KINGDOM is the body at its root: its regard is the root court's, felt in a member's court at PL's one crown-reach share (`crownReachAt(...).share01` — the liege share for a direct vassal, that share × the paramount's reach by the hold for a sub-vassal), RN keeping no reach map of its own (§5.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-12 (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.8)]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-2 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-2, executed) · RN-r1-12.]

**RN8 — EACH ACT COMES FROM ITS OFFICE.** A court's attempt toward the party or a person is made by the member whose office does that work (the closed `ACT_OFFICE` table over the landed `INSTITUTION_ROLES` and `rulerId`, §5.2), driven by that member's own regard as well as the court's derived stance — so a welcoming court can still have the party watched by a hostile officer, and on scrutiny the why is that officer's grudge. RN supplies the read; the act itself is a hearing card owned by the program that owns the act (PT, CH). How courts treat each other stays with the landed diplomacy and war deciders (law E14).

**RN9 — EVERY ACTOR READS A DEED THROUGH TWO LENSES.** A deed's INHERENT VALUE is derived from its kind and its target on the landed alignment axes (kindness and lawfulness, plus aggressiveness as the third coordinate) — never a second judgment the DM must make (§6). Lens one: what the act is, read through the holder's own alignment. Lens two: whom it helped or hurt, read through the holder's friends and rivals — for a town or a court, FIRST its LANDED relationship with the town the deed helped or hurt (`relationshipState`: `rival · cold_war · hostile · allied …`, with trust and resentment), AS THE HOLDER BELIEVES IT (`readBeliefRelationship`; RULINGS-ROUND4 §E) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — OD-RN-48]; for a person or the party, RN's regard link. PERSONALITY sets the mix (the landed trait adjectives); a holder with no tie to anyone the deed touched judges the act alone. A good or neutral rival grumbles and holds nothing against the doer; an unambiguously evil rival keeps the grudge; an evil holder with no tie shrugs at a kindness and holds no grudge; condemning a deed good on its face without a reason costs the condemner its own people's regard (one bounded term, never legitimacy); an ambiguous act reads NEUTRAL until the DM marks it. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-1 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-1, executed): lens two reads the landed rivalry first · RN-r1-3: evil shrugs at kindness · RN-r1-8: the mix keys on landed traits.] A TOWN's character enters lens one only as the alignment axes' LANDED BANDS (`lawWordFor`, `natureWordFor`) and never through the aggression drive, so the host's catalogued alignment defects can move a town's reading only across a band edge (§6.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7 (RULINGS-ROUND1 ADDENDUM, as worded)].

**RN10 — THE PARTY IS JUDGED, NEVER JUDGES.** The party is a SUBJECT of fame and regard and a holder of credibility; it holds no regard of anyone, because the party's feelings are the table's. The engine never moves, captures or harms the party (PT's law); RN only reads what the world believes of it. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-7: the brief left the party's own regard unstated; a party-held regard would be the engine deciding the players' feelings.]

**RN11 — THE DM'S HAND IS TOTAL.** The DM may set any holder's regard of any subject at will (a `dmSet` that wins until the DM lifts it), author seeds (the allies-and-enemies list: `declaredStance: ally · enemy`, or any regard band as an authored baseline), strike any deed (a mask, so unstriking is exact), and override a deed's moral weight (PT's mark). Every act is one typed DM verb through the established store-action lane (requirement 14; L10's one door) under the AUTHOR hat — stage-then-approve, the landed shape of every AUTHOR act (ODQ §129) — with its inverse for undo. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-9 · RN-r1-15 · RN-r1-23.] The AI proposes typed operations only (finite semantics).

**RN12 — RENOWN LEANS, NEVER WRITES OTHER TRUTHS.** Renown never writes legitimacy, a court's credibility stock, a share, a strength or a stressor. Where fame or regard should matter to another program, it enters as ONE bounded lean `{ mult, reason }` at ONE landed seam that already receives `worldState` (§5.4), each lean named and capped; it never takes the coup's open slot (the budget is 0.815 of 0.82).

**RN13 — BEHIND THE SCENES.** The surface speaks in the world's words ("known across the river towns as a fair judge"; "in Varn they curse the party's name"); the reasons and the path of a tale appear on scrutiny (the Why panel, the cause walk); bands, weights and terms only on an intentional look. Unpredictable in prospect, legible and coherent on scrutiny: every regard has its reason, every fame its deeds.

**RN14 — THE MOUNT RULE.** Zero lines in `pulseKernel.js` (1581), `applyWorldPulse.js` (907) and `warTermination.js` (818); nothing in `generosityKernel.js` (800) or `armyTransitKernel.js` (798). Every RN module is a leaf under `src/domain/renown/`; every host it enters is named with its effective lines (§5, §10), and the one writer of persisted regard rides THE ONE PERSON STAGE in `envoyPulse.js` (RULED by the chair, `briefs/RULINGS-AFTER-COMPILE.md` RN-S1; §5.1).

**RN15 — THE PHYSICAL WORLD IS A SEAM.** Until the physical world lands, a place is a settlement and "how far word goes" is the rumor road's own scope words over settlement hops; afterwards the road arms take map distance through the rumor road's own relay timing (`hopWeeks`, the curve `hopDelayTicks` also prices) unchanged, and the omniscient arm stays undelayed (the landed identity) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the carried minor on §4.3]. Every RN wave is whole without a canonized map: aspatially, a deed is known at once inside its projected scope (the omniscient arm, §8). On a map-module world (HOST-REQUIREMENTS-2026-10-18 H5/H22) `hopDelayTicks` reads the digest the host rebuilds, unchanged; the road arm keys on the preset's infoMode, never on the canon's entitlement (§8). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — HOST-r1-2.]

**RN16 — ONE IDENTITY PER PERSON.** A person-keyed link (as holder or subject) keys on the durable `wnpc_` id and nothing else (`characterDrift.js`'s law: positional ids rebind on reroll). Graduation is the ledger's own (`graduateNpc`); until `npcConsequencesEnabled` lights (C1's lighting unit, OD-FC-39's slot), RN's regard is town-and-court level only — bodies derive their regard from their ruler's character over the versions their town heard (the deeds inside scope in the omniscient arm) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-2] — and person fame is office fame plus the deeds of persons that already have a durable key (the party has its own ref, `{kind:'party'}`). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6 (`briefs/RULINGS-ROUND2b.md`: "a corps keys in RN as `u:<unitKey>` (a UNIT holder/subject; the `c:` prefix stays the court body; the refused-pair rule does not apply to `u:`)"): a CORPS (CH §16b) is not a person and takes no `wnpc_`; it keys on WC's free-unit key (`spatialLedgers.freeUnits[unitKey]`, WC §1.7.2: `unit.<rootOriginId>.<fissionTick>` for a fission product; a DM-authored company's key minted by WC's DM-authority birth event, CH §16b) and on nothing else. It exists only where CH's `paragonCorpsEnabled` and WC-10's `freeCompaniesEnabled` are lit — over the person tier, because CH's pass runs only under `npcConsequencesActive` — so until then no `u:` key exists anywhere.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes: a NAMED BODY keys on THE BODIES' durable `bodyId` (`body:<kind>:<fnv>`, BD §2.2 — minted once by BD's one writer, collision-checked, world-scoped) and nothing else — `g:<bodyId>` for a gang, its creed's `creed:<deityRef>` for a conclave (§2.2); it exists only where BD's `bodiesEnabled` is lit and only from the body's naming (L-BD 32b/33b), so until then no `g:` key exists anywhere.]

---

## §2 THE ONTOLOGY

### 2.1 The records (the only persisted shapes; lifecycles in §14)

**THE DEED** — `spatialLedgers.deeds = { [deedId]: Deed }`, append-only, one writer (`src/domain/renown/deedLedger.js :: mintDeed / strikeDeed`), absent-when-dark, drop-when-empty. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-9: RN owns the deed ledger because every person's fame is stored in it (party, paragons, graduated persons); PT owns the party-mark fields and the party doors that call `mintDeed`; CH owns the paragon sources; one normalizer names every field (the `normalizeEntry` lesson).]

| Field | Type | Owner | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `deedId` | `deed.<fnv>` | RN | zero-rng FNV-1a of the canonical composite `eventRef · tick · ordinal` (RULINGS-ROUND2 B), the `npcLedger` mint idiom WHOLE — `deed.` + `fnv1a32` hex (`kernel/proseHash.js :: fnv1a32`, the estate's one hash idiom, which `npcLedger.js` carries locally) over the three joined by the ledger's `KEY_DELIM` (`'\|'`), COLLISION-CHECKED against `spatialLedgers.deeds` exactly as `npcLedger.js :: mintDurableNpcId` is ("on a taken id the probe re-hashes the composite key with an incrementing salt … up to MINT_PROBE_BUDGET times" — 64 — then the widened form; it "never returns a taken id"), its idempotency asked FIRST of the stored `actRef` (below), never of the hash [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — found applying RULINGS-ROUND3 A.14 ("`hashInt` → the landed `hash01` / `fnv1a32`"): with idempotency read off a 32-bit hash alone, two DIFFERENT acts whose composites collide would read as one act and the second deed would be refused in silence — about a 5% chance of at least one collision across twenty thousand deeds, a century at the DRAFT rate (PLAUSIBLE arithmetic); the landed mint's doctrine is that a total mint beats a lucky one]. `eventRef` is the act's own ref as its door holds it — the composer event's id where the composer supplied one (`sourceEventRef`), else the feed key the party door's outcome writes (`feedRef`, tick-free by the landed code), else the door's own record key (the verdict's news id, the pledge's engagement id, `voice:<holderKey>:<subjectKey>`, a DM verb's command id); `tick` is `worldState.tick` read at the door (`applyPartyImpact`: `const tick = worldState.tick;`), never `now` and never the `runRecordPartyImpact` wall-clock stamp; `ordinal` is 0 for every door whose act has a unique ref (the composer's event id, the verdict's news id, the pledge's engagement id, a voice, a DM verb's command id), so a door re-reading its own record mints nothing; ONLY for a party-door act keyed by its repeating `feedRef` is it the number of deeds already carrying that `feedRef` at that tick (counted inside `mintDeed` from `deedIndex`), so two identical panel acts in one tick are two deeds [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-18] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8 (RULINGS-ROUND2 B): the tick enters the id, so the party door's tick-free feed key (three distinct removals share `party.remove_npc.remove_an_npc.s1`, EXECUTED by V-RN) never collapses two acts] |
| `tick` | integer | RN | the world tick of the act |
| `source` | `DEED_SOURCES` member | RN | which door minted it (§2.4) |
| `sourceEventRef` | string, or absent | RN (the door supplies it) | the COMPOSER event's id — on every `party_event` deed minted from a composer event (PT's own verbs — `SEND_WORD`, `RECORD_MEETING`, the answer `no`, the backing resolutions — have no composer event and key by their command id, `deedId` above) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-PT-r2 §5.1(b): "always" was false for PT's own verbs], and on a `party_impact` deed ONLY when the composer supplied it (`partyEventLinkage.js :: mapEventToPartyImpact` gains `sourceEventRef: event.id`, PT §3.1); never the party door's outcome id. ONE act is ONE deed across doors: a mint is refused when a deed of a DIFFERENT source already carries the same `sourceEventRef` (`deedIndex(...).bySourceEventRef` — RULINGS-ROUND1 PT-r1-3), so a door's own repeated acts never refuse each other; PT's source-event undo strikes by it (PT §3.4) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-8 · RN-r1-10] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8 (the verifier's cure): the round-1 fallback to the feed key refused every later act of the same kind and label at a target, forever] |
| `feedRef` | string, or absent | RN (the party door supplies it) | the eventRef of the feed entry the door's outcome writes (`newsEntryForOutcome`: `sourceEventId: outcome.id`; the seeder's `const eventRef = String(entry.sourceEventId \|\| entry.id);`) — tick-free, so NEVER an identity alone: the feed-suppression guard matches `(feedRef, tick)` (§3.4) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8 · RN-r1-10: the suppression key and the cross-door identity are two fields] |
| `actRef` | string | RN (the door supplies it) | the composite's `eventRef` exactly as the door handed it to `mintDeed` (the `sourceEventRef`, else the `feedRef`, else the door's own record key — the verdict's news id, the pledge's engagement id, `voice:<holderKey>:<subjectKey>`, a DM verb's command id), STORED so the mint's idempotency oracle (`deedIndex(...).byRefAndTick`, keyed `actRef · tick`, its list in ordinal order) tells the same act re-read from a different act whose id collided — the `npcLedger` split between an identity oracle and a collision-checked mint; FREE (`rg -w actRef`: 0 in `src`, `tests`, master `docs` and the ledger's `docs/`, `cure/D-RN-r3-probe/census.out`); named by the one normalizer [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the deed id's collision probe, `deedId` above] |
| `kind` | the source's closed kind | RN | for `party_impact` a `PARTY_IMPACT_KINDS` key; for `party_event` a settlement event type; for `paragon_act` CH's act kind; for `npc_verdict` a `VERDICTS` member whose landed audience is `public` (`jailed · banished · criminal_founding`; `turncoat` is `dm_only` and mints nothing, §3.1); for `party_pledge` `made_good · fell_short` (PT's; `came_to_nothing` mints nothing); for `voiced_regard` `praise · condemnation` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(b) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.3): one pledge vocabulary, PT's — `broken` is landed (`REMAINING_BANDS`, `conditionGrammar.js :: DAMAGE_STATES`) and `kept` is PL's `relief_answer` word in the opposite sense] |
| `doer` | actor ref | RN | `{kind:'party'}` · `{kind:'person', id:'wnpc_…'}` · `{kind:'settlement', id}` (a court's act) — a SETTLEMENT doer's deed moves no regard of that settlement held by a TOWN or a COURT (town↔town feeling is the landed pair state, RN5; the towns and courts it touched read it only through the owning program's landed input), while a PERSON's or a paragon's regard of the doing court moves as for any doer (subject `c:<id>`: CH's `barred_at_gate` and `covert_hire_exposed`; the causes `refused_at_gate` and `banned_by`), and a settlement doer's deed adds no fame — it is a tale (RN1); `{kind:'corps', id: unitKey}` — a free company's act (CH §16b's `sold_out` and every `paragon_act` kind a company can do): a corps doer's deed moves any hearer's regard of `u:<unitKey>` exactly as a person doer's moves `p:`, and the settlement-doer rule never applies to it; `{kind:'body', id: bodyId}` — a NAMED body's act (THE BODIES' `body_act`; a patrol it cut down, PR's `patrol_clash`; a site it took or cleared, ST's `site_verdict`; an attempt on a WARDEN, CH's `warden_attempted`, a `paragon_act` kind CH registers [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — `cure/D-CH-r5.md` §5 RN] — §3.1): a body doer's deed moves any hearer's regard of the body's key (`g:<bodyId>` for a gang, its creed's `creed:<deityRef>` for a conclave) exactly as a person doer's moves `p:`, the settlement-doer rule never applies to it, and where a PARAGON LEADS the body at the act's tick (CH's `leads`, L-BD 32) the doer stays the body (one deed, one doer) and the leader rides as `ledBy` (below); a nameless body and a beast are never doers (no key, §2.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (`cure/L-BD.md` §4 RN (1)–(2); L-BD 32b/33b)]; a body's ESCAPE from a court's hunt (`hunt_taken` closing `escaped`) is its own deed (`body_act` kind `escaped`); a hunt's SUCCESS is its LEADER's — the person, paragon or company the `hunt_taken` card named is the DOER of CH's `hunt_won`, never a `ledBy` rider (`ledBy` rides only a deed whose doer is a body) — ONE WORD, ONE MEANING: RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5's "doer the LEADER (`ledBy`)" names the HUNT CARD's leader (the person, paragon or company the `hunt_taken` card names, PR's detachment `leaderRef`, CH's `leads`), which becomes the deed's `doer`; RN's `ledBy` is the DEED field for a body's leader on a body-doer deed and never appears on `hunt_won` [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — RULINGS-ROUND6 B.7 "RN (`ledBy` wording)" · BD-r5-22 (BD §9.11 "DOER the LEADER the card named (`ledBy` — …)" vs this line): RN's half is this sentence; BD's "(`ledBy` …)" on the hunt's doer is struck in BD (routed)] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5 · RN-r4-verify RU-BD-9 · RN-X1] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6: the doer values were `party · person · settlement`, so the corps' `sold_out` deed had a doer the one normalizer does not name] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-1] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V4: the round-1 rule over-reached past §2.2's refused pairs and contradicted CH §11] |
| `target` | actor ref + optional `institution` / `factionId` / `stressorId` / `siteId` | RN | whom the act was done to (a positional NPC target is carried by its `wnpc_` when graduated, else by name, never by `npc_N`); a company is `{kind:'corps', id: unitKey}` (CH's `barred_at_gate` at a company) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]; a BODY is `{kind:'body', id: bodyId}` — the target of a court's or an army's act toward it (BD's `body_act`), of a patrol's clash (PR's `patrol_clash`), of a hunt's success and a claimed bounty — ONE deed, the hunt's own door's (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5: CH's `hunt_won` at the mission's close, its doer the LEADER the `hunt_taken` card named; a claimed bounty IS that row, no third deed; the party's hand is PT's `body_ended`), the body's defeat its outcome; a nameless body or a beast as a target is carried by its `bodyId` and keys no regard: lens two finds no tie in it and reads the PLACE it troubled instead — the deed's `actAt`, the town(s) of the land the hunt cleared, read as relief's relieved town (§6.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes; L-BD 33] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.5 · RU-CH-h (`cure/D-CH-r4.md` §6 RN: "a body target (`g:` / `creed:`; none for a beast — the place)"): round 4's "the CLAIMER's own door" is the hunt's door]; `siteId` is THE SITES' record key (`spatialLedgers.worldSites[siteId]`, ST §2.1), a SUBJECT qualifier beside the anchor `{kind:'settlement', id}` (the site's anchor town), never an actor ref — the party's cleared den, a site reached or made known (PT §4: `party_cleared_site` · `party_reached_site` · `party_made_known_site`), a paragon's act at a site [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RULINGS-ROUND3 B for RN (PT's carry, `skeptic/PT-r2-verify.md`: "RN's normalizer drops unnamed fields, and its `target` names no `siteId`"): the one normalizer names it, so a site deed's qualifier survives every read] — and it is never copied into the deed's TELLING (§3.4: the telling's content adds `doerRef` and the deed's `eventRef` only), so a deed at a `keptSecret` site (THE SITES' veil, RULINGS-ROUND4 A.16) reveals the site to no court's belief; whether the deed's LINE names the site is PT's line rule (routed) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — found checking A.16] |
| `witnessIds` | settlement ids, codepoint-sorted | RN (PT suggests) | the hop-0 witnesses: the target's settlement(s) + where the doer was present (the rumor road's witness meaning) |
| `projectedScope` | `settlement · regional · realm` | RN | frozen at mint (§3.2) |
| `reach` | HOP RINGS `[[ids at hop 0], [ids at hop 1], …]` — codepoint-sorted ids, EVERY reached settlement, no cap (at most `maxHopsFor + 1` rings) | RN | the frozen BFS result the observer read consults; the decayed scope shrinks through its rings (§4.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-29 · RN-r1-26: with no cap a `realm` deed is never `realm` at the world level and `unknown` at a town it reached; rings of ids, not an id→hop map, carry it in fewer bytes] |
| `magnitude` | `0..3` | RN | `rumorNetwork.js :: magnitudeBandOf(severity)` of the act's own magnitude (the DM's for a party impact) — a BAND, never the DM's 0..1 number (`if (s >= 0.8) return 3; if (s >= 0.55) return 2; if (s >= 0.3) return 1;`), so every default `resolve_stressor` · `clear_condition` · `remove_npc` (`partyImpactKinds.js`: `defaultMagnitude: 1.0`) is band 3 — `major` significance and §6.1's outward band step [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-22] |
| `backing` | `{ by: { kind: 'settlement', id }, backerRole, factionId?, creed? }` — `backerRole ∈ BACKER_ROLES` (`town_court · town_power · liege_court · paramount_court · creed_clergy`, PT §2.1) | PT | the DM's sanction mark ("sanction" is the prose word; `sanctioned` is a landed economic channel) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(d) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.6): PT's closed `backerRole` and its two qualifiers are named by RN's one normalizer] |
| `moralWeight` | `heroic · admirable · neutral · shameful · villainous` | PT | the DM's override; ABSENT = RN's derived inherent value; it REPLACES the derived band for every reader THAT CAN SEE IT — the DM's view always, a hearer only where its version shows the reason part (proof `spoken` or `shown`, at PT §11.1's threshold); an `unspoken` mark reaches no hearer (§6.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(c) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.2)] |
| `proof` | `unspoken · spoken · shown` (prose: kept quiet · told · shown) | PT | how the reason travels [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RULINGS-AFTER-COMPILE RN · L-FOLD unplaced: PT's code words (R-PT-1: `told` is a landed literal, `infoLure.js :: LIE_OUTCOMES`); `spoken`/`unspoken` are registered against `espionage/operationsVoice.js`'s keys (fold §5)] |
| `reasonGiven` | boolean | PT | whether a reason was told at all (the reason itself is the DM's prose, quoted, never parsed) |
| `correctsRef` | deedId | RN | this deed corrects that one — the ONE re-read link: PT's `SEND_WORD` correction (it ADDS the parts its telling carries) and a `backing_voided` / `backing_repudiated` deed (it MASKS the original's backing inside the voider's reach) both carry it (§3.3); PT's round-1 `voidsRef` is this field [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(d) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.4)] |
| `contradicts` | boolean, only on a `correction` | PT | the DM marked the correction as exposing the original's told reason as false (`SEND_WORD(…, { contradicts: true })`, PT §10.2/§11.2) — the party's credibility LIE charge (§7) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(d) · the verifier's minor on exposures: the exposure has a carrier and the normalizer names it] |
| `actAt` | settlement ids (1–2), codepoint-sorted | PT | WHERE the act happened (PT §2.1: the place that places the party, the evidence of a pledge made good, the voice's "at Kell") [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(d)] |
| `partySeq` | integer, only on a deed whose doer is `{kind:'party'}` | PT | the deed's place in the party's order of recording, stamped at mint from PT's sub-ledger (PT3; PT §2.1: every deed whose doer is the party takes the next `partySeq`, whichever door mints it), so `whereaboutsOf` orders two acts of one tick; RN reads it nowhere, and its one normalizer NAMES it because an unnamed field is dropped on every read (the `normalizeEntry` lesson) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-PT-r2 §5.1(a): PT's round-2 field was missing from RN's normalizer] |
| `struck` | `{ tick }` | RN | the DM struck it; it counts nowhere — a MASK every read applies; nothing derived from the deed is deleted, so unstriking restores every reading exactly (§3.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-15] |
| `causedBy` | provenance ref | RN | requirement 20 |
| `ledBy` | `wnpc_` id, only on a deed whose doer is a body | RN (BD's door stamps it from CH's `leads` retainer standing at the act's tick) | the PARAGON who LED the body (L-BD 32): the deed counts among the leader's own deeds for his fame (§4.1), and a hearer whose version shows the doer part takes a second term of HALF weight about `p:<ledBy>` — the backer's idiom ("shares the credit and inherits the grudges", §6.2); absent ⇒ a faceless body (BD's default); FREE (`rg -w ledBy`: 0 in `src`, `tests`, master `docs` and the ledger's `docs/`, `cure/D-RN-r4-probe/census.out`); named by the one normalizer [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — the seam between PR §9.3's "a DEED for the body or the villain that did it" and one doer per deed: the body is the doer, its leader shares; vetoable — the alternative gives a leader nothing from his body's deeds] |

**THE REGARD ENTRY** — `spatialLedgers.regard = { [holderKey]: { [subjectKey]: RegardEntry } }`, drop-when-empty at both levels, one writer (`src/domain/renown/regardLedger.js`), absent-when-dark. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-5: an entry is persisted ONLY for what derivation cannot reproduce: (a) the DM's authored seeds and sets, (b) the version of a deed a holder heard where the rumor road delays or weathers it (the belief presets), (c) a person's first-hand meeting recorded at the table (PT's deed). In the omniscient arm every heard-deed term is derived on read from the deed ledger (§8). The chair's single-ledger preference is kept: courts, persons, champions, towns and the party's subjects share this one writer.]

| Field | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| (`warmth`, `grudge` — DERIVED, NOT persisted) | `0..1` each (4-decimal) | the two signs, each the sum of its terms' decayed contributions, computed on read from `terms`; a persisted copy would be the running stock RN6 forbids (R2 j3) |
| `terms` | `[{ deedId, ver, mag, tick, cause, sev, sign }]` ≤ 8 | one per deed (RN6); `ver` = the visible-parts MASK heard (one bit per PT part: act · size · reason · proof · backing · doer); `mag` = the MAGNITUDE BAND as heard (`0..3`; the road's `degradeTelling` shifts it — `content.magnitude = clampBand(content.magnitude + (fork.chance(0.5) ? 1 : -1));`), so `ver`, `mag` and the entry's subject (the doer AS HEARD — PT's typed `doerRef` swap) are exactly the version a body re-reads (§5.3), and THE TRIPLE (`ver`, `mag`, the heard doer) is the heard pass's re-read key: a telling whose triple differs from the stored term's is re-read, and a changed heard doer MOVES the deed's one term to the new subject (§5.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2: round 2 stored `mag` but re-read only on a changed `ver`]; `tick` is the term's decay anchor; `cause` from `REGARD_CAUSES`; the strongest live term is the ONE reason [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V2: `ver` encoded only which parts were present, so in `full_simulation` a court re-read the TRUE magnitude, a Law One read that drives lens two's `magnitude/3` and §6.1's outward step. The road's `partyIds` swap moves where a telling says the tale STARTED; RN reads that nowhere — lens two reads the deed's target, the `act` part — declared] |
| ↳ `terms`.`ver` — the `oath` bit [ADDED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c brackets-verify; RULINGS-P6 §J.3 · `skeptic/BRACKETS-p6c-verify.md` F4)] [REFINED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c brackets-verify) — the proposed widening is named at the persisted row itself, not only in §6.2 and §16, so a reader of the shape sees it; vetoable] | PROPOSED, UNSIGNED — the OWNER's persisted-shape widening (OD-RN-52 ⭐, §16) | one more bit on the visible-parts mask, `oath`: set when the telling carried the doer's public oath, never under `secret_faith`; part of THE TRIPLE's `ver`, so a better telling that adds the oath re-reads the term (§5.1); UNTIL SIGNED the mask is the six bits above and lens one reads NO oath (§6.2's §J.3 bracket); its lifecycle rows (§14) are declared at the signing |
| `declared` | a `REGARD_BANDS` word (the DM's list keeps `ally` = `fondness`, `enemy` = `enmity` as aliases) | the AUTHORED seed (`declaredStance`; a champion's authored relation) — the BASELINE every term decays toward; never evicted |
| `dmSet` | `{ band, tick }` | the DM's override; wins until lifted; never evicted |

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-21: `warmth`/`grudge` are derived and `week` is struck (each term carries its own `tick`); the voice cooldown is read from the deed ledger (§5.5), so the entry declares no voice field · SEAMS-r1-9: an authored `esteem` or `hatred` is a baseline that deeds move and time returns to, not a freeze — the owner's "both emergent and dynamic over a set amount of time"; `dmSet` stays the DM's freeze.]

**THE PARTY'S CREDIBILITY** — a holder `{ score, lastUpdateTick }` on the party sub-ledger (`spatialLedgers.party.credibility`; the sub-ledger is PT's, the holder's law is RN's, §7). The holder is the DM's view, the world's widest knowledge; every town reads the party's word only through the pledge deeds IT heard (`partyCredibilityAt`, §7). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-9.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(a) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.1: PERSISTED; RN wins, PT drops "derived"): the holder is the running fold of the landed law over the party's credibility charges, and a strike, an unstrike or a source-event undo of a charge REBUILDS it by refolding the unstruck charges in tick order — exact, because the charges are deeds and deeds are never pruned, where un-charging by subtraction is not (the `SCORE_MAX: 12` clamp; EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r2-probe/r2Probe.out.json` e: −4.2356 by refold against −4.1017 by subtraction) (§7).]

### 2.2 The regard keys (closed grammar; RN-local, mapped from the actor-ref convention `{kind, id?}`)

| Key | Holder? | Subject? | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `p:<wnpc>` | yes | yes | a graduated person (an ordinary NPC, a court member, a paragon) |
| `u:<unitKey>` | yes | yes | a free company carrying the paragon mark (THE CORPS, CH §16b; actor ref `{kind:'corps', id: unitKey}`) — a holder as a person is: its authored relations (CH's `relations[]` through `DECLARE_STANCE`), the DM's sets and the deeds heard at the town its unit stands at (§5.1); its lens is its mark's authored `alignment` and `character.axes` (§6.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6 (RULINGS-ROUND2b): CH's round-2 `c:<unitKey>` would have read a company as a court BODY with a derived regard and refused it any feeling about the court that hires it] |
| `g:<bodyId>` | yes (a NAMED gang) | yes (a NAMED gang) | a gang of THE BODIES once BD names it (L-BD 32b/33b; actor ref `{kind:'body', id}`, its kind a field of its record) — a holder as a person is: the DM's sets, and the deeds heard at its home (§5.1) — its feeling for the courts that hunt, harbour, employ or sponsor it (BD §2.1; L-BD 46); a subject a town's commons may fear or love, and a court's regard of it is derived (§5.3); its lens the villain's axes THE BODIES gives a gang, or its leader's chart (§6.2); lit only with BD's `bodiesEnabled` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (`cure/L-BD.md` §4 RN (1)); census: no quoted `g:` literal in master `src`, `cure/D-RN-r4-probe/census.out`] |
| `party` | **never** (RN10) | yes | the party (`{kind:'party'}`, one per campaign; a second party is PT's additive slot) |
| `s:<settlementId>` | yes | yes (as the town) | the town's people, the commons |
| `c:<settlementId>` | authored/DM only (RN7) | yes | the court — a BODY, its regard derived |
| `f:<settlementId>:<factionId>` | authored/DM only | yes | a faction — a body |
| `creed:<deityRef>` | authored/DM only | yes | a creed's faithful — a body (the followers act; a god holds nothing); ALSO the key of a CONCLAVE of THE BODIES — a moving flock keys its creed (BD §2.1: "a conclave's key is the creed's EXISTING key"), so a named conclave's deeds move hearers' regard of the creed, never of a second key [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (`cure/L-BD.md` §4 RN (1))] |
| `c:<rootId>` read as THE KINGDOM | authored/DM only | yes | a kingdom (PL's polity) IS its root's court: the key for "the kingdom of Merewick" as a subject; as a body its regard is felt in a member's court through the paramount's reach (§5.3); with `polityEnabled` dark there is no kingdom, only each court's own `c:` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-12] |

A menace is never a key: it is an ACTOR of the convention (`settlement · person · party · menace · corps · body` — SIX kinds, `briefs/RULINGS-ROUND4.md` A.12 amending RULINGS-ROUND3 A.7, a body's kind a field as a person's archetype is) because its DM-authored course chooses for it (MN12, MN6), yet it holds no regard and is regarded only through MN's response channels; a SITE is a WHERE and a subject, never an actor and never a key (A.7; RULINGS-ROUND2b ADDENDUM ST-r2-14) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.7 · `skeptic/FOLD-r2-verify.md`'s minor ("The site rationale also excludes `menace`"): the menace's reason is its authored course, not "no mind"]. The `p:` and `u:` keys are also the `subjectKey` of PL's one ban grammar `polity_ban:<subjectKey>:<banTick>` (A.8; PL §4); RN mints no ban and keeps no ban field [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.8, RN's half: the key grammar named as the ban's subject]. A BODY (THE BODIES) is the sixth kind: it DECIDES (its weekly choice, BD §4), and once NAMED it is a key — `g:` for a gang, the creed's key for a conclave; a BEAST holds and is no key (BD10) and, like a menace, is never regarded by RN — so a court's threat from a pack is THE BODIES' own read of the pack's believed harm, never an RN fame (BD §2.5 `bodyThreat01`, cured on BD's side in round 5; RN supplies nothing for a beast) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD-r4-8, routed "BD or RN": BD's half carries it, RN's law stands]; a NAMELESS body (below BD's naming threshold — L-BD 32b/33b: "NO belief record of a body (item 45 opens at naming)") is no key either. The threshold OPENS a body's renown — its key, its fame and its deeds as a doer — exactly where it opens the town's belief record of it (L-BD 45), and RN mints, stores and derives nothing about a nameless body [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RULINGS-ROUND4 A.12 · B (L-BD 32b/33b) · BD's routes].

**Refused pairs.** A holder `s:`/`c:` never holds an entry about a subject `s:`/`c:` (another town or court): that feeling is the landed pair state (`relationshipState`, law E14), which lens two reads (§6.2); `DECLARE_STANCE` between two towns is refused at the writer and the verb card points the DM to the landed relationship acts (the composer's `SETTLEMENT_DISPUTE` · `BROKERED_ALLIANCE`). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-1.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6: the rule binds `s:` and `c:` only — a `u:` holder may hold an entry about a town or a court (the company's feeling for the court that retains it, CH §4.4 and §7), a town's commons may hold one about a `u:` (a company's deeds heard), and a court's regard of a company is derived like any other (§5.3).] [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-11, the third volume to ask RN for court↔court feeling (after PS-r2-17 and RN-V4): `regardOf` and `courtRegardOf` asked for a refused pair return `null` with the reason `pair_state` — never a derived number — and a program that needs how one town or court stands with another reads the landed pair state (`relationshipState`; `pairNet`, §2.3, is RN's READ of that state for lens two, never an RN regard); RN-1 pins it (§13), so no later reader can take a court's feeling about a town from RN.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RULINGS-ROUND3 A.4 · RN-V6 (RN unchanged; R51 stands): A.4 rules that CH §11's `enemy_in_service` read the landed pair label and CH §9's leak move the pair through a typed relationship patch (cause `paragon_leak`) — CH has applied both (CH §11: "a court whose LANDED PAIR LABEL with this court is `hostile`"; `paragon_leak`, checked by grep at 18:0x); PS has struck "and a court's fame moves" at both sites (PS l.122 and l.577, checked by grep at 18:0x) — so no volume asks RN for a court's feeling about a court, or for a court's fame.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes: the rule binds `s:`/`c:` × `s:`/`c:` only, so `g:` stands on either side of every other pair — a town's commons fear or love a gang (`s:` × `g:`), a court's regard of a gang is derived like any other (`c:` × `g:`, §5.3 — a sponsor's of its client, L-BD 46), a gang feels for the courts that hunt, harbour, employ or sponsor it (`g:` × `c:`). ONE SECOND REFUSED CLASS: a body's feeling about another BODY — `g:` × `g:`, and `g:` × a conclave's `creed:` — is THE BODIES' pair state (`spatialLedgers.bodies.pairs`, the landed labels `allied · neutral · rival · hostile`, one writer, BD §7.2), never an RN entry: the writer refuses it, `regardOf` returns `null` with the same reason `pair_state`, and lens two reads BD's label (§6.2).] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND4 §E OD-RN-48 (the verifier's FAIL A.8): the refused-pair rule STANDS (null, reason `pair_state`), and a program that reads the landed pair state instead reads it THROUGH BELIEF where the read DECIDES for an actor (`beliefMap.js :: readBeliefRelationship(observerId, subjectId, worldState, truthType)`, A.8's form, the truth type only its fallback input) and as truth only where it resolves the physics (CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)); RN's own `pairNet` is such a read (§2.3, §6.2).] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the verifier's minor ("The `g:` × `creed:` refusal also forbids a gang any feeling about a creed's faithful in a town"), DECLARED: the creed key is ONE key for a conclave and for that creed's faithful (BD §2.1: "a conclave's key is the creed's EXISTING key"), so the second refused class is whole — a gang's feeling about a creed, its flock or its faithful, lives only in THE BODIES' `pairs` (with that creed's named flock); where BD holds no pair the gang has no tie (lens two reads none), never an RN entry. A town's commons, a court or a person may still feel about the creed (`s:`/`c:`/`p:` × `creed:` is never refused).] [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-15 (WC-RN-49; RULINGS-P5 C.15: "a DM-authored f:×f: regard is refused by the writer (RN-49 — `factionPairStates` is the one home)"): A THIRD REFUSED CLASS — a faction's feeling about another FACTION, `f:<s>:<a>` × `f:<s′>:<b>`, is the landed faction-pair state (`worldPulse/factionPairLedger.js`: "a SYMMETRIC pairwise sub-record carrying BOTH signs — resentment AND alliance/trust", `worldState.factionPairStates[factionPairKey(a, b)]`, CONFIRMED by read at `81f1cd963`), never an RN entry. An `f:` holder is authored/DM only (above), so the class closes the one road a second faction↔faction truth could enter: a `DECLARE_STANCE` or `SET_REGARD` naming two factions is REFUSED at the writer, its card pointing the DM to the faction layer (that pair moves only by its own typed incidents, `factionPairLedger.js :: mintFactionPairIncident`); `regardOf` and `courtRegardOf` asked for it return `null` with the reason `pair_state`; a program that needs how one faction stands with another reads `factionPairOf(worldState, a, b)`, never RN. `f:` still stands on either side of every OTHER pair (a town's commons, a court, a person, a company or a gang may feel about a faction; a faction's baseline about a person, a company, a gang or the party may be authored)]

**Backers as subjects** (PT's `backing`, the backer's half-weight term in §6.2): a backer maps to ONE key by PT's closed `backerRole` — `town_court` → `c:<by.id>`; `town_power` → `f:<by.id>:<factionId>`; `liege_court` → `c:<by.id>` (the liege's seat); `paramount_court` → `c:<by.id>` (the root); `creed_clergy` → `creed:<creed>` (the faithful body; there is no per-town clergy key); with `polityEnabled` dark the two polity roles are not offered (PT §2.1). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-11.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2 A.6: PT's five roles win — the crown and the liege are told apart; the round-1 `polity: true` row is struck.]

### 2.3 The derived reads (signatures; all pure, rng-free, memoized per snapshot by WeakMap, the `tickIndices.js` idiom)

[REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)'s corpus law ("a shared leaf reads BELIEF when it DECIDES for an actor and TRUTH when it RESOLVES the physics; its owner names which, once"), named once for every RN read: BELIEF — `heardAt`, `fameIn`, `readDeed`'s version, `regardOf` and `courtRegardOf` in the road arms, `partyCredibilityAt`, the notoriety projection at an observer, `pairNet` (the holder's landed pair state AS IT BELIEVES IT, which A.15's relation factor also reads, §5.5); TRUTH — `fameOf` (the world's widest knowledge, the DM's view), `deedIndex`, `inherentValueOf` (the act's face from the deed record), `partyCredibilityOf` (the DM's view); in the omniscient and aspatial arms the belief reads ARE the truth (the landed identity). A caller never re-reads.] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND4 §E OD-RN-48 (the verifier's FAIL A.8: "§2.3 (l.181) lists `pairNet` under 'TRUTH'"): `regardOf`/`courtRegardOf` are inputs to actors' CHOICES, so the pair state they consult through `pairNet` is read through belief; `pairNet` moves from the TRUTH list to the BELIEF list; RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-1's "the landed relationship FIRST" stands — read through belief.]

- `fameOf(worldState, actorRef, tick) → { scope: FAME_SCOPES member, office01, deeds: [{ deedId, scope }] }` — §4; `actorRef` is a person, a corps, the party or a NAMED body (RN1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes].
- `deedRingAt(worldState, actorRef, settlementId, tick) → { scope, deeds: [deedId] }` — THE ACTOR'S OWN RING: where the actor's OWN deeds still ring by its own reckoning — each unstruck deed whose doer is the actor (a `ledBy` deed for its leader, as §4.1 counts it), decayed exactly as §4.1 and its ring found by `RING_BY_DECAY` (§4.3), the settlement counting when it lies inside that ring; the widest such decayed scope is `scope`. NO HEARD TEST: an actor knows its own deeds (the `belief` read's self arm, "`if (String(observerId) === String(subjectId)) return { source: 'truth' };`"), so this is `fameIn`'s omniscient-arm form taken in EVERY arm, by the ONE ring rule — never a second. BELIEF (the actor's own, named once, CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)): its reader is an actor deciding on its OWN notoriety — THE BODIES' gang choosing where it is NOT known (BD §6.8 (a) and (e); RULINGS-P5 C.15: "the gang's founding read = its OWN belief of its notoriety (BD-34)") — never another town's heard test and never `believedBodyOf`; pure, memoized per snapshot [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — BD's route (`cure/D-BD-p5.md` §4: "§4.3 — expose the ring test over an actor's OWN deeds (`RING_BY_DECAY` around each deed's place, no heard test; `fameIn`'s omniscient-arm form) as one named read, or admit that arm by argument, for BD §6.8 (e)"): one named read; FREE (`deedRingAt` 0 in master `src` · `tests` · `docs` and the ledger's docs, `cure/D-MN-RN-p5b-p5-probe/tokens-rn.out`)].
- `fameIn(worldState, actorRef, observerSettlementId, tick) → { scope, heard: [deedId], arm: 'omniscient'|'road'|'aspatial' }` — fame as belief at one town (§4.3).
- `inherentValueOf(worldState, deed) → { kind01, law01, force01, ambiguous: boolean }` — §6.1 (`inherentValue`, never stored).
- `heardAt(worldState, deed, observerSettlementId, tick) → { heard: boolean, arm, ver, mag, doerKey, heardTick }` — THE ONE HEARD TEST (§4.3): in the omniscient and aspatial arms, inside the deed's WHOLE projected reach at once (no delay: `hopDelayTicks` reads 0 there, the landed identity) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the carried minor on §4.3]; in the road arms, a live telling there or a stored heard term; a CAPPED deed (an `npc_verdict` deed, §3.1) is heard only inside its capped `reach`, in every arm [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7]; `ver` and `mag` the version heard; `doerKey` the doer AS HEARD — the live telling's `doerRef` while its doer part is visible (`ver`'s doer bit, VAGUE 0.3), else the subject of the stored term at the town's `s:` holder once the telling is gone, `null` when the version names no doer, and the deed's own doer in the omniscient and aspatial arms [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2: the doer as heard is part of the version, so fame, regard and the whereabouts read one doer]. Regard, a body's re-reading and the party's credibility at a town read it; fame adds the decayed ring [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V1 · RN-r1-11]. `heardTick` is when that version was heard: the telling's `arrivalTick` in the road arms (the rumor module keeps the earliest — `arrivalTick: Math.min(existing.arrivalTick, incoming.arrivalTick)`), the stored heard term's `tick` once the telling has expired, `deed.tick` in the omniscient and aspatial arms. `heardAt` is therefore THE PER-DEED ARRIVAL READ that CH's `believedWhereabouts` composes: the newest heard deed whose doer AS HEARD is the paragon (`deedIndex(...).byDoer`, then `heardAt` at the observer's town, whose `doerKey` must name it; newest `heardTick`, ties by deed tick, then id), its place the deed's `actAt[0]`, else its first witness; a deed done by someone ELSE about the paragon (the paragon its target) places nobody, and a version heard under another doer places nobody [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RULINGS-ROUND3 A.12 ("the newest deed WHOSE DOER is the paragon (RN §2.3 wins)"); CH aligned the same round] — RN mints no second arrival test [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-CH-r2 §5.2(c): CH's read was PLAUSIBLE until RN named it].
- `readDeed(worldState, holderKey, deed, version) → { sign, sev, cause } | null` — the two lenses (§6.2); `version = { ver, mag, doerKey }` as heard (the deed's own parts in the omniscient arm) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V2].
- [ADDED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c; `cure/L-CH-PD.md` §4 RN (a) · RULINGS-P6 §F.1 · §F.3) — `lensOne(coords, face) → number` — LENS ONE'S PURE CORE, EXPORTED: the `act` formula of §6.2 over a holder's three coordinates `coords = { lawfulness01, malice01, aggr01? }` and a deed face's three `face = { kind01, law01, force01 }`, rng-free; `readDeed` CALLS it (one reading law, never a copy), and THE CHAMPIONS' sworn lean (`swornLean`, CH §2.2 / CH22 — the token of RULINGS-P6 §F.3; L-CH-PD's `patronLean` renamed) calls the same export with the sworn god's coordinates as CH supplies them. PLAUSIBLE until RN-3's car-0 names it; CH-11's car-0 waits on it (H-PD-4: "one reading law, never a copy in CH"). The MAPPING from a god's recorded character to the coordinates is CH's (RULINGS-P6 §F.1: MONOTONE in the god's character — a warlike god's sworn leans MORE toward war deeds than a peacelike god's), never RN's.]
- `regardOf(worldState, holderKey, subjectKey, tick) → { band, warmth, grudge, reason, arm }` — §5; for a body it composes `courtRegardOf`.
- `courtRegardOf(worldState, holderKey, subjectKey, tick) → { band, members: [{ key, weight, band }], record: [deedId], baseline }` — §5.3.
- `actingOfficer(worldState, settlementId, act) → { key, office, band }` — §5.2.
- `ransomRegardBand(worldState, homeSettlementId, personKey, tick) → 'forgotten'|'valued'|'beloved'` — §4.6's projection, the producer the ransom leaf lacks.
- `notorietyProjection(fameScope, moralRead) → NOTORIETY_BANDS member` — §4.4; also a NAMED body's notoriety at a town, read by THE BODIES (L-BD 38, 42 (e)) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes].
- `partyCredibilityOf(worldState, tick) → { score, weight, band }` — the DM's view (§7).
- `partyCredibilityAt(worldState, observerSettlementId, tick) → { score, weight, band, heard: [deedId] }` — the party's word as ONE town believes it: the landed person law folded over only the `party_pledge` deeds and the contradicting corrections (exposures, `contradicts: true`) that town heard by `heardAt` (§7) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-9] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor: the exposure's carrier is named].
- `pairNet(worldState, snapshot, holderSettlementId, otherSettlementId) → { net, label, labelIsTrue, trust, resentment }` — the landed pair state AS THE HOLDER BELIEVES IT, as lens two's net on [−1, 1] (§6.2's table): `label` = `readBeliefRelationship(holderSettlementId, otherSettlementId, worldState, trueLabel)` (the true label only that read's fallback input; a TRUE `vassal` read true — the landed contract carve-out), `labelIsTrue` whether that label is the true one (κ's trust/resentment term rides only such a label) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-1] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND4 §E OD-RN-48; EXECUTED `cure/D-RN-r5-probe/r5Probe.out.json` A].
- `deedIndex(worldState) → { byDoer, byTarget, byWitness, bySourceEventRef, byFeedRef, byRefAndTick }` — a per-snapshot index (WeakMap); every read finds its deeds through it and none scans the ledger; `byRefAndTick` keys on the stored `actRef` and the tick — the mint's idempotency oracle and the ordinal's count [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the collision probe, §2.1] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-31] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8: the feed guard's and the ordinal's two keys].
- THE BODIES' `believedBodyOf(worldState, townId, bodyId)` (L-BD 45: "believed size band · believed den · last heard · the leader if known — the rumor ledger with the body as doer, RN1's tale idiom") is BD's ONE read, never an RN store: RN supplies its renown inputs and nothing else — the deeds the town HEARD with the body as doer as heard (`deedIndex(...).byDoer`, then `heardAt(...)`: its `heardTick` the record's "last heard" of a deed, the deed's `actAt` its place — the composition `believedWhereabouts` uses for a paragon, one arrival read), the body's fame there (`fameIn`), the commons' regard of it (`regardOf(s:<townId>, g:<bodyId>)`, or the creed's key for a conclave) and the leader where the version shows the doer part (`ledBy`); the might SEEN is a SIGHTING ROW of MN's ONE sighting store (`spatialLedgers.sightings`, keyed court × subject — the subject `{ kind: 'body', id }`), written by its one door `recordSighting` into the sighting court's belief and READ by BD's `believedBodyOf` (RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2; RULINGS-ROUND5 A.5: "never onto ST's holder object"; ST stores none), never an RN fact [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r6) — RULINGS-ROUND6 B.2: "BD's own record" → the one store MN owns] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND5 A.5, routed by `cure/D-ST-r5.md` §5 ("RN l.197 … → 'a sighting row of BD's own record (A.5)'"): round 4's "THE SITES' `seenBy` belief write into BD's record" (A.17) named a write ST no longer makes]; the record OPENS at naming (L-BD 32b/33b) because RN's inputs exist only for a named body [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RULINGS-ROUND4 B (L-BD 45 · 32b/33b)]. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — `courtRegardOf`'s parameter was `bodyKey`; THE BODIES' `bodyId`/`bodyKind` now share the surface, so RN's derived-holder parameter takes the plain word `holderKey` (no code exists; a signature word, §2.4's `body` homonym)].

### 2.4 The closed vocabularies (each checked with `rg -w` on master; receipts `cure/L-RN-probe/tokenCensus.out.tsv`; fold §5 rows owed at P4)

| Vocabulary | Words | Census and ruling |
|---|---|---|
| `FAME_SCOPES` | `unknown · settlement · regional · realm` | REUSES `RumorContent.scope` (`'settlement' \| 'regional' \| 'realm'`, the road's own "how far word goes") plus `unknown` (the landed `NOTORIETY_BANDS` mint rung, same meaning). `realm` here is the campaign world — the rumor road's landed meaning; the kingdom takes `polity`. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-2: fame's band IS its scope; no fame word list is minted (R2 recommended `fameScope` with the scope words; the field is unnecessary because the band and the scope are one).] |
| `REGARD_BANDS` | `hatred · enmity · mistrust · indifference · fondness · esteem · adoration` (ascending; `indifference` = no feeling) | FREE or PROSE-ONLY: `hatred` (2 comments), `enmity` (one reason-string literal in `settlementPolitics.js`, same meaning — registered), `mistrust` (1 prose line), `indifference` (3 prose), `fondness` · `esteem` · `adoration` (0). [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-1: the champions brief's ladder `hatred · enmity · wary · neutral · warm · loyal · devoted` collides: `wary` is a `houseActs.js` rung and a `religiousAcceptance` value; `warm` is `canonRelationshipImpact.js`'s polarity; `loyal` is a personality TRAIT read by `thirdPartyRansom.js :: REFUSE_TRAITS`; `devoted` a mentor tone; `neutral` an `ALIGNMENT_READS` rung on the same roamer facets surface.] |
| `REGARD_CAUSES` | `heard_deed · met · pledge_kept · pledge_broken · ransomed_by · abandoned_to_captivity · refused_at_gate · banned_by · betrayed · authored · condemned_a_good_deed · praised · condemned · paid_well · sheltered · slandered` | the incident types (the dominant one is the reason); FREE as literals (`gift` is prose) except `abandoned_to_captivity`, the LANDED grievance kind (`ransomChoices.js :: ABANDONMENT_GRIEVANCE_KIND = 'abandoned_to_captivity'`, "One kind, one owner") consumed, never re-worded — its term decays on `a_generation` (the grievance is kept; the regard it colours fades only across a generation); PT/CH append rows by registration only [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-19] [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-14 (WC-RN-50; RULINGS-P5 C.14: "RN's `REGARD_CAUSES` appends CH's `paid_well · sheltered · slandered` through CH's registration row (RN's lane; CH cites)"): APPENDED through CH's registration row (CH §10: each with its ONE door, holder and subject, through RN's one regard writer — `paid_well` the paragon's or company's warm term toward the court that retained it on `handsome`/`princely` terms; `sheltered` toward the court of a member town that admitted it under its crown's ban; `slandered` toward the LIAR court of an IN lie that named it, DARK until IN's lie family reopens `LURE_AXES` `employer`, SLOT C1), so RN's §14 normalizer now keeps them; FREE as literals (`paid_well` 0 `src`; `slandered` one `src` prose comment) save `sheltered`, a HOMONYM of the landed `defensiveTerrain` band (`mapProfile.js`: "`'exposed', 'open', 'mixed', 'sheltered'`" — `cure/D-MN-RN-p5b-p5-probe/tokens-rn.out`), a regard cause only, never printed bare (CH: "Ashford gave her shelter when Merewick's crown had barred her"); the fold §5 ↳ `REGARD_CAUSES` P5 row mirrors them.
