## §3 THE DEED — THE ONE STORE (RN-0)

### 3.1 The mint (one writer, many doors)

**Reads:** TRUTH — a deed is what the DM recorded (canon by construction, the party door's law) or what a landed engine verdict decided; it is the world's fact, and every BELIEF about it is read downstream (§4.3, §5.1). **Writes:** `spatialLedgers.deeds` through `deedLedger.js :: mintDeed({ worldState, source, sourceEventRef, feedRef, recordKey, tick, kind, doer, target, witnessIds, magnitude, partySeq, snapshot }) → { worldState, deedId, minted }` (`partySeq` from PT's door for a party doer, D-PT-r2 §5.1(a); the composite's ref STORED as `actRef` and the id collision-checked, §2.1 [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3)]) only (the ordinal: 0 for a uniquely keyed act; counted from `deedIndex` only for a party-door act keyed by its `feedRef`, §2.1) — idempotent (the same composite key `eventRef · tick · ordinal` mints nothing twice; and ACROSS DOORS, a mint whose `sourceEventRef` a deed of a DIFFERENT source already carries mints nothing — RULINGS-ROUND1 PT-r1-3, SEAMS-r1-8; a door's own repeated acts are told apart by the tick and the ordinal and never refuse each other [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8]), absent-when-dark (`renownEnabled !== true` ⇒ the same `worldState` reference, `deedId: null`). **Callers (the `DEED_SOURCES` rows):**

| Source | Door (owner) | Seam | Host eff. lines | Lit when |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `party_impact` | `partyImpact.js :: applyPartyImpact` (PT) — one call after the outcomes are built; every door that reaches it (the World Pulse panel, the composer's checkbox through `mapEventToPartyImpact`, the decree's party hand, the Surveyor, `roadsRescueInflame.js`) mints through it | `partyImpact.js` | 327 | `renownEnabled` ∧ PT's `partyEnabled` |
| `party_event` | the composer's `partyCaused` path (`settlementLifecycleHelpers.js`, `drainQueuedEvents.js`) for every party-caused settlement event that does NOT ripple — the six that ripple mint ONCE, through `party_impact` (PT's static skip: the composer's `party_event` door passes over every event `mapEventToPartyImpact` maps, PT §3.1), so R2 R-14's "attribution only — no ripple" events leave their world trace here — plus PT's other `party_event` acts: a suggestion answered, the party's hand on MN's verbs, PT's own verbs (`SEND_WORD`, `RECORD_MEETING`, the answer `no`) and the backing resolutions (PT §3.1) (PT) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-PT-r2 §5.1(b): "for EVERY party-caused settlement event, not only the six that ripple" contradicted PT's one-act-one-deed door] | those two call sites, each one call | — (PT measures at its car) | as above |
| `party_pledge` | PT's pledge verdict (`party_pledge_due`, PT §8.6): `made_good · fell_short` (`came_to_nothing` mints nothing), doer `{kind:'party'}`, target the requester, `recordKey` the engagement id [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-7] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2 A.3] | PT's engagement pass inside THE ONE PERSON STAGE | 332 (`envoyPulse.js`) | `renownEnabled` ∧ PT's `partyEnabled` |
| `paragon_act` | CH's weekly pass when a paragon uses its gift or fails (CH); and CH's HUNT DOOR at a hunt's close — the act kind `hunt_won` (CH §2.2b), ONE deed per LED hunt's success, minted at the mission's close (`hunt_taken`'s climax), its DOER the leader THE BODIES' `hunt_taken` card named — the court's public-security office-holder on the person ledger (`p:`; L-BD 40 (1); BD §9.11: "an office-holder on the person ledger by the same registered row" — the WATCH office is among RN's bounded `in_office` graduations, §5.2), a retained or bounty-answering paragon (`p:`) or a company (`u:`) — and its TARGET the body (`g:` / its creed; a beast no key — the place, §2.1); ONE HUNT, ONE DEED: PR's `patrol_clash` mints NO deed for a led hunt (its row is the clash's record), ST's `site_verdict` none for a site contest a led hunt won, and a claimed bounty IS this row (no third deed); PT's `body_ended` (the party's hand) reads its value rows (§6.1) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5 · RN-X1 = SEAMS-r4-7 (MAJOR: "One paragon-led hunt mints TWO deeds") · RU-CH-h (`cure/D-CH-r4.md` §6 RN: "`hunt_won` … joins `paragon_act`'s registered kinds") · `cure/D-BD-r5.md` / BD §19.0b RN (a): "register `hunt_won`'s doer as any leader the `hunt_taken` card names"]; and CH's WARDEN-ATTEMPT card at its close (CH §11, the road arm's second card) — the act kind `warden_attempted` (CH §2.2b, two rows: a bribe, a strike), ONE deed per card whatever its verdict, its DOER the NAMED body that made the attempt (`{kind:'body', id}`, keyed `g:<bodyId>`; a nameless body mints nothing, no key, §2.2), its TARGET the warden (`p:<wnpcId>`); the card's owner mints, never a `body_act` beside it [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — `cure/D-CH-r5.md` §5 RN, a sibling round-5 route read live by the resumed seat: "CH's registered kinds gain `warden_attempted` (doer a NAMED body by its RN key `g:<bodyId>`, target the warden `p:<wnpcId>`; two rows, bribe and strike)"] | CH's leaf in `envoyPulse.js` (RF-17); the hunt door at CH's close of the `hunt_body` aim | 332 | `renownEnabled` ∧ CH's flag ∧ `npcConsequencesEnabled`; a CORPS' acts also `paragonCorpsEnabled` ∧ WC's `freeCompaniesEnabled` (CH §16b) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]; `hunt_won` also THE BODIES' `bodyEffectsEnabled` (the hunt card's flag, B.16) — with persons dark no hunt is LED in RN's sense (no leader has a key), so a clash the riders win is `patrol_clash`'s, its doer the TOWN (below); `warden_attempted` also CH §11's TIER (`paragonGateEnabled` ∧ THE BODIES' `bodiesEnabled`) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the same route] |
| `npc_verdict` | the W-H2 verdict's own LEDGER RECORD, read by RN's pass in THE ONE PERSON STAGE (RULINGS-ROUND2 B), ADDITIVE to the stamp the verdict writes (RN4). Each tick the pass mints one deed for every person-ledger record whose `verdictCause` is a `VERDICTS` member, whose `sinceTick` is this tick or the last (the kernel applies verdicts at its verdict step, BEFORE the rumor step and the envoy pulse — measured — so either order is read, and the mint is idempotent by its record key), and whose verdict news for that tick stands in the stage's `wizardNews` (`npcVerdictApply.js :: verdictNewsIdFor(settlementId, wnpcId, tick)` — the id `verdictHeraldItem` already writes, exported as ONE formula with two readers — so a later `moveNpcRecord` restamp of an old record never reads as a new verdict); a PUBLIC verdict only (`VERDICT_AUDIENCE`, exported: `turncoat` is `dm_only` and is never told). Doer the sentenced person (`p:<wnpc>`), kind the `verdictCause`, target the sentencing court (`{kind:'settlement', id: originRef.settlementId}` — the court's institutional record), witness the sentencing settlement, magnitude the stamp's notoriety rank (`whispered` 1 · `known` 2 · `notorious`/`infamous` 3), `recordKey` the verdict's news id, `tick` the verdict's; the exposed wrong is the deed. **THE STAMP IS THE LOUDER TRUTH (RN-V7).** The deed may never out-shout the landed stamp it rides beside (Law One; one reputation truth): (1) its `projectedScope` is CAPPED at the stamp's breadth — `whispered` → `settlement`, `known` → `regional`, `notorious`/`infamous` → the walk (§3.2) — and its `reach` keeps only the cap's rings (`settlement` → ring 0, the sentencing town; `regional` → rings 0..2, `RING_BY_DECAY`'s regional rule); (2) its significance follows the cap — `notable` under a `settlement` or `regional` cap (two hops on the road, the 13-week TTL), §3.2's rule for the walk (a `notorious`/`infamous` stamp is magnitude 3, so `major`) — and §3.2's "its target is … a court" clause never applies to it, because its court target is the sentencing court's institutional record, not an act upon a court; (3) the cap binds EVERY arm: the deed is heard and known only inside its capped `reach`, whatever carrier takes its telling further (§4.3's off-reach rule never applies to a capped deed); (4) the notoriety projection it sets never reads louder than the stamp (§4.4's ceiling). The landed table stamps no `whispered` (`npcVerdictTable.js`: `jailed: 'known'`, `banished: 'notorious'`, `turncoat: 'known'`, `criminal_founding: 'notorious'`, one repeat-offender step up — EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r3-probe/r3Probe.out.json` B1), so the quietest verdict a town can read is a first jailing at `known`; the `whispered` row is kept for totality (any later stamp source). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7: since round 2 every verdict was `major` (its magnitude band ≥ 2, or its court target), walked four hops (`maxHopsFor`: 4) and projected `notorious` (`infamous` at realm) wherever the town was not warm, arming `rejectionFor`'s `strict` (`loudness >= NPC_CONSEQUENCES_TUNING.REJECTION_NOTORIETY_RANK`, 2) and its outright `infamous` refusal four hops from a jailing the table stamped `known` — EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r3-probe/r3Probe.out.json` B4.] **ITS FADE IS THE LADDER'S "A REPUTATION".** An `npc_verdict` deed's fame decays on `a_year` (`bandedStock.js :: HALF_LIFE_WEEKS`: `a_year: INTERVAL_WEEKS.one_year, // 52 — a season's grudge, a reputation`), the one decay law on its own band (as `abandoned_to_captivity`'s terms take `a_generation`): a `known` jailing reads `regional` about twenty-two weeks and is forgotten at its own town in about two years, beside the landed fade's forty weeks a band (`BELIEF_FADE_TICKS: 40`: `known` → `whispered` at week 40, `unknown` at 80) — where `a_decade` held it `regional` for over four years and known at home for twenty (EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r3-probe/r3Probe.out.json` B3; DRAFT, UNMEASURED, FP-46 roster) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7's pace half ("RN's `a_decade` pace keeps a regional verdict notorious for roughly four years, against the landed fade of one step per `BELIEF_FADE_TICKS: 40`"); the person's ONE FADE (RN4) is unchanged: each deed decays on its source's band and the landed `beliefFadeSteps` is never stacked]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-6 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-6): `npcVerdictTable.js :: npcVerdictFor` is pure ("writes nothing anywhere") and cannot host a mint.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-6 (RULINGS-ROUND2 B): the round-1 host `applyNpcVerdict` has no snapshot — its only caller is the frozen kernel — so `projectScope` could not walk the graph without a kernel line; the stage already receives `snapshot` and `regionalGraph`.] | THE ONE PERSON STAGE (RN's pass) | 332 (`envoyPulse.js`); `npcVerdictApply.js` 296 → ≈ +6 (two exports; the `in_office` re-stamp, D-RN-r2-N1, §5.2) | `renownEnabled` ∧ `npcConsequencesEnabled` |
| `voiced_regard` | RN's own heard-regard pass (§5.5) for a voice about a person, a paragon or a court; for a voice NAMING THE PARTY, PT's `party_voiced` hearing when it resolves `spoken` (PT §7.3) — RN's crossing is its producer, RN's pass hands it on, RN's writer mints the deed; a COURT's voice naming the party included (any court, heard by its standing; doer `{kind:'settlement', id}`, a tale — §5.5) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RULINGS-ROUND3 A.11] | THE ONE PERSON STAGE (RN's pass; PT's engagement pass for the party's) | 332 (`envoyPulse.js`) | `renownLeansEnabled` (voices are leans, §12); the party's also PT's `partyEngagementsEnabled`; a court's voice needs no person lit [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-6 (RULINGS-ROUND1 ADDENDUM; RULINGS-ROUND2 A.5)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.11] |

A deed at a site carries the site's anchor settlement in its `witnessIds` (and `actAt`); a site is a WHERE and a subject, NEVER an actor (RULINGS-ROUND2b ADDENDUM ST-r2-14; RULINGS-ROUND3 A.7 — the round-2 "ST's actor convention gains `site`" is withdrawn) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the verifier's minor · `skeptic/PT-r2-verify.md`'s carry ("ST §7.3 and RN l.243 still carry the withdrawn widening")], holds no regard and is never an RN key (like a menace), and a site deed names it by the target's `siteId` qualifier (§2.1); so fame and regard follow a cleared den, a fort taken or a shrine defiled through the doors that already mint: PT's `party_event` for the party (ST S8), CH's `paragon_act` for a paragon, and `site_verdict` for an engine court once ST lands it. A watchtower's witnesses ride the rumor road, so a deed's telling hears what the road hears (ST §7.1). PR reads RN at ONE seam — a traveller's cover by fame AT THE PATROL'S TOWN (`fameIn`, the belief read; never `fameOf`, the world's widest knowledge, §4.1 — PR §8.1 as cured) — and names two engine deed sources for the slot table above (`patrol_clash`, `patrol_catch`). `patrol_joins`' default between the ally court and the asking town reads the LANDED PAIR STATE in every arm (PR §10.1(d) as cured), never `courtRegardOf`: a court's feeling about a town is a refused pair (§2.2), and RN's regard could enter only through a PERSON decider (`actingOfficer`, §5.2) if a later wave wants one [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-11: this bracket itself named `courtRegardOf` for a court↔town question RN never holds]. Patrols stay a map-only picture while PR is dark; when PR lights, the host's patrol facts go OUT through the events WINDOW (H9) like every other fact — H16 is the map's INBOUND door, which the host never consumes (RULINGS-ROUND2b HOST-r2-5; PR §13) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-5, the chair's correction (routed by `cure/D-PL-r2b.md` §5.3): "opens H16" echoed the chair's withdrawn words] — so no RN read of a patrol exists before PR lights; plague-R7 (quarantine as a stance input) touches no RN read.]

**The counterforce (requirement 2):** a deed only ever ADDS a fact; what pushes back is time (§4's decay), the other lens (§6), the strike (the DM), the correction (§3.3) and the rival's voice (§5.5). No deed raises fame or regard without a half-life.

**Determinism:** ids are FNV-1a over a canonical composite (never `now`, never `Math.random()`, the `runRecordPartyImpact` wall-clock stamp is never an input — R2 hazard 15); iteration codepoint-sorted.

### 3.2 The projected scope (frozen at mint; replaces "peak reach observed")

[REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — per RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH §5 (R2 R-2): the chair's original "peak reach written as the tale arrives, a monotone max" needs a per-tick writer the frozen kernel cannot host; the projected scope is computed ONCE at mint by a pure BFS and stored, immutable — the deed's own telling, part of the event.] `deedLedger.js :: projectScope({ regionalGraph, digest, witnessIds, significance, score }) → { projectedScope, reach }`: a breadth-first walk from the witnesses over the landed carriers' adjacency — `rumorNetwork.js :: tradeNeighbours(graph, id)` (confirmed trade channels, both ways, codepoint-sorted), plus `distanceRead.js :: seaLaneNeighbourMap(digest)` and `teleportNeighbourMap(digest)` where a digest exists — to the rumor road's own hop budget `maxHopsFor({ significance, score })` (major 4, 5 at score ≥ 95; notable 2). The deed's significance comes from the DEED, not from `newsEntryForOutcome`'s severity×80 score (R2 R-18): `major` when its magnitude band is ≥ 2 or its target is a ruler, a court or a settlement-wide condition; else `notable` (DRAFT, UNMEASURED) — save an `npc_verdict` deed, whose significance, scope and reach follow its stamp's cap (§3.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7]. Scope: `settlement` when the walk reaches only the witnesses, `regional` when it reaches beyond them, `realm` when it reaches DRAFT ≥ 60% of the world's settlements. `reach` keeps every reached settlement as hop rings (codepoint-sorted; no cap) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-29]. The live army, smuggle and migrant carriers are NOT read at mint (they are transient); the rumor road still carries the tale over them in the belief presets, and the road's actual arrival is what a holder hears there (§5.1) — the projected scope is the deed's fame BAND and the omniscient arm's knowing set; in the road presets the road's own arrival is what a town knows, for fame and regard alike (§4.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-11: the REFINED rationale above is superseded on this point — the per-tick writer it said the frozen kernel could not host now exists (THE ONE PERSON STAGE), so the road arms read what was heard].

### 3.3 Strike and correction

`strikeDeed({ worldState, deedId, tick })` sets `struck: { tick }`; every read MASKS it — fame, the observer read, every regard term carrying that `deedId`, a body's re-reading — and NOTHING is deleted: the persisted terms stay where they are, skipped while the mark stands. `unstrikeDeed` clears the mark and every reading returns exactly (the terms were never dropped, so the telling's 26/13-week expiry does not matter). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-15: R2 R-13's "drop on the next normalize" made unstrike inexact once the telling had expired.] A CORRECTION is minted as its own deed (`correctsRef` = the original; PT's door: "the truth can chase the rumor") and travels the same road at the same speed; wherever it is heard, the holder's term for the original is REPLACED by its RE-READING of the original under the corrected version (§5.1) — ONE re-read mechanism for every correction: PT's `correction` ADDS the parts its own telling carries (the reason, the backing; a correction whose telling lost both brings nothing and replaces nothing, PT §11.2); a `backing_voided` or `backing_repudiated` deed MASKS the original's backing part, and only for hearers inside the voider's reach (PT §10.3); a correction marked `contradicts` is also the party's LIE charge (§7). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(d) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.4): a void is a correction (`correctsRef`), never a masked re-read of PT's own; `voidsRef` is folded into `correctsRef`.] Corrections never re-use the original's `eventRef` (`mergeArrival` keeps `relayedTick`: "a settlement never re-relays a corrected telling", R2 hazard 4).

### 3.4 The deed's telling on the rumor road

The deed seeds the road FROM ITS OWN LEDGER inside `rumorNetwork.js :: advanceRumorLedgers` (it already receives `worldState`; zero kernel lines; it bypasses the feed's significance gate and `normalizeEntry`'s allowlist, R2 R-1): a deed arm reads `spatialLedgers.deeds` for unstruck deeds minted within `RUMOR_SEED_LOOKBACK_TICKS` and seeds an arrival record at each witness with `eventRef: deed:<deedId>`, significance and score from §3.2 (a capped `npc_verdict` deed's from its cap, §3.1), and a typed content field `doerRef` (an actor-ref string; NEVER `partyIds`, whose entries are witnesses and settlement-shaped — R2 hazard 2). Host: `rumorNetwork.js` 364 → ≈ +30 in a leaf `renown/deedTelling.js` the host calls once. A non-deed event is byte-identical (the arm reads nothing when the ledger is absent). ONE ACT, ONE TELLING: where a deed carries `feedRef`, the feed seeder skips the feed entry whose eventRef AND tick match the deed's `feedRef` and `tick` (one guard beside `const eventRef = String(entry.sourceEventId || entry.id);`, reading `deedIndex(...).byFeedRef`; host +1) — the tick qualifies a key the landed code writes tick-free [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8 · RN-r1-10: keyed on `sourceEventRef` alone the guard would also have skipped a LATER act's entry sharing the key] — so the act travels once, as the deed's telling — R2's executed probe has `impose_condition` at magnitude 1 `rumorSeedable: true` through the feed, which would otherwise give one act two tellings that `beliefMap.js :: reportsBySubject` counts as two reports. The telling's content additions (`doerRef`; `eventRef: deed:<deedId>`) are persisted rumor-ledger fields with their own lifecycle row (§14); and because `reportsBySubject` reads every telling as a report about its witness towns, deed tellings also refresh beliefs about those towns — that belief-map shift is measured at RN-0's car and disclosed with the flag's same-seed shift (§12). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-10 · RN-r1-30.] A `dm_only` verdict mints no deed (§3.1), so the deed arm never tells a covert fact; a public verdict's own feed news is `notable` at a score below the seed floor (`wizardNews.js :: normalizeEntry` gives `severity * 70` = 35–49 against `RUMOR_NOTABLE_SCORE_FLOOR = 60`), so the feed never tells it today and the deed's telling is its one telling — part of the disclosed lighting shift (§12) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-6: the verdict's door moved, and its telling is declared]. PT-4 adds the deed's PARTS (backing, reason, proof) and their visibility thresholds to the read model; RN reads only the visible version (`ver` and `mag`, §5.1).

**REGISTERED TELLINGS THAT ARE NOT DEEDS** (`TELLING_SOURCES`, §2.4). The same one call inside `advanceRumorLedgers` (it already receives `worldState`: `export function advanceRumorLedgers({ worldState, feedEntries, graph, tick, … })`) also seeds the tellings of a CLOSED registration list — THREE rows: CH's LEAK of a covert hire (CH §9 road 1: `eventRef` `leak:<wnpcId>:<employerId>:<retainer.sinceTick>`, one per HIRE; hop-0 origin the worked-against town); CH's POSTED OPERATIVE'S FINDINGS (CH §8: one telling about its post, `eventRef` `rendezvous:<wnpcId>:<aim.sinceTick>`; hop-0 origin the EMPLOYER'S SEAT — the liaison carries the report home); and THE BODIES' LEAK of a covert tie — an employed gang's hire or a sponsored body's tie (BD §7.1, §10.4: `leak:body:<bodyId>:<courtId>:<sinceTick>`, one per tie, on CH's one covert-hire idiom; its origin and declared content are BD's — CH's `paragon_leak` content widened with `bodyId`/`bodyKind`; a caught courier seeds it at the CATCHING town, BD §10.4) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RU-BD-7]. Each row is a pure function of `(worldState, tick, { digest, season })` — the stage's own digest and the season the kernel hands the rumour stage (`pulseKernel.js`: `advanceRumorLedgers({ … tick: worldState.tick, season: roadSeason, … })`) — returning its seeds `{ eventRef, eventTick, originIds, significance, score, content, relayedTick?, arrivalTick? }`. The arm builds each seed as it builds a deed's — the landed feed seed's own record (`advanceRumorLedgers`' seed: `arrivalTick` the event's tick, `hopCount: 0`, `provenance: { originId: sid, relayIds: [] }`, `completeness01: 1`, `accuracy01: 1`, `relayedTick: null`) — and COPIES the two fields a row DECLARES: a declared `relayedTick` (a finite integer: the CONTINUATION GUARD PRE-STAMPED, so the relay's `if (record.relayedTick != null) continue;` never carries the seed past its origin) and a declared `arrivalTick` (clamped to at least `eventTick`: a telling CARRIED to its origin is IN TRANSIT until then — the landed record "with arrivalTick > current tick is IN TRANSIT (parked in its destination ledger, the arrival-queue idiom): invisible to the read model until it arrives", and "In-transit records (arrivalTick > now) never age"). A row that declares neither is relayed exactly as a feed seed (both leaks). THE FINDINGS ROW declares both: `relayedTick` = the seed's tick, and `arrivalTick` = the seed's tick + the relay's OWN price from the meeting town to the seat — 0 when they are one town (`distanceRead.js :: hopWeeks`: "same settlement ⇒ 0"), else the relay's `travelTicks` line exactly, `Math.max(1, finiteNumber(hopWeeks(digest, meetingId, seatId, season), 1))` (no second speed for news) — so the report reaches the home court ALONE, a carry's weeks after the meeting, and lives there its `notable` thirteen weeks from its arrival. EXECUTED (`cure/D-RN-r4-probe/r4Probe.out.json` A, on the verifier's five-town graph): the cured seed is held by `home` only, visible from tick 11 (a one-week carry), gone at tick 25; the round-3 text's seed (origin the meeting town, the guard unstamped) reached all five towns; a row declaring neither field relays as a feed seed [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RN-r3-verify FAIL ("RN's seed is held by `fourth, home, meet, post, third`, which includes the post that was spied on. CH's seed is held by `home` only") and its ROUTED-UNAPPLIED (a)–(c) (`cure/D-CH-r3.md` §5 RN), under RULINGS-ROUND4 A.9 ("COPIES a row's declared `relayedTick` onto the seed … the findings' origin is the EMPLOYER'S SEAT; the leak `eventRef` gains `:<retainer.sinceTick>`; the seed's `arrivalTick = tick + hopWeeks(meeting, seat, season)`"); the round-3 bracket's "`relayedTick: null` … relays it exactly as a feed seed" holds only for a row that declares no guard]. The arm copies only the structured content fields the row DECLARES (`captureContent`'s law: "ONLY structured fields are copied"; each declared field is a persisted rumor-ledger field whose lifecycle row is its OWNER's — CH's; BD's), and never writes a person, the party or a body into `partyIds`. A registered telling carries no `deed:` eventRef, so RN's heard pass (§5.1) never reads it: it moves no fame, no regard and no credibility; its OWNER's pass reads its arrivals (CH's `paragonBeliefs`; BD's for its row). Its belief-map refresh through `reportsBySubject` is its owner's flag's disclosed shift (CH's `paragonCovertEnabled`; BD's), never RN's. Host: zero further lines — the list is read inside `renown/deedTelling.js`, so CH's ≤ 3-line fallback in `rumorNetwork.js` is not needed (PLAUSIBLE until CH-7's car-0 measures that each row's inputs stand on `worldState` at the rumor stage, the tick after CH's pass wrote them). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-CH-r2 §5.2(d): CH's leak (§9) and findings (§8) named RN's telling arm as their seeding seam, PLAUSIBLE until RN's arm took a registered source; it now takes them, by a closed list.]

**Clock:** mint at the DM's act (immediate); scope frozen. **Receipt:** "Word of the deed at Kell goes out along the river road" (the dossier of each witness town). **Surface/scrutiny/machinery:** the surface says where the tale started and that it travels; scrutiny shows the deed, its witnesses and how far it could go ("as far as the river towns"); the BFS and the hop rings only on intent.

## §4 FAME (RN-0)

### 4.1 The read

`fameOf(worldState, actorRef, tick)` = the widest of (a) the OFFICE scope (§4.2) and (b) each unstruck deed whose `doer` is the actor, its scope decayed: `ordinal = decayTowardNeutral(SCOPE_ORDINAL[projectedScope], 0, ageWeeks, band)`, rounded half-up to a `FAME_SCOPES` index, `band` the deed source's fame band — `'a_decade'`, and `'a_year'` for an `npc_verdict` deed (§3.1) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7's pace]. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-3: band `a_decade` (520 weeks; R2 offered `a_few_years` or `a_decade`) with rounding gives "obscurity returns with time" at a felt pace: a realm-wide deed is known realm-wide about two and a half years, across its region about ten, in its own town about twenty-six (DRAFT, UNMEASURED, FP-46 roster).] **Reads:** the deed ledger (truth of what was done) and office (truth of position); the RESULT is the world's widest knowledge, never a town's belief — the observer read (4.3) is the belief. **Writes:** nothing (derived; memoized per snapshot). **Counterforce:** time, by the one half-life; a struck deed; no deed, no fame beyond office. The actor is a person, a corps or the party (RN1); a CORPS holds no office, so its office scope is `unknown` and its fame is its deeds' alone — its `legend_recorded` at introduction first (CH §2.2b) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]. A NAMED BODY likewise holds no office: its fame is its deeds' alone (the `body_act`, `patrol_clash` and `site_verdict` deeds whose doer it is — its `escaped` from a court's hunt among them, so a failed hunt makes it louder, BD §9.11), on the one `a_decade` band, beginning `unknown` at its naming (L-BD 32b/33b); a paragon who LED it counts the deeds stamped `ledBy` him among his own (§2.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes; THE BODIES reads it for L-BD 38's recruitment and 42 (e)'s end place] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RU-BD-9 · B.5]. A hunt's LEADER counts the hunt's `hunt_won` as his own deed (he is its doer, §3.1) — the person, paragon or company the card named.

### 4.2 Fame by office (the closed `OFFICE_SCOPE` table)

| Office (landed read) | Scope | Why |
|---|---|---|
| the ruler (`rulingPower.js :: governingFactionOf` → its leader, read as `rulerId`) of a polity's root (PL's `rootId`) whose polity (`polityOf(...).members`, the root counted) holds ≥ DRAFT 25% of the world's settlements | `realm` | a great crown is known across the world |
| the ruler of any other polity root or liege (a two-town pair's lord included), or of a `city` / `metropolis` | `regional` | known across its lands — the office's reach is the seat's trade neighbours within the notable hop budget (`tradeNeighbours`, `maxHopsFor` notable = 2), §3.2's walk from the seat |
| the ruler of any other settlement | `settlement` | known at home |
| an `INSTITUTION_ROLES` pillar (`High Priest`, `Watch Captain`, `Magistrate`, `Guildmaster`, `Master of Coin`, `Master Smith`) or a faction `leader_champion` seat | `settlement` | known in their town |
| everyone else | `unknown` | ordinary NPCs "stay locally known, as they should" — known only by deeds |

**PL-dark arm** (`polityEnabled` off — every preset today): no root and no liege is read; a `city` or `metropolis` ruler is `regional`, every other ruler `settlement`. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-13: genesis makes 87–94% of polities two towns (PL §0), so `realm` by office comes only past a share of the world and a pair's lord is never famous everywhere; `regional` office reach is defined.]

The office term is ALSO the landed scalar `embassyEnvoyWeight01({ importanceWeight01, factionPower01 })` (`office01`), which RN exposes for the two consumers that already read it (envoys and spies); office never decays while the office is held, and drops the day it is lost (a new ruler is the one known by the crown).

### 4.3 Fame as belief (one town at a time)

`fameIn(worldState, actorRef, observerId, tick)`: each unstruck deed of the actor is first DECAYED exactly as in §4.1 (its source's band, rounded) and its RING found by `RING_BY_DECAY` (DRAFT): decayed ordinal equal to the projected one → every ring of `reach`; decayed to `regional` below a `realm` deed → rings 0..2 (the rumor road's notable hop budget); `settlement` → ring 0, the witnesses; `unknown` → nowhere. It then counts at the observer when the town HEARD the deed by THE ONE HEARD TEST, `heardAt` — OMNISCIENT and ASPATIAL: the observer lies inside the deed's WHOLE projected reach, at once — the omniscient presets apply `hopDelayTicks` 0, as the landed belief read does under omniscient (`believedReputation`'s receipt: `hopDelayTicks: 0`), and an aspatial world has no digest [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the verifier's carried minor: the round-2 delay term contradicted this paragraph's own "no delay"]; ROAD (the three belief presets): its rumor ledger holds a live telling `deed:<deedId>` (an army, smuggle or migrant carrier counts), or a heard term for that deed stands at its `s:` holder (which outlives a telling evicted from the top 24 or expired), and that version NAMES the actor as its doer (`heardAt(...).doerKey`: a version heard under another doer — PT-4's swap — or under none counts for nobody's fame at that town) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2's doer as heard, carried into fame: a town that heard the tale told of someone else does not know the true doer by it]; a deed stamped `ledBy` counts ALSO for its LEADER at a town whose heard version names the body he led as the doer (`heardAt(...).doerKey` = the body's key, its doer part visible — the leader rides with his body, §2.1), so `fameIn` and `fameOf` count one leader's led deeds alike; a version that names another doer, or none, counts for neither [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RN-r4-verify minor: "`ledBy` deeds count in `fameOf` but not in `fameIn`, because `doerKey` names the body"] — AND, in every arm, once the scope has decayed below its projected band, the observer lies inside the shrunken ring (a road-heard town off the projected reach keeps fame only while the deed is undecayed); a CAPPED deed (`npc_verdict`) is known only inside its capped `reach`, in every arm (§3.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7]. `heardAt` alone, without the ring, is what regard, a body's re-reading and the party's credibility read (§5, §7): a hearing is never undone by the fade [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V1 · RN-r1-11: the omniscient arm's heard test for regard was the decayed ring, so a term vanished when fame shrank (week 137 for a realm deed), not on its own half-life]. It knows an office iff the observer lies inside the office's scope (its own town; the seat's two-hop trade reach for `regional`; the world for `realm`). The observer's fame of the actor = the widest decayed scope it knows. ARMS: omniscient presets (the default `realistic_regional`, `quiet_local`, `static_campaign`, `narrative_campaign`) — known at once inside the ring, no delay, the full story (R2 R-15; R2 Q3 "yes": the party has fame in the default preset); the belief presets — the road's own arrival; aspatial — no delay (no digest). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-4: the observer read decays and its ring shrinks, so "Few outside Kell remember the siege now" fires from it · RN-r1-11: the road arms read what was heard, so the dossier's "Known here" and the Why panel cannot disagree.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-11: that last claim is withdrawn — "Known here" (fame) and the Why panel's reason for a regard (the heard term) may part once a deed has decayed; both are said in words (RN3).] This is the roamer precedent (`believedReputation`) generalized from one origin to a list of deeds, which fixes its single-origin key (R1 RF-16, H15). THE ACTOR'S OWN RING (`deedRingAt`, §2.3) is this read's omniscient-arm form taken in every arm by the actor about ITSELF — the ring test with no heard test, for an actor deciding on its own notoriety (THE BODIES' gang, BD §6.8 (a), (e)) — one ring rule, two readers [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — BD's route].

### 4.4 The notoriety projection (RN4)

| fame at the observer | believed moral read ≤ `shameful` — the holder-town's net regard ≤ `mistrust`, OR the dominant known deed's FACE (its inherent-value band, §6.1, or the DM's mark where the town can see it) is `shameful`/`villainous` while the town's net regard is below `fondness` | otherwise |
|---|---|---|
| `unknown` | `unknown` | `unknown` |
| `settlement` | `whispered` | `whispered` |
| `regional` | `notorious` | `known` |
| `realm` | `infamous` | `known` |

Under `renownEnabled` (ONE flag; persons need `npcConsequencesEnabled`), every OBSERVER read of `reputation.notorietyBand` — `npcCirculation.js`, `npcCirculationTable.js :: rejectionFor`, `npcCirculationBelief.js :: believedReputation`, and the display reader `src/components/map/heraldWanderers.js` through `npcLedgerProjection.js` — reads it through `notorietyProjection` for that observer. THE STAMP STAYS: the verdict still writes `notorietyBand` (truth); `reputationRaceConsumer.js` reads the stamp DIRECTLY (`told: NOTORIETY_BANDS.indexOf(rec.reputation.notorietyBand) > 0`) and is unchanged — a third read site, truth by its own design, named so no car scatters into it. ONE FADE: for a person with at least one RN deed, `believedReputation` returns the projection's band and does NOT subtract `beliefFadeSteps` (RN's decay and ring already priced time and distance); for a person stamped with no deed it runs the landed fade unchanged; the `unreliable` infoMode's one-step swing (no shipped preset uses that mode) applies once to whichever result. `scandalClass`, `alignmentRead`, `competenceRead` and `edictMark` stay the ledger's (they are not fame). Dark ⇒ the landed facet and the landed fade, byte-identical; dark-after-lit ⇒ the same (the stamp was never dropped). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-6 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-6): one flag, the stamp kept, the direct reader listed · RN-r1-5: one fade per person · RN-r1-24: the Wanderers path.] The dominant known deed is the one that sets the observer's fame (the widest decayed scope; the newest on a tie). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V3: the verdict deed now has moral rows (§6.1, by the stamp's scandal class), and the projection's "how badly" reads the deed's FACE wherever the town feels nothing warm — so `notorious`/`infamous` fire for an exile sentenced while RN is lit, at a balanced town too (whose lens reads a cruelty at 0), and `npcCirculationTable.js :: rejectionFor`'s `loudness >= notorietyRank('infamous')` bar stays live; a malicious town that admires the brute reads him `known` (EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r2-probe/r2Probe.out.json` d).] **THE STAMP IS THE CEILING** [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V7]: where the dominant known deed is the person's `npc_verdict` deed, the projection never reads louder than the stamp — `min(notorietyRank(projected), notorietyRank(stamp))` — so a `known` jailing reads `known` wherever it is known (never `notorious`), a banishment stamped `notorious` never reads `infamous`, and `rejectionFor`'s `infamous` refusal fires from a verdict only for a person the landed table stamped `infamous` (a repeat banishment); the projection may read QUIETER than the stamp (a warm town's `known`; a faded deed's `whispered`). EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r3-probe/r3Probe.out.json` B4: `known` → `regional` → a bad face's `notorious` → `known`; `notorious` → `realm` → `infamous` → `notorious`; `infamous` → `realm` → `infamous`. The round-2 bracket's "so `notorious`/`infamous` fire for an exile sentenced while RN is lit" holds UP TO the stamp. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (L-BD 38: "a body's NOTORIETY feeds its recruitment … feared gangs grow"; 42 (e): the founding end's place by "the gang's NOTORIETY as the towns there believe it … RN's `g:` fame projection with its reach and fade — one reputation truth"): the same table is a NAMED BODY's notoriety at a town — `notorietyProjection(fameIn(body, town), the town's moral read of its key)` (the town's net regard of `g:<bodyId>` or the creed, and the dominant known deed's face) — read by THE BODIES, pure; a body carries no stamp, so no ceiling binds it and nothing is written on its record.]

### 4.5 The operative's notoriety (RN4)

Under `renownLeansEnabled` (a lean, §12), `espionageMath.js :: operativeNotoriety01` returns `max(office01, FAME01[fameIn(…, the operative, the target court)])` with `FAME01 = { unknown: 0, settlement: 0.33, regional: 0.66, realm: 1 }` (DRAFT) — a spy whose deeds reached the court he spies on is easier to catch there; the office floor is the landed number exactly. THE SIGNATURE CAR (RN-4; three files): `operativeNotoriety01(homeSettlement, npc, { worldState, targetId, durableId } = {})` and `covertCompetence01(homeSettlement, npc, opts = {})` gain one optional context argument with an IDENTITY default (absent ⇒ the office scalar, byte-identical); `espionageGauntlet.js` (309) passes `{ worldState, targetId, durableId }` where it already holds the target (`competence01: covertCompetence01(homeItem, npc)`), the operative's durable id looked up from the roster identity the gauntlet holds (`npcLedger.js :: durableIdForRoster(worldState, homeId, rosterIdentity)`; none while ungraduated) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's detail on RN-r1-7: `gauntletCatchFactors` receives `npc`, not the cast entry's `durableId`]; `espionageMissions.js` (132) keeps the office-only call in the cast, where no target exists yet; `espionageMath.js` (163) ≈ +4. A spy with no `wnpc_` (the cast's `durableId: entry.durableId || ''` is optional) has office fame only. ES is dark everywhere: a lit-mutant pin, no shipped shift. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-7: the function took no `worldState` and no target court, so `fameIn` could not be computed inside it.]

### 4.6 The ransom projection (the unwired leaf's producer)

`ransomRegardBand(worldState, homeSettlementId, personKey, tick)` = the home COURT's derived regard of its own captive (§5.3), floored at `valued` for an office holder (a court values its officers by office), projected: `forgotten` ← `hatred · enmity · mistrust`; `valued` ← `indifference · fondness`; `beloved` ← `esteem · adoration`. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-4: R2 drafted `indifference → forgotten`; the leaf itself calls `valued` "ordinary regard" (`homeRansomAnswer`: "Strained means and ordinary regard: the price decides"), so a court that feels nothing in particular for its own man still values him; one idiom, a mapping table, per the brief's preferred arm.] The ransom leaf's `normalizeHomeCourt` row takes this band from RN when the ransom road that wires it lands (WR-7d's persistence is an OWNER gate; the roads hostage's own home pays over the term today) — RN is the producer, the consumer's wiring is the ransom road's unit.

**Clock:** derived per read; decay `a_decade` (`a_year` for an `npc_verdict` deed, §3.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RN-r3-verify minor: "§4.6's clock still says `a_decade`"]. **First-visible:** the week of the deed (the witness towns' dossiers). **Receipt / felt sentence:** "Known across the river towns as the ones who broke the siege at Kell." **Surface / scrutiny / machinery:** the surface says where a person is known and for what, in words; scrutiny lists the deeds behind it and where each reached; the ordinals, the half-life and `office01` on intent.

## §5 REGARD (RN-1, RN-2)

### 5.1 What a holder heard — the one writer and the derived arm

**Reads:** BELIEF — the version of a deed that reached the holder's town (the rumor ledger's record for `deed:<deedId>` at that settlement, its visible parts by the read model's completeness thresholds), or, in the omniscient arm, the deed itself inside its WHOLE projected reach (`heardAt`, §4.3 — never the decayed ring [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V1]). A person holder hears what the town it STANDS IN has heard (the npcLedger `residency`/`placed` host; R1 RF-6). A CORPS holder (`u:`) hears what the town its unit stands at has heard — the node its free unit's position names (WC §1.7.2's army-style stepper, read as `armyTransit.js :: currentRegion`: "the node at its position along the path (rounded to the nearest hop)"; PLAUSIBLE until WC-10 lands, named at CH-10's car) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]. A NAMED GANG holder (`g:`) hears what its HOME has heard — `homeId` (BD §2.2: "its tie town"), else the anchor town of its base site (`baseSiteId`), else the settlement of the country it stands in (`where.ids[0]`): "the deeds heard at its home" (BD §2.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes]. **Writes:** `spatialLedgers.regard` only, through `regardLedger.js :: writeHeardTerm({ worldState, holderKey, subjectKey, deedId, ver, mag, reading, tick })` — the ONE writer; it replaces the holder's term for that deed (or for the deed it corrects) and never adds a second (RN6). **The pass:** `renown/regardHeard.js :: advanceHeardRegard({ worldState, tick })` scans each settlement's rumor ledger for `deed:` tellings whose VERSION — THE TRIPLE (`ver`, `mag`, the heard doer) — differs from the holder's stored term (an arrival; a better telling superseding — `mergeArrival` keeps the earliest `arrivalTick`, so "arrived this tick" alone would miss a better version, and `pickBetterTelling` supersedes on CONTINUOUS completeness first (`if (a.completeness01 !== b.completeness01) return a.completeness01 > b.completeness01 ? a : b;`), so a better telling may change the magnitude or the doer inside one parts mask; or a correction, compared as the version of the original it yields, §3.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2, EXECUTED against the landed `mergeArrival` (`cure/D-RN-r3-probe/r3Probe.out.json` A1): a stored telling at completeness 0.765 and band 2 merged with one at 0.82 and band 3 keeps the parts mask (35 = 35) and takes band 3 — the round-2 `ver`-only key re-reads nothing, the triple re-reads it], reads each through §6's lenses for the town (`s:`), for the graduated persons standing there (`p:`), for a marked company standing there (`u:`) and for a named gang whose home it is (`g:`), and writes the term — about the doer AS HEARD (the telling's `doerRef`, PT §11.1's typed swap; a wrong doer is a belief a correction replaces), with the magnitude band AS HEARD (`mag`) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V2]. A CHANGED HEARD DOER MOVES THE TERM: the writer removes the holder's term for that deed from the subject it was filed under (the holder's term carrying that `deedId` under any subject but the deed's backer key — the backer's half term, §6.2, is its own) and writes it under the new doer, so the holder keeps ONE doer term per deed (RN6); the LEADER's half term (`p:<ledBy>`, §2.1) FOLLOWS the heard doer — written while the version names the body he led, removed with the doer term when the heard doer changes to anyone else, and, where the new heard doer IS the leader himself, replaced by his one doer term (ONE term per deed per subject, never a half beside a whole) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RN-r4-verify minor: "§5.1's term move exempts only the backer key, so a doer swap drops the leader's half term or collides with it"] and the old subject's entry drops when empty (a `voiced_regard` deed's term is about its TARGET, read through the speaker, §5.5: a changed speaker re-reads that term in place and moves nothing); a version that names NO doer (its doer part below VAGUE 0.3) writes no doer term — the town knows an act was done, not by whom — and since a better telling never lowers completeness (`pickBetterTelling`), a doer once heard is never un-heard [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2 ("a changed doer moves the deed's one term to the new subject (PT-4's swap)"), EXECUTED (`cure/D-RN-r3-probe/r3Probe.out.json` A2): a swap to `p:wnpc_vey` inside one mask — the `ver`-only key misses it, the triple moves the one term, and the next tick re-reads nothing]. A version whose heard doer is a SETTLEMENT (PT-4's swap draws its `doerRef` from real actors, a town among them) writes NO doer term at the town's `s:` holder and none that its bodies re-read — a town's or a court's feeling about a court is a refused pair (§2.2), so the town keeps the tale and no feeling — while the persons, companies and named gangs standing there file theirs under `c:<id>` as for any settlement doer (§2.1 `doer`); the term filed under the old doer is removed all the same (the move's first half) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RN-r3-verify minor: "A doer swapped to a settlement hits the refused pair at an `s:` holder, and the text does not say so"]. The town's `s:` term is ALSO the town's record of the VERSION HEARD that its bodies re-read (§5.3): the pass writes it even when the commons' own reading is nil (a zero-severity term) whenever the town's court re-reads that version above the prune floor, and eviction weighs the larger of the two readings. A party CREDIBILITY CHARGE heard (a `party_pledge` deed, or a correction marked `contradicts` about a party deed) is always written as a heard record at the town's `s:` holder, whatever its reading (filed under its heard doer: a charge whose version names another doer charges the party nothing at that town) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2], and is neither pruned nor evicted until the credibility law spends it (`MAX_LOOKBACK_TICKS: 260`), so `partyCredibilityAt` outlives the telling's expiry [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(a) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.1)]. A term whose deed is struck is never the one the term cap evicts (the cap evicts the weakest UNMASKED term) and decays as it would have, so unstrike restores it exactly [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor on RN-r1-15]. A telling of a CAPPED deed (an `npc_verdict` deed, §3.1) at a town outside its capped `reach` writes nothing — the cap binds every arm, and `heardAt` reads the deed unheard there whatever carrier brought the telling [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RN-r3-verify minor: "§5.1's writer scans every `deed:` telling with no cap filter"]. Bounded: ≤ 24 tellings per settlement (`RUMOR_TOP_K`), ≤ 8 terms per entry, ≤ DRAFT 12 emergent subjects per holder (the DM's `declared`/`dmSet` exempt). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-2 · RN-r1-16.]

**RN-S1 — THE HOST — RULED** (`briefs/RULINGS-AFTER-COMPILE.md` RN-S1; fold §3.1.22, OD-FC-58). The kernel persists only `rumors.next` into `rumorLedgers` (`pulseKernel.js`: `memoryState = setSpatialLedger(memoryState, 'rumorLedgers', rumors.next);`), so the pass cannot live inside the rumor stage. THE ONE PERSON STAGE: a single pre-gate leaf pass at the head of `envoyPulse.js :: advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse`, beside the ENC-3 drop pass that "RUNS ABOVE THIS FUNCTION'S OWN EARLY RETURN" — after rumors and beliefs, every tick in every preset, its world returned to the kernel unconditionally (`memoryState = envoys.worldState;`); `envoyPulse.js` 332 → ≈ 336; zero kernel lines. THE MOUNT THREADS THREE BINDINGS (measured at `81f1cd963`): the early return hands back its INPUTS (`if (!envoyDiplomacyActive(worldState)) { return { worldState: meetingLedgers.changed ? meetingLedgers.worldState : worldState, regionalGraph, wizardNews, settlementUpdates, …`), the full path re-reads the ORIGINAL parameters (`let feed = wizardNews;` · `let updates = settlementUpdates;`), and the kernel keeps all three (`memoryState = envoys.worldState;` · `settlementUpdates = envoys.settlementUpdates;` · `wizardNews = envoys.wizardNews;`) — EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r2b-probe/earlyReturn.out`: with the envoy gate shut the function hands back the very `worldState`, `wizardNews` and `settlementUpdates` it was given. The stage therefore REBINDS the three parameters `worldState`, `wizardNews` and `settlementUpdates` from its result before ENC-3's drop pass and the early return, so both returns and the full path's `snapshotWithUpdates(snapshot, settlementUpdates, state, regionalGraph)` read the rebound values with no further edit (no `no-param-reassign` rule in `eslint.config.js`); CH's court hits (CH §9, written into `settlementUpdates`) and any Herald line a registered pass appends survive a world whose envoy gate is shut. ≈ 4 host lines: one call and one rebinding [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-CH-r2 §5.2(a) · CH-r1-30: RN named no `settlementUpdates` threading, so CH's court hits would have been lost on the early return]. It runs, in order, RN's pass (the verdict deeds, §3.1; the heard terms; the voices, and the party crossings it hands on to PT) → CH's weekly pass → PT's engagement pass [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-6 · SEAMS-r1-6]; RN OWNS the mount (RN-1's car-0 measures it once for all three), and CH and PT REGISTER their passes on a closed registration list, pinned. The deed SEEDING stays in `rumorNetwork.js` (§3.4); only the heard pass rides the stage. The FALLBACK stays named: no persisted heard terms anywhere — the omniscient arm's derivation in the omniscient presets, and in the belief presets every holder, bodies included, reads only the LIVE tellings at its town (the version visible) and forgets a deed when its telling expires (26/13 weeks), so no body ever reads the deed ledger directly [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor on RN-r1-2: the round-1 fallback derived a body's regard from the ledger in a belief preset, a Law One residue]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RULINGS-AFTER-COMPILE RN-S1 executed: the STOP is closed; its measurement stands in `cure/L-RN.md` M1–M2.]

[ADDED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c; OP §3.1 · RULINGS-P6 §C RN · OP §15 RN row) — THE ONE PERSON STAGE'S REGISTRATION LIST GAINS ONE PINNED ENTRY: THE OPPORTUNITY's `src/domain/opportunity/opportunityStage.js :: advanceOpportunities({ worldState, snapshot, settlementUpdates, wizardNews, tick })`, placed AFTER CH's weekly pass (a paragon's aim is chosen before OP reads it) and BEFORE PT's engagement pass (nothing OP does reaches the party) — the order RN's pass → CH's pass → OP's stage → PT's pass; zero host lines of OP's own (RN owns the mount, `envoyPulse.js` 332 eff); runs every tick in every preset, does nothing unless `roadOpportunityEnabled === true`, returns its world unchanged when dark, and reads errand positions by the PURE schedule (`scheduledEnvoyPosition(errand, tick)`), never the envoy mover's later writes. NO CAPTURE TELLING SOURCE is added (OP's question "whether a capture TOLD on the rumor road is a telling source" is RULED NOT ACCEPTED — nothing; the captive's home learns of a hold only by the roads ransom asked or WR-7d's arrived demand, RULINGS-P6 §D.1).]

READ INSTEAD (§J.2): NO NEW deed or telling SOURCE KIND is minted in RN for a capture — no capture deed kind in `DEED_SOURCES`, no capture row on `TELLING_SOURCES` (the closed list stays CH's leak, CH's posted operative's findings and THE BODIES' leak, §3.4); a capture told on the rumor road reaches the captive's home through RN's EXISTING telling idiom — a telling the road already carries (a deed's telling, §3.4, or a registered non-deed telling), heard at the home by THE ONE HEARD TEST `heardAt` (§4.3) — and that arrival is a lawful road for the home call (OP §9's `toldBy.road: 'telling'`; CH §14's `captive_held` row). **The derived arm** (every holder in the omniscient and aspatial arms — NEVER a body in a belief preset, §5.3): `regardOf` sums, for each unstruck deed the holder has heard (`heardAt` from the holder's town — the deed's WHOLE projected reach, never the decayed ring [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V1]), the lenses' reading decayed on `a_few_years` from the hearing tick, keeping only the strongest 8 terms per subject — the same terms and the same term cap as the stored arm, so the two arms agree wherever the road neither delays nor weathers. The 12-subject cap is a STORAGE bound of the belief presets only: in the derived arm a holder may feel about more than twelve subjects — the one stated divergence. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-2 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-2): the derived arm is the omniscient arm only · RN-r1-16: the term cap holds in both arms and the subject cap's divergence is stated.] **The band:** `net = warmth − grudge` on `[−1, 1]` → `REGARD_BANDS` by DRAFT edges (±0.1 indifference; ±0.35; ±0.65), unless `dmSet` (wins) or `declared` (the baseline every term decays toward — any `REGARD_BANDS` word; the DM's list's `ally` ⇒ `fondness`, `enemy` ⇒ `enmity`; a band baseline enters `net` at its band's MIDPOINT, DRAFT: `hatred` −0.825 · `enmity` −0.5 · `mistrust` −0.225 · `indifference` 0 · `fondness` +0.225 · `esteem` +0.5 · `adoration` +0.825 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor on SEAMS-r1-9]). **The reason:** the strongest live term's `cause` and deed, in the world's words.

### 5.2 Each act from its office (`ACT_OFFICE`, closed)

| Act family (the owning program's hearing kinds) | Office that makes it | Landed read |
|---|---|---|
| summons, audience, invitation, recognition, offer of employment, a sanction voided or repudiated | the RULER | `rulerId` (the governing faction's leader) |
| arrest, warrant, refusal at the gate, a ban enforced | the WATCH | `INSTITUTION_ROLES.law_enforcement[0]` (`Watch Captain`) |
| judgment, a ban proclaimed, a bounty posted | the BENCH | `INSTITUTION_ROLES.civic[0]` (`Magistrate`) |
| a creed's welcome, condemnation or anathema | the TEMPLE | `INSTITUTION_ROLES.religious[0]` (`High Priest`) of that creed's institution |
| a price, a contract, a purse | the PURSE | `INSTITUTION_ROLES.finance[0]` (`Master of Coin`) or `trade[0]` (`Guildmaster`) |
| a quiet watch, a spy set on someone | no office — the espionage operative cast (importance-inverse, `espionageMissions.js`) | ES's own cast; RN supplies the cast person's regard |

`actingOfficer(worldState, settlementId, act)` returns the holder of that office (absent ⇒ the ruler acts) and their regard band of the subject; the owning program's card names the officer and the reason ("Kell's Watch Captain, who has not forgotten the riot, has posted men at the gate"). RN graduates these officers and the ruler on their first regard (`graduateNpc` with `hostSettlementId` = their own seat and `verdictCause: 'in_office'`, a member ADDED to W-H1's closed `VERDICT_CAUSES`; it "writes nothing to npcs[]") — at most six persons per court. The projection (`npcLedgerProjection.js :: projectNpcPool`, 113 effective) drops `in_office` placements from the pool it hands the Wanderers register (`heraldWanderers.js :: wandererRows`: `settled: pool.placed.map((p) => rowOf(p, nameFor, false))`), so a court officer who never wandered is never listed as a settled wanderer; `heraldWanderers.js :: CAUSE_LINE` ("Total over VERDICT_CAUSES") gains one `in_office` line for its totality pin, never shown (the row is filtered) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor on RN-r1-17]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-17: `placed` is the register's SETTLED half ("the people the pool has already placed"); an explicit cause, not an inference, because other `graduateNpc` callers (`characterDrift.js`, `npcCirculation.js`, `envoyDiplomacy.js`) also place persons.] [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-11: graduation is bounded to the `ACT_OFFICE` holders and the ruler, so "court NPCs carry their own regard" never graduates whole rosters.] **AN `in_office` GRADUATION IS PROVISIONAL.** Graduation is one-way (`npcLedger.js :: graduateNpc`: "An already-graduated person keeps their id and their record exactly as it stands; graduation is not an update verb"), so a W-H2 verdict on an officer RN graduated would leave the record `in_office`, placed at home, with no notoriety stamp — the exile never read as exiled at any gate. RN-2's car in `npcVerdictApply.js :: applyNpcVerdict` (≈ +4) therefore RE-STAMPS an existing `in_office` record through the ledger's own writer — `moveNpcRecord({ worldState, wnpcId, hostSettlementId: holding ? settlementId : null, patch: { verdictCause: decision.verdict, reputation: decision.reputation, dmTruth }, sinceTick: tick })` — so the verdict's cause, stamp and placement land exactly as on a fresh graduation; every other graduated person keeps the landed one-way law (a paragon sentenced by a W-H2 verdict is CH's to rule — routed to the chair). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-RN-r2-N1 (found by this lane; CONFIRMED by reading `graduateNpc` and `moveNpcRecord`): round 1's bounded graduation of officers (RN-r1-17) pre-empted the verdict's own graduation.]

### 5.3 A body's derived regard (`courtRegardOf`; RN-2)

`courtRegardOf(c:<sid>, subject)` = the authored baseline for the body (`dmSet` ⊳ `declared`) ⊕ `Σ_m w_m · net(regardOf(p:m, subject)) / Σ_m w_m` over the ruler and the `ACT_OFFICE` holders ⊕ the INSTITUTIONAL RECORD (each unstruck deed whose `target` is this settlement or its court, read through the ruler's lens at full weight — "what was done to the court as a body"). Weights (DRAFT, UNMEASURED): the ruler `1 + factionPowerShare01(governing faction)`; an officer the landed importance influence (pillar 0.85, key 0.60) × `(1 + its faction's share01)` when its faction governs; the ladder standing multiplies where `npcLadderEnabled` is lit (`spatialLedgers.npcLadder`). A faction body uses its seats (`FACTION_SEAT_ROLES`); a creed's faithful use that creed's temple officers in each town where the creed stands, weighted by the creed's standing (`cult · established · ascendant`). **What a body has heard.** OMNISCIENT and ASPATIAL arms: the deeds it heard by `heardAt` (the whole projected reach; §4.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V1]. BELIEF presets: ONLY the versions its town heard — the `deedId`, `ver` (the visible-parts mask), `mag` and subject (the doer as heard) of each term at the town's `s:` holder, written by the person stage at arrival and re-written whenever that triple changes (§5.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2]; the body re-reads each through its ruler's character with exactly that version (a reason its commons never heard is a reason the court never heard) and never reads the deed ledger directly. Once persons light, the members' own persisted terms (each hears what the town it stands in heard) replace the ruler-only re-read. THE PIN: a court never knows a part its commons did not hear. **Dark person arm** (`npcConsequencesEnabled` off, every preset today): members contribute through the RULER's character over what the body heard (no person key), so courts still feel; no officer differs from the court until persons light. A FACTION body's dark arm is its town's commons' regard plus its authored baseline and institutional record (its people are the town's people); a CREED's faithful read, in the dark arm, the commons' regard of each town where the creed stands, weighted by standing (DRAFT `cult` 0.5 · `established` 1 · `ascendant` 1.5), so "religions … reputation awareness" lives in every preset. **The kingdom** (PL lit): `courtRegardOf(c:<rootId>)` is the crown's regard; a member's court adds `share01 × net(the crown's regard)` to its own derivation, where `share01 = crownReachAt(worldState, snapshot, rootId, memberId, memberId).share01` — PL's ONE crown-reach read (PL §2, PL5, P37): the liege share `POLITY_LIEGE_SHARE[band]` for a DIRECT vassal (0.10–0.45 by the band of the owner's scalar, never 0), that share × `POLITY_PARAMOUNT_REACH[hold]` for a sub-vassal (0 under a `frayed` hold), the hold read as the member believes it (observer = the member); RN keeps no reach map; `DECLARE_STANCE` on a kingdom writes the root court's `declared` baseline, and members feel it at that share. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-12 (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.8): the round-1 hold band map is PL's reach to SUB-vassals only; a direct vassal's share is its liege share, and PT §10.1 reads the same quantity.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-2 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-2) · RN-r1-28 · RN-r1-12.] **Counterforce:** succession replaces the ruler's term at once (a new ruler brings new attitudes); members' terms decay; the institutional record decays like any term. **Never stored:** there is no court opinion field anywhere. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-8: the brief's court derivation is generalized to every BODY (a faction through its seats, a creed's faithful through its temple officers weighted by standing), so "religions" and "courts at large" in the owner's party message share one read and the deity doctrine holds (the faithful feel; the god does not).]

| Role | What the owner said | Seam (`path :: symbol`; host eff. lines) | Lean | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| spy | "a notorious spy is easier to expose" | `espionageMath.js :: operativeNotoriety01` (163) | §4.5 — fame at the target court replaces the office-only scalar, floor identical | buildable; ES dark (C1) |

[CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-21 (WC-CH-81, RN's half; RULINGS-P5 C.21: "ONE owned-unit row for the shared lean composers (RN owns; TRUTH)"; `cure/D-FOLD-p5.md` route 7): THE RENOWN OWNS BOTH LEAVES as ONE OWNED UNIT (the fold §3.1.24 ↳ P5; §3.3.16 ↳ P5 (13)), cut with its one manifest row IMMEDIATELY BEFORE the first wave in the order of the day that consumes either (RN-4 or CH-3) — never "the first to land" (CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)); it reads TRUTH, named once: it composes the leans' bounded multipliers on a term its host then resolves (the physics side of 18 (d)), each lean's own read staying its owner's (RN's priest lean reads the commons' regard, §5.4's row); the FIXED ORDER (RN's first, then CH's) and the two JOINT CAPS above stand; CH registers its terms there and nowhere else, and any later lean on either term registers in the same leaf.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-21 (RN's half).]

### 5.5 Voices that carry (opinion leaders; depth one)

A holder whose fame is `regional` or wider and whose regard of a subject crosses into `enmity`/`hatred` or `esteem`/`adoration` mints ONE `voiced_regard` deed (`praise` or `condemnation`; doer the holder, target the subject), at most once per (holder, subject) per year (DRAFT; the cooldown reads the holder's last `voiced_regard` deed about that subject through `deedIndex(...).byDoer` — no field on the entry, RN-r1-21). Its scope is the holder's own (`maxHopsFor` +1 hop when the holder is realm-famous — the bounded multiplier on the rumor's reach, DRAFT). A hearer reads a voiced deed through the SPEAKER: `sign × net(hearer's regard of the speaker) × DRAFT 0.5` toward the subject (a hearer who esteems the High Priest takes part of her contempt for the party; one who hates her leans the other way). **No cascade by construction:** a term whose cause is `praised` or `condemned` never triggers a crossing that mints another voice. **The condemner's cost (§8 of the party brief):** a `condemnation` of a deed whose inherent value is `admirable` or `heroic`, voiced by a holder the deed did not harm (its own lens two ≥ 0), writes one `condemned_a_good_deed` term (sev DRAFT 0.1) from the condemner's own town toward the condemner, unless the town's moral band is `malicious` (`natureWordFor`, §6.2 — its people share the malice) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7: the town reads its band] — regard only, never legitimacy. [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R-RN-6: "spreads further" is built as a depth-one opinion deed on the one road, so the bound is structural and the condemnation the owner said is "difficult … without reason" has a carrier.] The engine-minted voice rides THE ONE PERSON STAGE (RULED); under the fallback the DM records a voice as a deed. **A VOICE NAMING THE PARTY — RULED** (RULINGS-ROUND1 ADDENDUM SEAMS-r1-6; RULINGS-ROUND2 A.5). A renowned holder's public praise or condemnation of the party is an act of the world toward the party, so THE HEARING COMES FIRST: RN's crossing (the holder's regard of the party into `enmity`/`hatred` or `esteem`/`adoration`, the holder's fame `regional` or wider, the year's cooldown clear) mints nothing itself — the stage hands it to PT's engagement pass, which opens `party_voiced` (CLIMAX; verdicts `spoken · withheld`; the default `spoken`, which decides nothing FOR the party; the DM may strike or amend in its week; a long advance runs the default) — and the `voiced_regard` deed is minted only when that hearing resolves `spoken` (doer the voice, target the party, through RN's writer; `recordKey` the engagement id); at `withheld` nothing mints and PT's `strikes` quiet that voice for the year (PT §7.3). RN owns no kind. Voices about anyone else stay regard dynamics, minted at the crossing (they are no act upon the party). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-6: the round-1 STOP's proposed default "unspoken" and "a crossing whose subject is the party mints nothing" are superseded by the ruled `spoken`.] **A COURT'S VOICE — RULED** (RULINGS-ROUND3 A.11: "a COURT (any), or a PERSON or CORPS of `regional` fame or wider — a court is heard by its standing, a person by fame"). A court's derived regard of the party (`courtRegardOf(c:<sid>, party)`, §5.3) crossing into `enmity`/`hatred` or `esteem`/`adoration` is a crossing whatever the court's size (a court holds no fame, RN1), handed to PT's `party_voiced` exactly as a person's — the hearing first, the deed only at `spoken`, the year's cooldown read from the deed ledger; the voice is MADE by the court's RULER (`ACT_OFFICE`'s RULER row: a court's public word; PT §7.3's officer) and needs no person lit. Its `voiced_regard` deed's doer is the court (`{kind:'settlement', id}`), so it is a TALE (RN1): it adds no fame to anyone and moves no town's or court's RN regard OF the court. ITS HEARERS ARE THE TALE'S (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.10: "its hearer set is the towns and persons the tale reaches (the hop budget by the court's tier, the walk), never 'the ruler's office scope'"): its `reach` is §3.2's walk from the court's town over the landed carriers (`tradeNeighbours`; sea and teleport where a digest exists) to the rumor road's OWN hop budget (`rumorNetwork.js :: maxHopsFor`: "`if (record && record.significance === 'major') { return Number(record.score) >= 95 ? 5 : 4; } return 2;`") taken BY THE COURT'S TIER (`settlement.tier`, `TIER_ORDER = thorp · hamlet · village · town · city · metropolis`): a thorp's, hamlet's, village's or town's court `notable` (two hops — "a hamlet's court voice reaches its neighbours"), a city's or metropolis' `major` (four), a great crown's (§4.2's `realm` office row) `major` at the realm score (five) — DRAFT mapping, UNMEASURED, FP-46 — and that tier fixes the deed's significance and score (never §3.2's magnitude rule, as the verdict deed's cap fixes its own, §3.1), so the rumor road carries its telling the same hops in the belief presets and the omniscient and aspatial arms hear it inside the walk's rings at once, with zero delay (`heardAt`, §4.3). Its hearers: every town in rings ≥ 1, and every person, company or named gang standing inside the reach with its own regard of the court; the court's own commons and members take no term (A.15, below). NO HEARER, NO CARD: where the walk reaches no settlement beyond the court's own (no confirmed trade channel, no sea or teleport neighbour), the crossing opens no `party_voiced` — a voice no hearer could take moves nothing and decides nothing — and the crossing is receipted on the court's own page only (scrutiny; never pushed) [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the verifier's second cure ("or open no hearing when the reach holds no eligible hearer") kept beside B.10's reach, so an isolated court mints no empty card; vetoable]. EXECUTED (`cure/D-RN-r5-probe/r5Probe.out.json` B, on master's `tradeNeighbours` and `maxHopsFor`): a town's court reaches its three trade neighbours and a fourth town at hop 2, a hamlet's its one neighbour and two towns beyond, a city's four rings, an isolated hamlet's none — where round 4's "office scope" (a small town's `settlement`, ring 0) reached only its own commons, who take no term [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RN-X2 (MAJOR: "Below city tier, a court's voice has NO hearer in the omniscient and aspatial arms … `party_voiced` opens, resolves `spoken` and moves nothing") under RULINGS-ROUND5 B.10; round 4's "Its scope is its ruler's OFFICE scope (§4.2 — … a small town's at home), walked by §3.2 from the court's town" is struck]. Its effect is on hearers' regard OF THE PARTY, through the tale (the version heard), the court's CREDIBILITY and the HEARER'S SIDE OF THE COURT (RULINGS-ROUND4 A.15): `clamp(sign × credibilityWeight(credibilityScoreOf(worldState, sid, tick)) × DRAFT 0.5 × relationFactor, −0.4, +0.4)`. The credibility is the landed court stock, read and never written (`informationStatecraft.js :: credibilityWeight`: "NEUTRAL (0) ⇒ EXACTLY 1.0", `WEIGHT_FLOOR: 0.35` a proven liar, `WEIGHT_CEIL: 1.15` a trusted court; the stock is lit in no preset, so every court's word weighs 1.0 today — the belief layer's per-source weight, `makeCredibilityWeightFn`, weighs `party_slander`'s planted telling the same way). The cap is the per-deed cap 0.4 (§6.2's one term — it binds the person voice above too). `relationFactor` is the hearer's side of the court on the landed banding of a pair label into three words (`envoyChanceMeetingStage.js :: postureBandOf`: `if (['allied', 'patron', 'client', 'vassal', 'trade_partner'].includes(word)) return 'friendly'; if (['hostile', 'cold_war', 'rival'].includes(word)) return 'hostile'; return 'neutral';` — `envoyChanceMeeting.js :: POSTURE_BANDS`' words; RN-4's car adds the `export` keyword, zero lines): `friendly` +1, `neutral` 0 (no pair state reads `neutral`), `hostile` −1 — "one who hates the court leans the other way", the person voice's shape. WHOSE label: a TOWN hearer (`s:`) reads its landed pair state with the voicing court's town AS IT BELIEVES IT — `pairNet(...).label`, lens two's one read, through `readBeliefRelationship` (§6.2; RULINGS-ROUND4 §E: a hearer's reaction DECIDES for it, so it reads belief; the omniscient and aspatial arms read the truth by the landed identity), never an RN regard (a town's or a court's feeling about a court is a refused pair, §2.2); a PERSON, a COMPANY or a NAMED GANG hearer (`p:`, `u:`, `g:`) reads its own RN regard of `c:<sid>` on the same three sides (`fondness` or warmer +1 · `indifference` 0 · `mistrust` or colder −1) — belief by construction — else lens two's fallback pair state through the same belief read (a person's residency town's, a company's retaining court's, else its unit's town's — §6.2; a gang has none: 0) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — OD-RN-48 RULED (RULINGS-ROUND4 §E; the verifier's FAIL A.8: "§5.5's town factor reads `pairNet(...).label`" as truth)]. THE VOICE IS ITS OWN PEOPLE'S: the voicing court's own commons (`s:<sid>`) take NO term (no self-drag — their own lens judged the deed when they heard it: §17's "the lords curse … the commons say it was well done"), and neither do the court's own MEMBERS (its ruler and `ACT_OFFICE` holders, §5.2 — the voice IS their derived regard, §5.3, so their taking it would feed `courtRegardOf` back into itself) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — the members' exclusion: A.15 names the commons; a person who merely lives in the voicing town with no feeling of his own about the court reads 0 by construction — a town has no pair state with itself]. EXECUTED (`cure/D-RN-r4-probe/r4Probe.out.json` B): a condemnation at the stock's neutral weight reads −0.4 (the cap) at an `allied`, `trade_partner`, `patron`, `client` or `vassal` town, 0 at a `neutral`, `criminal_network` or unconnected town, +0.4 at a `rival`, `cold_war` or `hostile` one, and a proven liar's ±0.175; the round-3 formula gave −0.5 (`enmity`) to every hearer [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RN-W1 (`skeptic/RN-r3-verify.md`, MAJOR, EXECUTED: "the term is −0.5. That is `enmity` for a hearer indifferent to the court and the same −0.5 for the court's sworn enemy … above the per-deed lens cap of 0.4 … drags the voicing court's own commons"), under RULINGS-ROUND4 A.15, which re-rules A.11's "exactly as `party_slander`"]. The CONDEMNER'S COST of a court's condemnation falls on its RULER's key (`p:<ruler>`, persons lit — "their lord spoke against the ones who lifted the plague"); with persons dark nothing is written (a town's feeling about its own court is no RN pair). A court voices only about the PARTY (A.11's trigger); its feeling about a person or a corps acts through its officers' cards (§5.2) and mints no voice. A NAMED BODY never voices, about the party or anyone: its fame is a tale's, never a public word, and THE BODIES' law holds that no body acts toward the party (BD15); A.11's trigger names a court, a person or a corps [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes: with `g:` holders and body fame admitted, the famous-holder crossing above would otherwise reach a gang]. **A CROSSING, DECLARED ONCE** (every holder, person, corps or court): for a holder whose terms are STORED (the belief presets' persisted heard terms, and a body re-reading them) the holder's band read just BEFORE RN's pass writes this tick's heard terms against its band just AFTER them — never a rebuild of an earlier tick, which a term REPLACED in place (RN6) would falsify, so a replaced version can never voice twice once the cooldown lapses; for a DERIVED holder (the omniscient and aspatial arms) the band at this tick WITH the terms of the deeds it first hears this tick (`heardAt(...).heardTick` = this tick) against the band at this tick WITHOUT them — the derived twin of "before and after the pass's writes", both from the immutable deed ledger and the holder's CURRENT members, so a succession (a new ruler's terms), a strike or an unstrike moves both bands alike and is never a crossing [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RN-r4-verify minor: "A derived holder's 'crossing' can come from a succession or a strike" — round 4's "the band at this tick against the band at the previous tick" counted them]; RN stores no last band. Decay alone never crosses INTO an outer band (`decayTowardNeutral` is anti-ratchet: "never further from neutral"), and a DM's `SET_REGARD` or `DECLARE_STANCE` is no crossing (the DM voices by recording a deed), so a crossing is always a hearing's [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RN-r3-verify minor: "§5.5's crossing test 'band a tick ago' cannot be rebuilt after RN6 replaces a term in place, so a false re-voice is possible once the cooldown lapses"]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.11 · the verifier's minor ("PT §7.3's voice is 'a person', yet RN §5.5's famous holder may be a `u:` corps"; PT's side now reads "a court, a person or a corps") · §18's open question 4, closed: "a court means its ruler" is withdrawn — the ruler MAKES the court's voice, the court speaks; REFINED: the crossing's test is written down, which §5.5 had left implicit for derived holders.]

### 5.6 The DM's hand (requirement 14)

Verbs (the store-action lane, `operationRegistry`-registered, each with its inverse; each under the AUTHOR hat — ODQ §129.2 "every DM console verb declares its hat" — in the landed AUTHOR shape, stage-then-approve, as RULINGS-PL-MN SMN-19 records): `SET_REGARD(holder, subject, band | clear)` → `dmSet`; `DECLARE_STANCE(holder, subject, <REGARD_BANDS word> | ally | enemy | clear)` → `declared` (the allies-and-enemies list and any authored baseline; any holder kind, bodies and kingdoms included; town↔town, body↔body and faction↔faction refused, §2.2 — the last by P5 (WC-RN-49, 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5))); `STRIKE_DEED(deedId)` / `UNSTRIKE_DEED(deedId)`. THE DIALS (≤ 4, the MN actor convention): `targetSaveId` = the ANCHOR, always a real settlement (the holder's settlement; for a person, where it stands; for a corps, the town its unit stands at, else its employer's seat [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]; for a deed, its first witness); `holder` and `subject` ride actor-ref dials (`{kind, id?}`); the band or stance rides the fourth. No exemption from the proposal queue is claimed. The AI proposes these verbs from closed vocabularies only. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-23: the verbs declared no hat and borrowed the party door's `applyMode: 'auto'` without naming an exemption.] Lifecycle survival is §14.

**Clock (regard):** weekly hearing (the pass); emergent terms decay `a_few_years` (156 weeks, "a bond, a standing, a grudge"); authored baselines never decay; `dmSet` holds until lifted. **First-visible:** the week a tale arrives — the town's dossier line. **Receipt / felt sentence:** "In Varn they curse the party's name for saving Kell, their old rival; the commons say it was well done." **Surface / scrutiny / machinery:** words on the surface (behind the party view's — PT's `partyView` — "who has heard" toggle) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — HOST-r1-5]; the one reason and the deed on scrutiny; warmth, grudge, weights and terms on intent.
