## §6 THE INHERENT VALUE OF A DEED AND THE TWO LENSES (RN-3)

### 6.1 The inherent value (derived, never stored; `inherentValueOf`)

Three coordinates on the landed axes, each on `[0, 1]` with 0.5 = no signal: `kind01` (kindness — the inverse of malice), `law01` (lawfulness), `force01` (aggressiveness, the third coordinate, concern 9e). ONE normalizer, declared once: `force01 = clamp01(aggressiveness / 2)`, because `settlementAlignment`'s aggressiveness is centered on 1.0 ("EXACTLY 1.0 = no signal") while the other two are centered on 0.5 (R2 hazard 14; its range is measured at RN-3's car-0). The DM-facing band reuses PT's words (`heroic · admirable · neutral · shameful · villainous`) by `kind01` edges (DRAFT; inclusive upward: `heroic` ≥ 0.85 · `admirable` ≥ 0.62 · `neutral` > 0.38 · `shameful` > 0.15 · `villainous` ≤ 0.15), then ONE OUTWARD STEP for the relief and harm kinds (`resolve_stressor`, `ease_stressor`, `worsen_stressor`, `clear_condition`, `impose_condition`) at magnitude BAND ≥ 2 — both inside `inherentValueOf`, so the table's last column is its output, never a second rule. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-27 · RN-r1-22: `resolve_stressor` sat ON the heroic edge at 0.85; at 0.8 the step, not the edge, makes a full cure heroic, and every default party impact (band 3) takes that step.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7: since round 2 no TOWN's `aggressiveness` is read (§6.2); the normalizer serves only a 1.0-centred aggression read where one is taken, and a person's `personAggression` is a new leaf on [0, 1] with 0.5 = no signal (its scale measured at RN-3's car-0).] PT's `moralWeight` mark, when present, REPLACES the derived band for every reader THAT CAN SEE IT (the night's context differs): the DM's view always; a hearer only where its version shows the reason part (proof `spoken` or `shown`, completeness at the reason's threshold, PT §11.1) — an `unspoken` mark reaches no hearer, who reads the derived value, the act alone. A hearer who sees the reason takes the marked band whole: credibility does not scale it (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.2: "`spoken`/`shown` replace"). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(c) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.2): "for every reader" ignored PT's visibility rule.]

| Party-impact kind | kind01 | law01 | force01 | Target-dependent? | On its face |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `resolve_stressor` | 0.8 | 0.5 | 0.5 | no (relief) | admirable; heroic at magnitude band ≥ 2 (every default) |
| `ease_stressor` | 0.7 | 0.5 | 0.5 | no | admirable |
| `worsen_stressor` | 0.2 | 0.4 | 0.6 | no | shameful; villainous at magnitude band ≥ 2 |
| `name_attacker` | 0.5 | 0.5 | 0.5 | no | neutral (no moral content) |
| `broker_relationship` | 0.7 | 0.6 | 0.35 | no (peace on its face) | admirable |
| `inflame_relationship` | 0.3 | 0.4 | 0.7 | yes — AMBIGUOUS (neutral) when either side's ruler reads `malicious` | shameful otherwise |
| `clear_condition` | 0.75 | 0.5 | 0.5 | yes — by the condition's own valence (a harmful condition cleared is relief); a condition with no valence is AMBIGUOUS | admirable |
| `impose_condition` | 0.25 | 0.4 | 0.6 | yes — the inverse | shameful |
| `bolster_faction` | by target: `1 − malice01` of the faction's leader | 0.5 | 0.5 | yes — AMBIGUOUS when the leader reads `balanced` | — |
| `undermine_faction` | by target: `malice01` of the leader | 0.45 | 0.55 | yes — AMBIGUOUS when `balanced` | — |
| `empower_npc` | by target: `1 − malice01` of the NPC | 0.5 | 0.5 | yes — AMBIGUOUS when `balanced` | — |
| `remove_npc` | by target: `malice01` of the NPC (unseating a cruel tyrant is not unseating a beloved healer) | 0.3 | 0.75 | yes — AMBIGUOUS when `balanced` | heroic when the target is a `malicious` ruler [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-10: the backing's law shift is struck from this row; a backing enters every reading ONCE, as PT §10.2's term (§6.2)] |
| `opportunity_act` · `captive_rescued` [ADDED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c; OP §2.4 (OP PROPOSES, DRAFT — RN's to sign) · OP-r1-27)] | 0.7 | 0.3 | 0.6 | no | admirable (a cell broken is lawless) |
| `opportunity_act` · `rescue_foiled` [ADDED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c; OP §2.4, DRAFT — RN's to sign)] | 0.6 | 0.3 | 0.6 | no | admirable on its face (the attempt) |

AMBIGUOUS ⇒ the derived value is `neutral` until the DM marks it (concern 9f). Target reads are the landed person lens (6.2) and, for conditions, the condition catalog's row (PLAUSIBLE: the valence field is measured at RN-3's car-0; an absent valence is ambiguous). `party_event` kinds (the composer's event types, PT's registration — including the institution rows that RULINGS-ROUND1 PT-r1-2 keys on the target's category) append rows by registration in PT's waves, under the same three coordinates. **THE `npc_verdict` ROWS** are RN's own (RN owns the source), keyed by the stamp's `scandalClass` (`npcLedgerFacets.js :: SCANDAL_CLASSES`) — the exposed wrong IS the deed — DRAFT, UNMEASURED, on the FP-46 roster: `venality` 0.30 · 0.20 · 0.45 shameful · `betrayal` 0.20 · 0.30 · 0.50 shameful · `brutality` 0.10 · 0.30 · 0.80 villainous · `conspiracy` 0.25 · 0.15 · 0.55 shameful · `heresy` 0.50 · 0.30 · 0.50 neutral on its face (a lawful hearer condemns it by the law term) · `none` 0.35 · 0.30 · 0.50 shameful. A verdict deed is read by LENS ONE ALONE — a stranger's crime is weighed as what it was; its target is the sentencing court's institutional record (§5.3), never a party lens two helps or hurts. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V3: no verdict row existed, so every verdict deed read AMBIGUOUS ⇒ neutral, the town's regard never went negative, and §4.4 could never yield `notorious`/`infamous` from a verdict.] **THE `paragon_act` BLOCK** is ONE block of this table keyed (CH's act kind × `domain` × `polarity`), each row the three coordinates; CH SUPPLIES its values as a registration row at CH-3 through `inherentValue.js`'s registration door, and RN derives nothing CH did not register; a REGISTRATION WALKER pins that every (act kind × domain × polarity) CH can mint has its row, so no paragon act falls to `neutral` by omission (CH's emergent bans, scandal at the gate and redemption read these rows). Both volumes carry this sentence. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-2 (RN's half; CH §2.2b's "never authored by CH" is CH's lane to amend to "supplied by CH, derived by none").] The table's key is the ADDENDUM's general form, act kind × the act's category: for PT's composer rows the category is the TARGET's (PT-r1-2's institution rows); for CH's paragon rows it is the act's own domain and side (CH §2.2b), and a target-dependent paragon row reads its target through lens two (CH's "yes" column) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's owed note on SEAMS-r1-2]. **THE `body_act` BLOCK** is one more block of this table, keyed (BD's act kind × the body's kind × the target's category), each row the three coordinates; BD SUPPLIES its values as a registration row at its wave through the same registration door, RN derives nothing BD did not register, and the REGISTRATION WALKER pins every act kind BD can mint, so no body act falls to `neutral` by omission (a raid on a hamlet, alms given, a flock sacked) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (`cure/L-BD.md` §4 RN (2): "BD registers its act rows in the inherent-value table (the SEAMS-r1-2 idiom)")]. The walker's closed list is BD's (§3.1): `raided · robbed_road · fought · gave_shelter · merged · escaped · paid_quarter · fed_poor · robbed_quarter`, the six public `body_stance` words, and an army's sack of a flock; the three QUARTER acts are acts upon the town's OWN people, so the town's commons read them through lens two as acts upon themselves (+1 for `paid_quarter` and `fed_poor`, −1 for `robbed_quarter`, × the magnitude band, §6.2's "a holder the deed helped itself reads +1"), whatever the town's alignment — the owner's "good purchase" gang in an evil, chaotic town included; their lens-one values are BD's rows [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RU-BD-4 · RU-BD-9 · BD §19.0b RN (c)]. **CH's `hunt_won` ROWS** are two rows of the `paragon_act` block (CH §2.2b: a gang or a beast pack `0.70 · 0.65 · 0.65`, admirable; a conclave `0.40 · 0.60 · 0.70`, neutral on its face, its creed's faithful reading the scattering through their own stance), registered by CH whoever the leader is (the office-holder included, §3.1); CH's two **`warden_attempted`** rows (a bribe `0.30 · 0.20 · 0.40`, shameful; a strike `0.10 · 0.15 · 0.85`, villainous — CH §2.2b) are rows of the same block whose DOER is a body, read on the body's key exactly as any body doer's deed (§2.1), the walker counting them CH's [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — `cure/D-CH-r5.md` §5 RN]; and PT's `party_event` kind **`body_ended`** — the party's hand ending a body, the same fact by another doer — READS THOSE TWO ROWS by the body's kind (PT §3.3: "PT registers no third row"): the registration walker counts `body_ended` covered by CH's rows, so the party's ending of a gang reads admirable, never neutral by omission [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RN-r4-verify RU-PT-j2 (`cure/D-PT-r4.md` §5 RN: "RN reads `body_ended` on CH's `hunt_won` rows (registered by CH, no PT row)") · RU-CH-h · B.5 ("PT's `body_ended` reads `hunt_won`")]. **THE PLEDGE READING** (`party_pledge`): the REQUESTER takes one direct term — `made_good` → `pledge_kept`, + DRAFT 0.2; `fell_short` (a reachable miss, PT's fault) → `pledge_broken`, − DRAFT 0.3 — and every other hearer reads the deed through LENS TWO ONLY (its tie to the requester × the sign), CLAMPED: a `fell_short` reading is at most 0 (a broken word is no one's gift: a friend of the jilted town cools, its rival does not warm), and a `made_good` reading obeys the owner's rule (a non-malicious rival may grumble, never hold a grudge; a malicious one keeps it); a pledge has no lens-one reading, because keeping one's word is weighed by credibility (§7), never twice. PT's P8 pins it. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-7.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2 A.3 (PT's kinds) · the verifier's minor: unclamped, a broken pledge to Kell WARMED Kell's rival (+0.1333 before the clamp, 0 after; EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r2-probe/r2Probe.out.json` c).] `voiced_regard` has no inherent value (it is read through the speaker, §5.5). The party volume cites this table and never re-specifies it.

### 6.2 The two lenses (`readDeed`)

**The holder's coordinates** (one read per holder kind, all landed): a TOWN — the alignment AXES' LANDED BANDS, never the continuous drive: `lawWordFor(settlementAlignment(item, worldState).lawfulness01)` and `natureWordFor(…malice01)`, each band entering the lenses at its DRAFT centre (`lawless` / `benevolent` 0.17 · `balanced` 0.5 · `lawful` / `malicious` 0.83), and NO aggression coordinate (a town's `aggressiveness` is `computeAggressiveness` → `const pers = personalityDrive(settlement);`, whose weights cancel — alignment-3 — so the force term reads no signal for a town; H30's published aggression word is its slot) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7 (RULINGS-ROUND1 ADDENDUM as worded; RULINGS-ROUND2 B)]; a PERSON — the person lens `{ lawfulness01, malice01, aggression }` from the declared alignment, else the seeded `npcStates[...].alignment`, else `TRAIT_ALIGNMENT` / `TRAIT_AGGRESSION` (the logic of `warSeatBooks.js :: rulerAlignment`, which is MODULE-PRIVATE today and returns `{ lawfulness01, malice01 }` only: RN-3's car-0 moves `rulerAlignment`, `rulingSeatId` and `moralityBand` byte-identically to a leaf `renown/personLens.js` that `warSeatBooks.js` re-imports; the AGGRESSION coordinate is a NEW leaf function `personAggression(...)` over `TRAIT_AGGRESSION` with its own pin, never presented as a move [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-25]); a BODY — its ruler's person lens; a CORPS (`u:`) — its mark's authored `alignment` (`NPC_ALIGNMENTS`, CH P1) through the person lens's declared-alignment arm, its μ from the mark's `character.axes` exactly as a paragon's (below), and no force coordinate (it carries no roster traits, so the force term reads no signal, as for a town) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]. A NAMED GANG (`g:`) — the villain's axes THE BODIES gives a gang (BD §4.2: "a gang's 'alignment' is its DEEDS (villainous by nature)", `alignmentAxis: 'evil'`, `lawAxis: 'chaotic'`), entering at the town band centres (`malicious` 0.83 · `lawless` 0.17), or its LEADING paragon's chart where one leads it (CH's `leads`, L-BD 32; the person lens's declared arm); μ 0.5, or the leader's; no force coordinate (no roster traits) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes]. **Which character each holder reads, declared:** a town reads the BANDS of `settlementAlignment`'s two axes — requirement 13's W0 read; a body reads its RULER's person character (the seat books' logic); a person its own. A town and its own court can therefore read one deed differently — that is the owner's "the lords curse, the commons approve", not drift — and the Why panel NAMES which character carried each reading ("Varn's lords, by their ruler's temper"; "Varn's people, by the town's"). The town lens's inputs carry KNOWN SUBSTRATE DEFECTS (HOST-REQUIREMENTS-2026-10-18 alignment-3: the conscience and aggression weights cancel in the mean; alignment-4: one scored NPC sets the term; alignment-9a: moral drift's compounding fade; alignment-16: no `chaotic_good`), cited as such with the host's H30 as the cure's SLOT (its offer: "fix the moral drift's compounding decay and its freeze outside belief worlds"); because the town lens reads only the two axes' bands and never the aggression drive, RN's values never depend on the drive's weights, and a defect can move a town's reading only by moving its axis across a band edge. RN-3 lights on its own unit — NO lighting gate waits on the owner's alignment answers. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — HOST-r1-7.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7: the round-1 gate ("RN-3 LIGHTS only after the owner answers alignment-25e and -25g") was unruled and spread to C11's first chain and C13's PT-1/PT-2 through `readDeed`; it is struck, and H30 is named.]

**Lens one — what the act is:** `act = a·(kind01 − ½)·K + b·(law01 − ½)·(2·lawfulness01 − 1) + c·(force01 − ½)·(2·aggr01 − 1)`, with `K = max(0, 1 − 2·malice01)` for a kind act (`kind01 > ½`) and `K = 1 − 2·malice01` for a cruel one (DRAFT a 1.0, b 0.5, c 0.4): good holders approve kindness and condemn cruelty; evil holders SHRUG at kindness (K = 0 — never a condemnation) and may admire cruelty [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-3: the old factor turned an evil holder's view of every kindness into a grudge]; lawful ones weigh law and backing; a warlike COURT (its ruler's `TRAIT_AGGRESSION`) admires a brutal victory a peaceful one condemns, while a town reads no force term (above) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7]. **Lens two — whom it helped or hurt:** for each actor the deed helped or hurt (its target and, for relief, the relieved town; for the pair kinds, both sides), `ally = Σ ± net(holder, that actor) × magnitude/3` (magnitude the BAND as heard, `version.mag` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V2]), where `net` is, FIRST, for a town or court holder and a SETTLEMENT actor, the landed pair state: `pairNet = PAIR_NET[label] + κ·((trust − trust₀) − (resentment − resentment₀))`, clamped to [−1, 1], with `PAIR_NET` (DRAFT) `allied` +0.6 · `trade_partner` +0.3 · `patron` / `client` / `vassal` +0.2 · `neutral` 0 · `criminal_network` −0.2 · `rival` −0.4 · `cold_war` −0.6 · `hostile` −0.8, κ 0.5, and (trust₀, resentment₀) the label's own `RELATIONSHIP_DEFAULTS` — READ THROUGH BELIEF (RULINGS-ROUND4 §E; A.8's form): `label` = `beliefMap.js :: readBeliefRelationship(holderId, otherId, worldState, trueLabel)` — the believed `allianceLabel` where the holder holds a belief record of the other town, the true label otherwise (its fallback input: "truth / self / unknown ⇒ the known declared relationship") and in the omniscient and aspatial arms (`belief`: "`if (!beliefsActive(worldState)) return { source: 'truth' };`" — so those arms read exactly round 4's net); a TRUE `vassal` label is read true (the landed carve-out, `settlementStrategy.js`: "A vassal obligation reads the TRUE label … the belief fogs their STATE, not the contract"; PL16); κ's trust/resentment term — the pair's own memory of its dealings — rides ONLY a label that IS the true one, because the belief record carries none (`BeliefRecord`: `readiness · strengthBand · allianceLabel · faithLabel · confidence01 · lastUpdateTick`), so a stale believed label enters at its flat `PAIR_NET` value [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — two choices §E left to the leaf's owner, vetoable: the contract carve-out (a lord and its vassal know their own bond, as every landed decider reads it), and κ on a true label only (the alternatives — strike κ in every arm, a same-seed shift in the omniscient default, or apply the true pair's trust/resentment around a label the holder does not believe — were refused: the first moves the default preset for no belief reason, the second mixes a fact the record fogs into its believed label)]; a believed `unknown` (a record can carry it, `groundTruthBelief`) or any label off the table reads 0, no tie (the non-paranoid reading) — EXECUTED (`cure/D-RN-r5-probe/r5Probe.out.json` A: Varn and Kell truly `rival`, trust 0.20 / resentment 0.70 — omniscient −0.51, round 4's truth read −0.51; a current belief −0.51; a stale `hostile` belief −0.8; `unknown` 0; a vassal believed `neutral` +0.47 as `vassal`) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND4 §E OD-RN-48 RULED; RN-r4-verify FAIL A.8 ("§6.2 (l.402): 'the holder's OWN relationship, so no belief crossing' … `readBeliefRelationship` … returns a label only, so κ's trust/resentment term has no belief carrier"): round 4's "the holder's OWN relationship, so no belief crossing" is struck]; a holder the deed helped itself reads +1; for a person holder and a settlement actor, its RN link if any, else its residency town's pair state; for a CORPS holder (`u:`) and a settlement actor, its RN link if any, else the pair state of the court that RETAINS it (its employer's seat), else — a free company — that of the town at its unit's own position (`currentRegion`, §5.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RULINGS-ROUND3 A.12 · the verifier's minor ("a corps holder's lens two has no fallback for a settlement actor")]; for a PERSON or the PARTY actor, the holder's RN regard (§5); for a NAMED BODY actor (a court's hunt, an army's sack, a raid between bodies), the holder's RN regard of its key (`g:<bodyId>`, a conclave's creed) — and for a `g:` holder, THE BODIES' pair label between the two bodies on the same `PAIR_NET` (`allied` +0.6 · `neutral` 0 · `rival` −0.4 · `hostile` −0.8, BD §7.2's four landed labels; the refused class, §2.2), an act on the holder's own gang reading as an act on the holder itself; a nameless body or a beast is no tie [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes] — a deed done to a beast pack (a hunt won, `hunt_won`; the party's `body_ended`) reads its PLACE instead: the town(s) of its `actAt`, the land it troubled, as the relieved town of a relief deed (CH's route, "none for a beast — the place") [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RU-CH-h]. Helping a friend or hurting a rival is warm; helping a rival or hurting a friend is a grudge. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-1 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-1): RN's sparse ledger holds no town↔town tie by construction, so lens two reads the engine's own rivalry first — `cure/D-RN-r1-probe/lensProbe.out.json`: at the label defaults `rival` −0.4, `neutral` 0, `allied` +0.6, `hostile` −0.8.] **Personality sets the mix:** `reading = (1 − μ)·act + μ·ally` when lens two found at least one tie, else `reading = act` (a stranger judges the act alone), with μ (DRAFT) keyed on the LANDED trait adjectives of the holder's `personality` (`dominant`, `flaw`, `modifier`) through one table — the first of `dominant`, `flaw`, `modifier` that is in either list decides, so a positive `dominant` beside a negative `flaw` reads MAGNANIMOUS [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor on RN-r1-8] — SPITEFUL, μ 0.7: `vengeful · vindictive · petty · bitter · arrogant · proud · vain`; MAGNANIMOUS, μ 0.3: `magnanimous · merciful · compassionate · pious · fair-minded · gracious · warm-hearted`; 0.5 otherwise. A PARAGON reads its W-LIVES chart instead (`characterAxes` "AXIS:pole:level" — its `defining` position on a spite↔mercy axis maps to the table's two ends, measured at CH-1's car); a TOWN reads 0.5; a BODY its ruler's. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-8: `pride`, `vengeance` and `mercy` are tokens no person carries.] [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — a holder with no tie judges the act alone, so an empty lens two never halves a stranger's approval: a good non-rival reads +0.18 (`fondness`) for a lifted plague instead of +0.09 (`indifference`).] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor: the round-1 receipt `lensProbe.out.json` printed the pre-refinement mix in its `reading` field (0.09) and the act alone in `one` (0.18); re-executed under the refined rule, `cure/D-RN-r2-probe/r2Probe.out.json` a reads `goodNonRival_m02.reading` = 0.18.]

[ADDED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c; `cure/L-CH-PD.md` §4 RN (b) · RULINGS-P6 §F.1 · §F.3 · the owner's 2026-10-20 12:44 word "champions and villains should also have a patron diety option that would also contribute to their decision makings") — THE SWORN-DOER READING: a TEMPLE OFFICER of creed X (the acting officer of the creed body, §5.2's `ACT_OFFICE`) reading a deed whose HEARD doer is a paragon SWORN to X — THE CHAMPIONS' `swornDeityOf(paragon)` (the field `swornDeity`, RULINGS-P6 §F.3; public unless the oath is `secret_faith`) — reads LENS ONE with X's RECORDED COORDINATES in place of its own (the god's alignment and law axes and its DERIVED temper through the ONE temper seam `deityTemper`, mapped as CH states the mapping — MONOTONE in the god's character, RULINGS-P6 §F.1; RN mints no mapping): the faithful judge their sworn one by their god's character (the 09-12 carve-out "the followers of the diety acted that way"; BD4's idiom — THE FOLLOWERS ACT, THE GOD DOES NOT). Through `lensOne` (§2.3's P6c bracket); no new `REGARD_CAUSES` word (the term is `heard_deed`); PIN: a deed MATCHING the god's character warms the creed body's regard of its sworn one and a CONTRADICTING one makes a grudge (CH's S65 depends on it). RN §7 is UNCHANGED — no paragon credibility is minted (L-CH-PD route (c)). DARK until CH-11 lights.]

The temple officer's sworn-doer reading is REGISTERED HERE as the ONE OVERRIDE of "Which character each holder reads" above ("a person its own"): for this one doer class — a deed whose heard version carries an oath to the officer's own creed — the officer reads X's coordinates in place of the doer's own. The god-to-coordinate mapping is CH's (CH §10b, THE GOD'S THREE COORDINATES — RULINGS-P6 §F.1 as D-CH-PD-r1 derived it, its monotonicity PER HALF, CH S68 (i) as restated); RN mints none, and the bracket above's "MONOTONE in the god's character" reads per half. CH cites (CH §10b's flock paragraph).]

RULED (§J.3): the heard-version row GAINS AN OATH PART — `ver` gains ONE bit `oath`, set when the telling carried the doer's public oath, never under `secret_faith` — a PERSISTED-SHAPE WIDENING of RN's landed heard-version row, the OWNER's, VISIBLE (OD-RN-52 ⭐, §16; the ↳ row at §2.1's `terms`). UNTIL IT IS SIGNED lens one reads NO oath — no holder's reading of any deed takes a god's coordinates — and THE LIVE READ STAYS FORBIDDEN (never `swornDeityOf` on the live record: not as a fallback, not gated by the `doer` bit); the sworn lean applies only to the doer's own roll (CH's `weeklyChoice`, CH §10b) and to the temple officer's override registered above. [REFINED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P6c brackets-verify) — the override reads the CARRIED oath, whose only lawful carrier is the new bit, so it fires only on a heard version whose `oath` bit is set, i.e. once OD-RN-52 is signed; until then a temple officer reads a sworn doer's deed as any person does (its own character, "a person its own"), and CH S65's flock clause stays DARK (its row already reads "DARK until it lands"); vetoable — the chair's §J.3 parenthetical may be read as keeping the override live, which would need a carrier this bracket cannot name.]

**The owner's rule, by construction:** when lens one is ≥ 0 and the holder's landed morality band is not `malicious` (`warSeatBooks.js :: moralityBand` for a person or a body; `natureWordFor` for a town — the same edges; `balanced` = 0.33 < `malice01` < 0.67), lens two may cancel the warmth a good act earns but may not turn it into a grudge — the rival grumbles (a felt line about its enemy being helped, with no grudge term); a `malicious` holder's lens two stands, and the grudge is kept. Executed on the cured formula (`lensProbe.out.json`, resolve_stressor at band 3): a good rival (malice 0.2) reads 0 — it grumbles, no term; a balanced rival 0; a malicious rival −0.2, a grudge carried by lens two ("they saved Kell"); a malicious holder with NO tie 0 — it shrugs; cruelty reads −0.18 to a good holder and +0.24 to an evil one. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-3 · RN-r1-20.] TOWNS on their bands (EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r2-probe/r2Probe.out.json` b): a benevolent town reads a lifted plague at +0.198 alone and 0 as Kell's rival; a balanced town 0 either way; a malicious town 0 alone and −0.2 as a rival; cruelty −0.198 · 0 · +0.198 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7]. **The deed's parts:** where the version heard lost its reason or backing (PT-4's thresholds), lens one reads the act ALONE (the orphanage three towns over); where heard, PT's backing softens a villainous-looking act toward excusable inside the backer's reach and amplifies a good one — ONE carrier, PT §10.2's bounded term on the reading (no §6.1 row shifts for a backing) — and where the hearer's version shows the reason, a DM-marked `moralWeight` REPLACES the derived band (§6.1; RULINGS-ROUND2 A.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-10 · RN-r1-9] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(c): the round-1 credibility scaling of a told reason is withdrawn under A.2's "replace"; credibility stays the party's own fact (§7)]. **The result** is one term `{ sign, sev ≤ DRAFT 0.4 per deed, cause: heard_deed }` for the holder's regard of the doer — and of the backer, who shares the credit and inherits the grudges (PT's "shared ownership", read here as a second term of half weight, DRAFT; the backer's key by §2.2's backer table).

**Clock:** the reading is taken at hearing (weekly). **Receipt / felt sentence:** "Varn's lords curse your name for saving their rivals; their people do not." **Surface / scrutiny / machinery:** the place's verdict in words; on scrutiny which lens carried it ("they are Kell's rivals"; "they thought it cruel"); coordinates, μ and weights on intent.

## §7 CREDIBILITY (RN-1, the party's holder; the other three homes untouched)

The party's word is trusted or doubted by ONE PERSISTED holder `spatialLedgers.party.credibility = { score, lastUpdateTick }` (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.1) riding the landed person law verbatim: a `made_good` pledge adds `TRUE_RISE · mag`, a `fell_short` pledge (a reachable miss) subtracts `MISS_FALL · mag` (DRAFT 2.0, UNMEASURED — a missed promise is not a lie: one miss at magnitude 1 leaves the weight at 0.84, `doubted`, back to `ordinary` in about 37 weeks, where the lie charge would leave 0.47, `discredited`, for about 125 — `lensProbe.out.json`, on the landed law), and a reason EXPOSED as false — a `correction` the DM marked `contradicts` (PT §10.2) — the ONE exposure road [CURED 2026-10-20 (fp-final P5) — P5C-22 (WC-PT-60; RULINGS-P5 C.22: "the IN clause struck in PT §10.2 and RN l.413 (RN's lane)"): "or IN's exposure once C1 lands" is STRUCK — `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_IN.md` carries no party exposure (0 `party`), no fold row carries one, and PT §10.2's own next sentence says nothing else exposes a reason ("'contradicts' is never computed", Law One); PT struck its half] — subtracts `LIE_FALL · mag` (there is no separate "lie" mark on a miss: the DM amends the verdict or records the contradicting correction) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor: neither "exposures" nor "DM-marked lies" had a carrier] (`NPC_CREDIBILITY_TUNING`: 0.6 / 6.5 — "personal trust dies faster than a court's"), decayed by `decayedNpcCredibilityScore` (`HALF_LIFE_TICKS: 52`, spent past `MAX_LOOKBACK_TICKS: 260`), weighted by `npcCredibilityWeight` (neutral ⇒ exactly 1.0). **The writer** is the PARTY DOOR (PT's pledge verdict and exposure), never the dark statecraft mover — so the party's credibility lives wherever `renownEnabled` and PT's flag are lit, independent of `infoStatecraftEnabled` (R2 R-5). **The undo:** the holder is the running fold over the party's credibility CHARGES (its unstruck `party_pledge` deeds and contradicting corrections, in tick order, ties by deed id); a strike, an unstrike or PT's source-event undo of a charge REBUILDS the holder by refolding — exact, because the charges are deeds and deeds are never pruned — and never un-charges by subtraction, which the `SCORE_MAX: 12` clamp makes inexact (EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r2-probe/r2Probe.out.json` e: one lie of two struck, read at tick 60 — −4.2356 by refold, −4.1017 by subtraction). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(a) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.1): PERSISTED, RN wins; PT's lane drops "derived", and the inexact un-charge PT feared is cured here.] **The words** reuse `TESTIMONY_CREDIBILITY_BANDS`: weight ≥ 1.08 `trusted`, ≥ 0.9 `ordinary`, ≥ 0.6 `doubted`, else `discredited` (DRAFT) — machinery words; on the dossier the party's word speaks in sentences, because the court stock's DM read (`credibilityRead.js :: BAND_WORDS`) uses `doubted` for its neutral middle (§2.4). **The observer read.** The holder is the DM's view. A TOWN reads the party's word through `partyCredibilityAt(worldState, observerId, tick)`: the same law folded over only the charges that town has HEARD (`heardAt` — the whole projected reach in the omniscient arm; the road's arrival, or the heard record the person stage keeps until the law spends the charge, in the belief presets, §5.1), so a broken pledge it never heard of does not lower its trust [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V1 · RN-V5(a)]. **Readers:** an actor's willingness to accept "later" (PT's answer `later`: `partyCredibilityAt` at the requester's town), the dossier's sentence [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-PT-r2 §5.1(c): "the truth chasing a rumor (PT)" is struck — once A.2 withdrew credibility's scaling of a told reason, PT's correction (`SEND_WORD`) reads no credibility: it replaces a version by the parts it carries (§3.3)]; a told reason is no longer scaled by it (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.2, §6.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(c)]. It never merges with fame or regard (RN1). A paragon's credibility is the landed `npcCredibility` holder for its `wnpc_` (lit where `npcCredibilityEnabled` is; world-level by the landed law's own shape — a per-hearer read of a paragon's word is a slot with CH, C12, not invented here); a court's is the landed stock. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-9: the town's read is real and a miss has its own charge (PT §8.6's "− LIE_FALL · mag" for a reachable miss is PT's lane to re-point to `MISS_FALL`) · SEAMS-r1-22: the two ladders stated.] **Felt sentence:** "In Tolle they say the party's word is worth little since they never came to Ashford."

## §8 THE ARMS — what each world gets (requirement 17; RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH §7)

| World | Fame | Regard | The version heard | Persons |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| the default `realistic_regional`, and `quiet_local`, `static_campaign`, `narrative_campaign` (infoMode omniscient — no rumor road) | THE OMNISCIENT ARM: every deed known at once inside its projected scope; office fame | derived on read from the deed ledger through the lenses (towns, courts, the party as subject) | the deed itself (no mutation) | dark until `npcConsequencesEnabled` lights (C1's unit) |
| `dramatic_campaign`, `living_realm` (perfect_delayed, canonized map) | what the town heard (the road's own arrival, §4.3) | persisted heard terms (THE ONE PERSON STAGE) — the road's own arrival; bodies re-read the version their town heard (§5.3) | the whole tale, late (completeness stays 1.0) | as above |
| `full_simulation` (full, canonized map) | as above | persisted heard terms | the WEATHERED version (PT-4's parts fall away in the fixed order); a correction replaces | as above |
| any aspatial world | no delay (no digest) — the omniscient arm | derived | the deed itself | as above |
| `npcConsequencesEnabled` dark (every preset today) | office fame for roster persons; deeds only for actors with a key (the party; graduated persons once lit) | towns and courts (bodies through the ruler's lens); no officer differs from its court | — | no person keys (RN16) |

THE PAID TIER: renown is part of the simulator, which is premium (requirement 24(e); OD-FC-43): no free-tier arm.

**On a map-module world** (HOST-REQUIREMENTS-2026-10-18): H22 canonizes every new map-world campaign at birth "whatever its tier" — RULED COMPATIBLE (RULINGS-ROUND1 ADDENDUM HOST-r1-2): the world canon is geography, "canonized ≠ premium", and the premium gate is on the simulator's lanes [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the verifier's minor: the "OWNER row" wording was stale]. RN's arms do not move with it: the road arm keys on the preset's infoMode (the default preset is omniscient on any map), the canon only prices arrival (the rumor road's relay over `hopWeeks`) where a belief infoMode is lit — the omniscient arm reads no delay [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the carried minor on §4.3] — and RN's paid tier is the simulator's, not the canon's. RN sends nothing through the map port in this program; its two ledgers live in `spatialLedgers`, which the one audience projector (`worldSnapshotPublic.js`) hard-denies, so any future renown lens row (H23) is DM-only and fail-closed by construction. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — HOST-r1-2.]

## §9 THE VOICES (RN-4)

- **The dossier** (requirement 6's round trip): each town's page gains a "Known here" block under the party view's (PT's `partyView`) and the court page's toggles [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — HOST-r1-5] — who is known in this town and for what ("the ones who broke the siege at Kell"), and how the town and its court take them ("the commons speak well of them; the Watch Captain does not") — in the world's words, the reason one click deeper, bands only on intent. A person's page shows where they are known and their ties with reasons ("an old enmity with Merewick's crown, since the burning of Tolle").
- **The Why panel and the cause walk** (C0.5): every regard term and every fame entry carries `causedBy` to its deed, and a voiced deed to the regard that voiced it, so the generic walk renders "why does Varn hate the party?" two hops deep (requirement 20; THE LEGIBILITY PIN).
- **The Herald:** one registered kind, `voiced_regard` (routing, voice pool, rumor phrase, the kind-registration freezes `KIND_REGISTRATION_FREEZES` +1 — R2 Q9); fame and regard changes themselves are CHRONICLE lines, never pushed (the push budget, chair notes item 2); the deed's own news is PT's/CH's. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — requirement 16 for the slow fade (chair notes item 5): fame's fade is derived on read, so its sign is the dossier's on-read line ("Few outside Kell remember the siege now"), which the age receipt (C8) may lead with; no writer is minted to push it.]
- **The Evidence Law:** who, which power, what effect — "The High Priest of Kell, held in esteem across the river towns, has condemned the party; Kell's faithful have cooled toward them." Never a forecast; never a numeral; never a mechanism name.

## §11 THE HEARINGS

RN owns NO engagement kind and registers no row in the engagement registry (fold §3.4): it is a read model and a ledger of facts. Its reads appear ON other programs' cards — the acting officer and that officer's reason (§5.2), the requester's fame-born motive (PT's call board), a champion's regard (CH) — and RN's BANDS DO FEED those programs' defaults (CH's `person_banned` and PT's `party_banned` default to `banned` at `hatred`; the gate watches at `mistrust`): the owning program maps the band to its default, and RN never chooses a verdict. Requirement 24's three choices therefore apply to nothing RN mints; a voice naming the party is PT's `party_voiced` hearing — RN's crossing its producer (a court's crossing included, A.11), RN's writer its deed at `spoken` (§5.5) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-6, RN's half: "never decide a card's default" was false] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-6 RULED]; the DM's hand over renown is §5.6's verbs, available any week. **Long advances (requirement 24(c)):** a month, season or year advance runs the heard pass every tick inside the span (in THE ONE PERSON STAGE), decays by age, and mints voiced deeds where crossings occur (a voice naming the party is PT's hearing, resolved on its default `spoken` inside a long advance); nothing queues and nothing waits for the DM; the party's default ("nothing has happened to the party yet") is untouched because RN never acts on the party. A `body_act` deed (§3.1) is minted from THE BODIES' own hearings' closing rows — or, for a body's act toward its own town's people that resolves as a receipt, from BD's act receipt — and is never a hearing of RN's; RN never acts on a body either [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RU-BD-4]. A hunt's outcome deeds are the hunt's own door's (CH's `hunt_won`; a body's `escaped` through BD's) at `hunt_taken`'s close, never RN's (B.5). A court's crossing whose walk reaches no settlement beyond its own opens no `party_voiced` card (§5.5) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RN-X2 · B.10; REFINED].

## §12 THE FLAGS (virtual; absent-when-dark; read `=== true`; each with its certification row and lit-mutant pins)

[REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — the brief named one flag; the leans that move other programs' numbers are split onto a second flag so the reads and the store can light where the leans' same-seed shift has not been signed.]

| Flag | Gates | Host flags | May light in |
|---|---|---|---|
| `renownEnabled` | the deed ledger and its mint, the deed's telling arm, fame and regard reads, the notoriety projection into every observer read (§4.4), the regard ledger and the heard pass, the DM's verbs, the dossier blocks, the party's credibility law, the ransom projection | PT's `partyEnabled` (party deeds), CH's flag (paragon deeds), `npcConsequencesEnabled` (person keys and `npc_verdict`), CH's `paragonCorpsEnabled` with WC's `freeCompaniesEnabled` (corps keys `u:`; SEAMS-r2-6), THE BODIES' `bodiesEnabled` (body keys `g:`, body doers and `body_act`; RULINGS-ROUND4 B) and `bodyEffectsEnabled` (the `hunt_taken` card, so CH's `hunt_won` and a body's `escaped`; RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5 · B.16) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.5], a spatial digest ∧ a non-omniscient infoMode (the road arms) | every world-alive preset incl. the default (its derived arm is aspatial-safe and display-side: the deed telling seeds rumors only where a road exists) — lighting is a disclosed same-seed shift where a rumor road exists (deeds now seed it; the feed seeds they suppress, §3.4; the belief-map refresh their tellings cause through `reportsBySubject`, measured at RN-0's car) and a display shift elsewhere |
| `renownLeansEnabled` | the priest's lean (§5.4), the operative re-point (§4.5), voiced regard (§5.5) | `renownEnabled`; the faith local lane (deity worlds); ES (`espionageActive`); `npcConsequencesEnabled` (person and company voices, the priest's lean, the operative; a COURT's voice needs none, A.11) | where each host is lit; a signed same-seed shift at its unit |

**Certification rows:** both virtual keys enter the simulation-rules manifest dark, with the absent-when-dark pin. **Lit-mutant pins:** (1) both dark ⇒ byte-identical on every golden seed; (2) `renownEnabled` lit with NO deed and NO DM seed ⇒ byte-identical (nothing is minted, nothing derives — the null world); (3) dark-after-lit ⇒ the ledgers kept, every read inert, every lean returns its host's identity; (4) `renownLeansEnabled` without `renownEnabled` ⇒ refused (reads `renownEnabled` first); (5) a person stamped while RN was dark reads by the landed fade after lighting (no deed, so no projection change). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-6 (RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-6): the projection is `renownEnabled`'s — one flag; `renownLeansEnabled` carries only leans into other programs' numbers · RN-r1-30: the belief-map shift is disclosed with the lighting.] `hearingWeekEnabled` is not a host flag (§11).

## §13 THE WAVES (every wave: leaf modules · hosts · FIRST-VISIBLE · TIER · DEGRADED ARM · EDITOR (seal · direction row · counterparty · states at the chokepoint · the cause) · Alignment · `causedBy`)

A coherence chain **C11 · THE RENOWN** (fold §6, at P4): it precedes C12 THE CHAMPIONS and C13 THE PARTY because both read it (chair notes item 6). Its first felt chain is ONE HEAD UNIT, RN-0 + PT-0 (the deed door and the party's marks land together: with persons dark until C1's unit, party deeds are the only deeds a DM can make, and RN's `party_impact` row is lit only with PT's `partyEnabled`) → RN-1 → RN-2 → RN-3 (→ RN-4's dossier lines): the DM records a deed, the witness towns know it that week, and a rival town's LORDS — RN-2's `courtRegardOf`, which PT-1's net effect and every PT-2 officer also read — curse it while its commons approve. RN-2 therefore lands before C13's PT-1 and PT-2; PT §15 and the fold §6 C11/C13 rows are owed the same sentence (PT's lane; the fold's bracket is the chair's). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — SEAMS-r1-15.]

**RN-0 — THE DEED AND FAME.** Leaves `renown/deedLedger.js` (`mintDeed`, `strikeDeed`, `projectScope`, the normalizer naming every field incl. PT's), `renown/deedTelling.js` (the deed arm of `advanceRumorLedgers`), `renown/fame.js` (`fameOf`, `fameIn`, `OFFICE_SCOPE`). Hosts: `partyImpact.js` (+3, the first `DEED_SOURCES` row; PT's door), `rumorNetwork.js` (+5: the call and the feed-suppression guard). Car-0: MEASURED in round 2 for the doors — the composer's action carries no event id today (V-RN's probe: `composerActionCarriesEventId: false`), so PT's `mapEventToPartyImpact` adds `sourceEventRef: event.id` (PT §3.1), and the party door's feed key is tick-free (`feedRef`, §2.1); the car still measures the belief-map shift of deed tellings through `reportsBySubject` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8]. Pins: two removals in one settlement mint two deeds and two tellings (R2 hazard 6, negative case); two IDENTICAL acts in one tick mint two deeds (the ordinal); one act through the party door and the composer door mints ONE deed (`sourceEventRef`); three panel removals at one target, two at tick 10 and one at tick 200, mint three deeds and none refuses another, each feed entry suppressed only at its own tick (SEAMS-r1-8); one act gives ONE telling (the feed seed suppressed); a realm deed's observer ring shrinks with its decay; every read finds deeds through `deedIndex`, none scans the ledger; a struck deed counts nowhere; a deed minted dark is never minted; `projectScope` is deterministic over a codepoint-shuffled graph; a realm deed reads realm about two and a half years and then regional; a registered `TELLING_SOURCES` telling seeds once per seed, copies only its declared content fields and moves no fame, regard or credibility (no `deed:` eventRef — D-CH-r2 §5.2(d)); a corps doer and a party deed's `partySeq` survive a save round-trip through the one normalizer (SEAMS-r2-6, D-PT-r2 §5.1(a)). Round 3 pins: two distinct acts whose composites hash to one id mint two deeds (the probe's salt; the stored `actRef` tells them apart) and the same act re-read mints none; a site deed's `siteId` survives a save round-trip through the one normalizer (RULINGS-ROUND3 B). Round 4 pins (RULINGS-ROUND4 A.9; BD's routes): a registered row declaring `relayedTick` and `arrivalTick` seeds a record its origin alone ever holds, invisible until its arrival and gone thirteen weeks after it (the findings — EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r4-probe/r4Probe.out.json` A); a row declaring neither relays as a feed seed; the leak's key carries the hire's `sinceTick`; BD's `leak:body:` row seeds as CH's leak does and moves no fame, regard or credibility; a nameless body's act and a beast's mint no deed; a body doer, a body target and `ledBy` survive a save round-trip through the one normalizer; a patrol's clash with a body mints PR's `patrol_clash` and never a `body_act` beside it. Round 5 pins (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5; the verifier's RU rows): ONE HUNT, ONE DEED — a LED hunt's win mints ONE `hunt_won` (CH's door, doer the leader the card named, the office-holder included) and NO `patrol_clash`, NO `site_verdict` and no bounty deed beside it (negative case: the round-4 text's two deeds); an UNLED clash the riders win mints `patrol_clash` with the TOWN as doer (no fame for the town; no town's or court's RN regard of it moves); a hunt that closes `escaped` mints the named body's `escaped`, and a pack's escape nothing; the party's `body_ended` reads CH's `hunt_won` rows (a gang's admirable — never neutral by omission); a named gang's `paid_quarter` and `robbed_quarter` mint from BD's act receipt with the act's id as `recordKey`, the same act re-read minting none; a `site_sale` `sold` is a court's tale and its `refused` the flock's deed; a covert tie's leak keys `leak:body:<bodyId>:<courtId>:<sinceTick>`, a SPONSOR's tie included, one per tie. FIRST-VISIBLE: the week of the deed — the witness town's dossier "word of the deed at Kell goes out along the river road" (requirement 16). TIER: aspatial-safe (the omniscient arm); the road arm on a canonized map with a belief infoMode; party deeds need PT's `partyEnabled`. DEGRADED ARM: aspatially and in the omniscient presets, known at once inside the projected scope. EDITOR: seal — `STRIKE_DEED` (and unstrike) · direction row — the DM's deed record (PT's door) · counterparty — `real-only` (a deed's target is a real actor) · states at the chokepoint — `deedLedger.js` is the only writer · the cause — the DM's act or the engine verdict. Alignment: READ — the deed records the act; its moral read is RN-3's (alignment-empty here, by design: fame is not judgment). `causedBy`: each deed names its source act (the party impact's command, the verdict).

**RN-1 — THE REGARD IDIOM (the one writer; credibility; the ransom projection).** Leaves `renown/regardLedger.js` (the entry, the ladder, decay, the cap, `SET_REGARD` / `DECLARE_STANCE` and their inverses), `renown/regardHeard.js` (`advanceHeardRegard`), `renown/partyCredibility.js` (the holder's law; its writer is PT's door), the `ransomRegardBand` projection. Hosts: THE ONE PERSON STAGE — the envoyPulse pre-gate pass (+4; RULED; RN owns the mount and its closed registration list, CH and PT register). Pins: one deed moves one holder once per version (a correction replaces — negative case: re-hearing the same version adds nothing); an emergent term decays to indifference and an authored one to its declared band (an authored `esteem` is moved by a deed and returns with time); the thirteenth EMERGENT subject drops the weakest (the smallest strongest decayed term), never a `declared` or `dmSet` entry; town↔town entries are refused at the writer; a `fell_short` pledge charges `MISS_FALL`, a contradicting correction `LIE_FALL`; striking a charge after a month refolds the holder to the never-charged history exactly; a town that never heard of a broken pledge keeps its trust, and one that heard keeps it heard past the telling's expiry; unstrike restores every term exactly, and so does undoing a KILL (a removed holder's entries are skipped, pruned only by decay); the party never holds an entry (refused at the writer); `dmSet` wins until lifted and survives reload; the ransom projection's three rows. Round 2b pins: the stage's rebinding — a registered pass's write into `settlementUpdates` or `wizardNews` reaches the kernel with `envoyDiplomacyActive` false and true alike (D-CH-r2 §5.2(a)); a refused-pair read (`regardOf`/`courtRegardOf` with an `s:`/`c:` holder and an `s:`/`c:` subject) returns `null` with reason `pair_state`, never a number (SEAMS-r2-11); a `u:` holder keeps an entry about the court that retains it, and a town's commons one about the company (SEAMS-r2-6); a disbanded company's entries are skipped and return exactly when the disbandment is undone. Round 3 pins (RN-V2): a better telling that keeps the parts mask but changes the magnitude band re-reads the term (R41); a swapped doer moves the deed's one term to the new subject and the next tick writes nothing (negative case: an unchanged triple writes nothing); a version below VAGUE writes no doer term; a body re-reads the moved term's version (R58). Round 4 pins (BD's routes; the verifier's minors): a named gang holds an entry about the court that hunts it and a town's commons one about the gang; `regardOf(g:<a>, g:<b>)` and `regardOf(g:<a>, creed:<x>)` return `null` (`pair_state` — THE BODIES' pairs); a body's end, or its lapse below the naming threshold, skips its entries, and the undo or a re-naming restores them exactly; a capped deed's telling off its capped reach writes nothing; a version swapped to a settlement doer writes no `s:` term and removes the old doer's. Round 5 pins (the verifier's minors): a doer swap away from a led body removes the leader's half term with the doer term, and a swap TO the leader leaves him one term for the deed, never two; a deed stamped `ledBy` counts for the leader in `fameIn` wherever the heard version names his body. FIRST-VISIBLE: the week a tale reaches a town — its dossier's "Known here" line in that town's voice. TIER: towns and courts in every world-alive preset (derived); persisted heard terms where a rumor road exists; persons need `npcConsequencesEnabled` (C1). DEGRADED ARM: derived everywhere (the fallback). EDITOR: seal — `SET_REGARD`, `DECLARE_STANCE` · direction row — the DM's allies-and-enemies list · counterparty — `real-only` · states at the chokepoint — `regardLedger.js` alone writes `spatialLedgers.regard` · the cause — the deed heard or the DM's act. Alignment: READ (the lenses, RN-3). `causedBy`: each term names its deed; each DM set names the DM act.

**RN-2 — THE COURT'S DERIVED REGARD AND THE OFFICE OF THE ACT.** Leaves `renown/courtRegard.js` (`courtRegardOf`; faction and creed bodies), `renown/actOffice.js` (`ACT_OFFICE`, `actingOfficer`, bounded graduation of officers), the notoriety projection (`notorietyProjection`) and its two chokepoints, and the `npc_verdict` deed source — RN's pass in THE ONE PERSON STAGE reading the verdict's ledger record (§3.1), with `npcVerdictApply.js`'s car (+≈6: two exports and the `in_office` re-stamp; ADDITIVE — the stamp stays, RN4) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-r1-6 · D-RN-r2-N1]. Pins (added in round 1): in a belief preset a court never knows a part its commons did not hear; an `in_office` placement never appears in the Wanderers register; a kingdom's declared enemy is felt in a direct member's court at its liege share and not in a sub-vassal's under a `frayed` hold (`crownReachAt`); a `turncoat` verdict mints no deed; an `in_office` officer later banished is re-stamped and has one `npc_verdict` deed; a record restamped by a move mints nothing; a creed's faithful feel in the dark arm; a person stamped with no deed fades by the landed fade, one with a deed by RN's alone. Round 3 pins (RN-V7): a first jailing stamped `known` reads `known` (never `notorious`) inside rings 0..2 of the sentencing town and `unknown` at hop 4, in the omniscient arm and on the road alike (a telling carried past the capped reach counts nowhere); a `notorious` banishment never reads `infamous`; an `infamous` stamp still arms `rejectionFor`'s outright refusal; the verdict deed reads `regional` about twenty-two weeks on `a_year` (R56, R57). Pins: a succession replaces the ruler's term the same week (the court's stance moves with its people); a court whose officers never heard of the party is indifferent; the institutional record reads only deeds whose target is the court; no court opinion field exists anywhere (a source-scan pin); graduation touches at most the ruler and the `ACT_OFFICE` holders. FIRST-VISIBLE: the next card an officer makes (PT/CH) names the officer and the reason — or, with no card, the court page's "the Watch Captain does not forget the riot". TIER: aspatial-safe; officers distinct from the court only with `npcConsequencesEnabled`. DEGRADED ARM: the court through its ruler's lens alone. Alignment: READ (the ruler's lens). `causedBy`: an officer's card names the officer's term.

**RN-3 — INHERENT VALUE AND THE TWO LENSES.** Car-0: extract `rulerAlignment`, `rulingSeatId` and `moralityBand` from `warSeatBooks.js` to `renown/personLens.js` leaf-first (byte-identical behaviour); the aggression read `personAggression` is a NEW leaf function with its own pin; measure `personAggression`'s scale for the declared normalizer; measure how often the host's catalogued defects (HOST-REQUIREMENTS-2026-10-18 alignment-3, -4, -9a, -16) move a town's axis across a band edge — the only way they reach a reading (§6.2) — with H30 the cure's slot; NO lighting gate waits on the owner's alignment answers [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-25 · HOST-r1-7] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r1-7: the gate is struck]. Leaves `renown/inherentValue.js` (the twelve-row table and its registration door for PT/CH/verdict rows; the condition-valence read, measured at the car), the lenses inside `readDeed`. Pins: THE OWNER'S RULE — a good rival reading a good act that helped its enemy grumbles and holds no grudge, an evil rival keeps it (both negative cases); the orphanage without its reason reads as cruelty, with its reason and backing inside the backer's reach as excusable; an ambiguous act is neutral until marked; an evil holder with NO tie shrugs at a kindness (no grudge — negative case); a rival town's grudge reads the landed `rival` pair state; every (act kind × domain × polarity) CH can mint has a `paragon_act` row (the registration walker); the pledge reading (the requester direct, every other hearer lens two only); the DM's `moralWeight` replaces the derived band for every reader that can see it (an `unspoken` mark reaches no hearer); a warlike court admires a brutal victory a peaceful one condemns, and a town's reading carries no force term; a town reads its axes' bands (a balanced town reads a lifted plague at 0, a benevolent one warms — executed); a broken pledge never warms the requester's rival; a verdict deed reads by its scandal-class row. Round 5 pins (RULINGS-ROUND4 §E, OD-RN-48 RULED): `pairNet` reads the pair label THROUGH BELIEF — byte-identical to round 4 in the omniscient and aspatial arms (EXECUTED: −0.51 = −0.51), the believed label in the belief presets (a stale `hostile` −0.8), a true `vassal` read true, κ only on a true label, a believed `unknown` 0; a hunt won over a beast pack is read by its PLACE (the relieved town), the pack no tie; a gang's `paid_quarter` warms its own town's commons by lens two whatever the town's alignment. EDITOR: seal — PT's `moralWeight` mark · direction row — none · counterparty — `real-only` · states at the chokepoint — derived · the cause — the deed's kind and target. Alignment: THE ENGAGEMENT — all three axes, both lenses (requirement 13's quadrant read). `causedBy`: the term names the deed and the lens that carried it.

**RN-4 — THE VOICES.** The dossier blocks, the person page's ties with reasons, the Why panel's renown rows over `causedBy`, the `voiced_regard` Herald kind (registered at every wall), voiced regard and the condemner's cost (§5.5; `renownLeansEnabled`; a voice naming the party is PT's `party_voiced` hearing, its deed minted at `spoken`), the priest's lean and the operative re-point (§5.4/§4.5; `renownLeansEnabled`; the operative's three-file signature car and the two shared-seam leaves). Round 3 pins (A.11): a court of any size whose derived regard of the party crosses into `enmity` opens PT's `party_voiced` with no fame and no person lit; at `spoken` its `condemnation` deed is a tale — no court fame, no RN regard of the court — and a hearer's term about the party scales by the court's credibility weight (1.0 while the court stock is dark); a court's condemner's cost falls on its ruler's `p:` key, or nowhere with persons dark; no court voices about a person or a corps (R59). Round 4 pins (RULINGS-ROUND4 A.15; the verifier's minor on the crossing): a court's condemnation moves an `allied` town's regard of the party by −0.4 (the cap), a `hostile` town's by +0.4, an unconnected town's by nothing, and its own commons' and members' by nothing (R62); a person who esteems the court cools, one who mistrusts it warms; the crossing compares a stored holder's band before and after the pass's writes, so a replaced term never voices twice. Round 5 pins (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.10; RULINGS-ROUND4 §E; the verifier's minor): a TOWN's court's condemnation in the default preset is heard by its trade neighbours within two hops (its walk at `notable`) and moves their terms by their relation factors — never only its own excluded commons (negative case: round 4's office scope, ring 0); a hamlet's court reaches its one neighbour; an ISOLATED court's crossing opens no `party_voiced` card; a city's walk is four hops; a town hearer's relation factor reads the pair label it BELIEVES (a stale `hostile` belief of an ally reads −1); a succession or a strike that moves a derived court's band across an edge opens no hearing (the band with and without this tick's first-heard terms). Host: `envoyChanceMeetingStage.js` (484) exports `postureBandOf` — the keyword alone. FIRST-VISIBLE: the dossier and the Why panel at once; a voice the week the crossing happens. TIER: aspatial-safe; the priest lean in deity-bearing worlds; the operative where ES is lit. DEGRADED ARM: dossier lines without voices. EDITOR: seal — none new · direction row — none · counterparty — `real-only` · states at the chokepoint — the voice is a deed (RN-0's writer) · the cause — the crossing. Alignment: READ (the condemner's people's malice). `causedBy`: a voice names the regard term that crossed.

**RN-5 — THE MEASURE.** Envelopes on a seed family per lit preset: regard entries per holder (≤ cap), deeds per campaign-year BY SOURCE (`body_act` included — the bodies' rate, round 4), the share of deeds that reach `regional`/`realm`, the fame half-life felt (how long a realm deed stays realm-known), voices per year (bounded), the court-vs-officer divergence rate once persons light, the heard pass's cost per tick and a COST PIN on it and on `courtRegardOf` over `deedIndex` (RN-r1-31); the deed ledger's bytes per campaign-year with `reach` rings, against the blob budget, including the owner's advance-by-N-years spans (chair notes item 1; RN-r1-26); the experience read (requirement 19) on C11's chain sentence. ## §14 THE LIFECYCLE OF EVERY PERSISTED SHAPE (requirement 22a)

**The deed — `spatialLedgers.deeds[deedId]`**

| Path | What happens |
|---|---|
| create | only `deedLedger.js :: mintDeed`, called by a registered `DEED_SOURCES` door; idempotent by `eventRef · tick · ordinal` (asked of the stored `actRef`, the id collision-checked by the landed probe — §2.1 [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3)]), and across sources by `sourceEventRef`; refused dark; a `dm_only` verdict never mints [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-8 · RN-r1-6]; a `body_act` deed keys its `recordKey` on BD's closing row (the engagement id) or, for an act with no hearing, on BD's act receipt id, so a re-read of either mints nothing; a hunt's success mints once, at CH's door, never again at PR's or ST's (B.5) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RU-BD-4 · B.5] |
| read | fame, the observer read, the lenses, the deed telling, the court's institutional record, the dossier, the Why panel |
| persist | rides the world blob inside `spatialLedgers` (zero first-paint bytes when absent); the normalizer names every field (the `normalizeEntry` lesson: an unnamed field is dropped) — `actRef` and the target's `siteId` included [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RULINGS-ROUND3 B (`siteId`); REFINED (`actRef`)]; the body actor refs and `ledBy` too [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes; REFINED (`ledBy`)] |
| regenerate | world-scoped, keyed by ids, untouched by a settlement reroll; a target that was a positional roster NPC is carried by its `wnpc_` or its name, never by `npc_N` (the `characterDrift` rebind lesson); a target's `siteId` is world-scoped (a site outlives its settlement, ST13) and resolves at read; a body ref is THE BODIES' world-scoped `bodyId`, untouched by a settlement reroll [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes] |
| undo | the DM's `STRIKE_DEED` (a `struck` mark — the party door has no undo, `undoState:'none'`); `UNSTRIKE_DEED` clears it and every reading returns exactly (a mask; nothing derived is deleted); an engine week is undone by the pulse's own snapshot restore, which drops the week's mints with it |
| import | the normalizer heals garbage to absent ("total on garbage") |
| migrate | an additive conditional key: no `WORLD_STATE_MIGRATIONS` entry (the additive-key rule) |
| dark-after-lit | kept, unread; nothing mints |
| death | the doer's or target's death leaves the deed standing (lived history); names resolve at read — a BODY's from BD's record or its `ended` ring (cap 24), and past the ring's horizon the deed reads by its kind and its place (`actAt`: "a gang of the Tolle hills, long gone"), never a stored copy of the name [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's `ended` ring is bounded and RN's facts are not] |
| veil | the deed is TRUTH (the DM's view shows it whole); every town sees only the version that reached it (§4.3, §5.1) |
| horizon (requirement 23) | FACTS NEVER PRUNED (lived history, the `concludedWars` idiom); `reach` COMPACTABLE. Bytes (PLAUSIBLE, measured at RN-5): ~200 bytes of facts plus `reach` as hop rings of ids at roughly 16–20 bytes per reached settlement, so a regional deed reaching a dozen towns carries ~0.4 KB and a realm deed in a sixty-town world ~1.2 KB (the old "~200 bytes plus a ≤ 64-hop map" understated it several-fold). At a DRAFT 200 deeds a year a century is ~20,000 records — several megabytes unchecked — and the owner's advance by N years (chair notes item 1) makes long spans the expected use. RN-5 measures the real rate against the blob budget; the COMPACTION (OD-RN-14, accepted by the chair) keeps every deed's facts and drops only `reach` for deeds whose fame has decayed to `unknown` — never a deletion [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-26] |

**The deed telling's additions — records of `spatialLedgers.rumorLedgers[settlementId]`** (the rumor module's own shape; RN adds two structured content fields) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-30]

| Path | What happens |
|---|---|
| create | the deed arm of `advanceRumorLedgers` seeds an arrival with `eventRef: deed:<deedId>` and a typed `doerRef` (an actor-ref string, never in `partyIds`); the same call seeds the registered `TELLING_SOURCES` rows' tellings (§3.4), whose declared content fields carry their OWNER's lifecycle row (CH's; BD's for its leak) and whose declared `relayedTick`/`arrivalTick` RN copies onto the seed (§3.4) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-CH-r2 §5.2(d)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.9 · BD's routes] |
| read | `settlementRumors.js`'s read model; RN's heard pass; `beliefMap.js :: reportsBySubject` (every telling is a report about its witness towns — the disclosed belief-map shift, §12) |
| persist | the rumor ledger; `doerRef` joins the content whitelist's structured fields by declaration (`captureContent`'s law: "ONLY structured fields are copied") — measured at RN-0's car |
| regenerate | world-scoped; untouched by a settlement reroll |
| undo | the pulse's snapshot restore |
| import | absent or garbage `doerRef` heals to absent |
| migrate | additive; no migration entry |
| dark-after-lit | existing tellings age out on their landed TTL; nothing new seeds |
| death | the doer's death leaves the telling (the tale outlives the man) |
| veil | a telling is BELIEF — what that town heard |
| horizon | the host's own: `RUMOR_TOP_K = 24` per settlement, TTL 26/13 weeks |

**The regard entry — `spatialLedgers.regard[holderKey][subjectKey]`**

| Path | What happens |
|---|---|
| create | the DM's verbs (`dmSet`, `declared`); the heard pass's `writeHeardTerm` (THE ONE PERSON STAGE), which MOVES a term to the new subject when the heard doer changes, the old entry dropped when empty [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — RN-V2]; PT's meeting deed read at the table; never an `s:`/`c:` holder about an `s:`/`c:` subject (refused) |
| read | `regardOf`, `courtRegardOf`, the officers' cards, the dossier |
| persist | blob; drop-when-empty at both levels |
| regenerate | person keys are `wnpc_` only (RN16); town and court keys are settlement ids, stable across rerolls; corps keys are WC's `unitKey` (world-scoped lived history, untouched by a settlement reroll) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]; body keys are THE BODIES' `bodyId` (world-scoped, untouched by a settlement reroll) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes] |
| undo | a struck deed's terms are MASKED, never dropped, so unstrike is exact; DM verbs have inverses; the engine week by snapshot [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-15] |
| import | normalizer; terms > 8 truncated weakest-first (a masked term is never the one truncated); unknown causes dropped; an absent or garbage `mag` heals to the deed's own band [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V2] |
| migrate | additive; none |
| dark-after-lit | kept, unread; no term written |
| death | a holder's death (the DM's `killNamedNpc` → `npcLedger.js :: removeNpcRecord`, which deletes `placed`/`roamers`/`exclusions` only) leaves the holder's entries in place, SKIPPED on read while the holder is absent and pruned only by decay — so undoing the KILL (`restoreNpcRecord`) restores the holder's regard exactly; entries ABOUT the dead stay until they decay (the dead are remembered) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-15]; a CORPS' disbandment (the DM's `disband`, CH §16b; WC's `dissolving` road) is its death — its entries are skipped while its unit is absent and pruned only by decay, and undoing the disbandment restores them exactly [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-6]; a NAMED BODY's end (BD's `BODY_ENDS`, its `ended` ring) or its lapse below the naming threshold is its death for RN — its entries skipped while it is ended or nameless, pruned only by decay, and a re-naming or an undone end restores them exactly; entries ABOUT it stay until they decay [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes] |
| veil | entries are BELIEF (what each holder heard); the DM's view may show the whole |
| horizon | bounded: ≤ 12 EMERGENT subjects (the DM's seeds and sets exempt) × ≤ 8 terms per holder; terms decay on `a_few_years` (`abandoned_to_captivity` on `a_generation`) and are pruned when both the commons' and the town court's decayed readings of a term fall below DRAFT 0.02 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-16 · RN-r1-19 · RN-r1-2] |

**The party's credibility — `spatialLedgers.party.credibility`** (the sub-ledger is PT's; this holder's lifecycle is RN's)

| Path | What happens |
|---|---|
| create | the party door's first `made_good` or `fell_short` pledge, or a contradicting correction (an exposed false reason) |
| read | `partyCredibilityOf` |
| persist | blob, inside PT's sub-ledger |
| regenerate | world-scoped; untouched |
| undo | a strike, an unstrike or PT's source-event undo of a charge REBUILDS the holder by refolding the unstruck charges in tick order (exact; never subtraction — §7); the engine week by the pulse snapshot [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RN-V5(a) (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.1)] |
| import | normalizer (finite score, integer tick) |
| migrate | additive; none |
| dark-after-lit | kept, unread |
| death | n/a (the party's fate is the table's) |
| veil | the holder is the DM's view; a town reads `partyCredibilityAt` — the law folded over only the pledge deeds and exposures it heard (§7), so a broken pledge it never heard of does not lower its trust [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-9] |
| horizon | one holder; spent past `MAX_LOOKBACK_TICKS: 260` |

**The `in_office` cause — a value ADDED to W-H1's closed `VERDICT_CAUSES`** (on the placement record RN-2's officer graduation writes) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r1) — RN-r1-17]: create — RN-2's bounded graduation only (the ruler and the `ACT_OFFICE` holders); a W-H2 verdict RE-STAMPS it to the verdict's cause, stamp and placement (`moveNpcRecord`, §5.2) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — D-RN-r2-N1]; read — the projection drops it from the Wanderers pool, and the gate and circulation read the person as any placed person; persist — the ledger's normalizer accepts it as a closed member (`closedValue(r.verdictCause, VERDICT_CAUSES)`); regenerate — the durable id survives rerolls (W-H1's law); undo — the pulse snapshot; import — an unknown cause normalizes to `none` as today, so an OLDER build reading a newer save would list the officer (a declared one-way compatibility note); migrate — an additive member, no migration entry; dark-after-lit — the persons stay placed and are never listed; death — as any placed person; veil — DM-only, like the pool; horizon — at most six per court.

| OD-RN-3 | Who carries renown ("appropriately applied to all of the other NPC's" · "also for the court NPC as well" — THE OWNER'S) | every person by office; deeds for every actor with a key; court members with their own regard once persons light; the party as subject only |
| OD-RN-4 | The store | the deed ledger `spatialLedgers.deeds` (a persisted shape), append-only, minted ids, `struck` and `correctsRef`; RN owns it, PT and CH write through it (R-RN-9) |

| OD-RN-7 | One fame, one fade | the stamp stays and every observer read goes through the projection under `renownEnabled`; the verdict adds a deed where it is applied; one fade per person; the operative read re-points through a signature car (RN4; RULINGS-ROUND1 RN-r1-6) |

## §17 WHAT A DM READS (under the Evidence Law: who, which power, what effect; deeds, never forecasts)

- "Known across the river towns as the ones who broke the siege at Kell." *(surface; fame and its deed)*
- "In Varn the lords curse the party's name for saving Kell, their old rival; the commons say it was well done." *(surface; the two lenses, two holders)*
- "In the hill towns they say you burned an orphanage; they have not heard why." *(surface; the version heard — scrutiny shows where the reason was lost)*
- "Kell's Watch Captain, who has not forgotten the riot in the square, has posted men at the gate." *(surface on an officer's card; the officer's own reason)*
- "Merewick's crown counts the party among its enemies." *(the DM's declared stance, in the world's words)*
- "The High Priest of Kell, held in esteem across the river towns, has condemned the party; Kell's faithful have cooled toward them." *(a voice and its echo)*
- "Ashford's people grumble that their lord spoke against the ones who lifted the plague." *(the condemner's cost)*
- "In Tolle they say the party's word is worth little since they never came to Ashford." *(credibility, from a broken pledge heard)*
- "Captain Vey and Merewick's crown have been at odds since the burning of Tolle." *(a tie with its reason, on a person's page)*
- "Few outside Kell remember the siege now." *(fame faded — said only when looked at, never pushed)*

- "Little spoken of in Varn now; Varn has not forgotten the siege." *(fame faded, the grudge held — two facts, RN3)*
- "Varn's court has denounced the party; its friends along the river think the worse of them, and Merewick, which has no love for Varn, the better." *(a court's voice — heard by its standing, weighed by its word and by each hearer's side of Varn; RULINGS-ROUND3 A.11, RULINGS-ROUND4 A.15; said only after PT's `party_voiced` resolves `spoken`; Varn's own people judge the deed by their own lights)*
- "In Ashford they curse the Hollow Road gang since it burned the mill; word of it has gone up the river road." *(a named body's deed and its fame — THE BODIES' `body_act`, RN's tale; round 4)*
- "Kell's warden broke the Hollow Road gang at the ford; the river towns speak of her now." *(a led hunt's ONE deed — CH's `hunt_won`, its leader the doer and the fame hers; round 5, B.5)*
- "Ham's lord has spoken against the party; in Kell, which trades with Ham, they think the worse of them." *(a hamlet's court voice heard by its neighbours — the tale's reach, RULINGS-ROUND5 B.10; said only after PT's `party_voiced` resolves `spoken`)*

Every line names who and what was done; bands, weights and the half-life wait for an intentional look. The two voice lines (the High Priest's condemnation and Ashford's grumble) name the party: each is said only after PT's `party_voiced` hearing resolves `spoken` (§5.5); spoken of a paragon, they stand as written. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r1-6 RULED · RN-r1-11.]
